### [1] [Dynamic HBM Repartitioning for Multi-Turn MoE Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13537)
*Jinpyo Kim,Mingi Kwon,Younghoon Min,Jongryool Kim,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: VAMP 是一个 MoE 推理服务框架，在运行时动态改变 HBM 中专家权重与 KV 缓存之间的边界：当 KV 分配无法满足时，比较三种方案的预估未来工作量，并在惩罚最低时将有限的专家权重区域转换为 KV 缓存容量，通过 CUDA 虚拟内存页重映射实现无需拷贝的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时多轮请求会累积可复用的 KV 状态，一旦超出固定的 GPU KV 缓存分配，系统就会驱逐可复用前缀、重复预填充甚至抢占请求。该问题在 MoE 模型中尤为突出：专家权重占据了大部分 HBM，而每个 token 只激活稀疏的专家子集，权重与 KV 缓存之间的静态边界使系统无法利用专家内存来保存随对话增长的可复用状态。

Method: 在 vLLM 服务引擎中实现 VAMP，基于 Qwen3-Next-80B，在录制的与受控的多轮负载上进行评估；核心机制是在 KV 分配无法满足时对比三种备选方案的预估未来工作量（从主机内存暂存专家权重、驱逐可能需重新预填充的缓存前缀、抢占并重调度请求），当该动作预估惩罚最低时，把有界的专家权重区域转换为 KV 缓存容量，并用 CUDA 虚拟内存管理页重映射完成转换，不复制常驻 KV 数据。

Result: 在五次重放的记录型 2,103 轮 SWE-bench agent 负载上，采用 15% 最大专家卸载比例的 VAMP 相比未修改的 vLLM，将首 token 时延（TTFT）p90 从 26.1 s 降至 1.10 s（提升 23.6 倍），请求吞吐量提升 20.7%，同时每输出 token 耗时（TPOT）增加 31.1%。

Conclusion: 通过运行时动态调整 MoE 模型 HBM 中权重与 KV 缓存之间的边界，VAMP 能在长时多轮场景下大幅降低 TTFT 并提升吞吐，代价是 TPOT 有所上升，说明将稀疏激活的专家内存灵活复用于可复用 KV 状态是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Long-running multi-turn requests accumulate reusable key-value (KV) state. Once this state exceeds a fixed GPU KV-cache allocation, serving systems evict reusable prefixes, repeat prefill work, and may preempt requests. This pressure is particularly acute for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models: their expert weights occupy most GPU high-bandwidth memory (HBM), even though each token activates only a sparse subset of experts. A static boundary between weights and the KV cache prevents serving systems from using expert memory to preserve reusable state as conversations grow.
  We present VAMP, an MoE serving framework that changes this HBM boundary at runtime. When a KV allocation cannot be satisfied, VAMP compares the estimated future work of three alternatives: staging expert weights from host memory, evicting cached prefixes that may require re-prefill, or preempting and rescheduling requests. It then converts a bounded expert-weight region into KV-cache capacity when that action has the lowest estimated penalty. CUDA Virtual Memory Management page remapping performs this conversion without copying resident KV data.
  We implement VAMP in the vLLM serving engine and evaluate Qwen3-Next-80B on recorded and controlled multi-turn workloads. Across five replays of a recorded 2,103-turn SWE-bench agent workload, VAMP with a 15% maximum expert-offloading ratio reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) p90 from 26.1 s to 1.10 s (23.6 times) and increases request throughput by 20.7% relative to unmodified vLLM, while increasing time per output token (TPOT) by 31.1%.

</details>


### [2] [PEAT: Pseudo-Error Assessment for GPU Kernel Validation in DNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13544)
*Xuan Truong Nguyen,Hong Quan Tran,Tuan Duc Chu,Thanh Tuan Dao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PEAT，一个用于DNN训练中GPU内核验证的轻量级伪误差评估框架，通过Profiler、Analyzer和Detector三个组件高效刻画新内核实现的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN训练系统软件栈发展迅速，大量代码被移植到不同框架或为GPU/专用加速器重写。然而验证DNN训练中的内核实现非常耗时且需要大量存储，核心问题在于如何刻画新内核实现集成到训练流程后的行为，这一问题在文献中尚未被充分研究。

Method: PEAT框架包含三部分：（1）Profiler受传统故障注入启发，在训练流程中调用逐操作内核以收集模型状态（如检查点和激活值），并引入播放式故障注入和基于频率的运行时故障注入两种技术，利用训练过程中的持久内核调用；（2）Analyzer刻画剖析得到的误差，从内核误差分布中相对黄金内核提取签名特征；（3）Detector将若干已知误差模型与签名模式关联，提供充分条件的指导准则。

Result: 在NVIDIA V100和AMD MI250两种主流厂商的GPU上，针对从视觉任务到语言模型的多种AI模型，覆盖预训练和微调场景，展示了该方法的适用性并给出结果与分析。

Conclusion: PEAT作为一种轻量级伪误差评估框架，能够有效刻画和验证DNN训练中GPU内核实现的行为，具有良好的跨硬件平台和跨模型任务的适用性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are widely adopted in various fields, driving an emerging trend in developing software stacks associated with DNN training systems. For example, many codes have been ported across different frameworks or developed to leverage the computing power of GPUs or domain-specific accelerators. However, validating a kernel implementation in DNN training is time-consuming and generally requires massive storage. Specifically, this poses a fundamental question: how to characterize the behavior of a new implementation when it is integrated into a DNN training flow. Unfortunately, this problem is not well investigated in the literature, to the best of our knowledge. To address this shortcoming, we present PEAT - a lightweight inspection framework for \underline{P}seudo-\underline{E}rror \underline{A}ssessment associated with GPU kernel validation in DNN \underline{T}raining. Firstly, inspired by conventional fault injection (FI), PEAT's Profiler invokes an operation-wise kernel in a training flow to collect a DNN model's states (e.g., checkpoints and activations). More importantly, the Profiler introduces two simple yet effective techniques, playback FI and frequency-based runtime FI, leveraging persistent kernel calling during the training process. Secondly, PEAT's Analyzer characterizes profiled errors, revealing some signatures from the error distribution of a kernel compared to the golden one. Lastly, PEAT's Detector provides some guidelines as a sufficient condition, which enables associating several well-known error models with signature patterns. We demonstrate the applicability of our approach by presenting the results and analysis using GPUs from the two most popular vendors, NVIDIA V100 and AMD MI250, on various AI models, from vision tasks to language models, for both pretraining and finetuning scenarios.

</details>


### [3] [The Essence of Git: Concepts for P2P Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13555)
*Erick Lavoie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于Git对象模型与增量复制协议构建的各类非版本控制应用，并探讨其与分布式算法的联系及潜在通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Git通常被视为去中心化版本控制系统，但其底层优雅的对象模型和增量复制协议已被用于构建远超版本控制的应用。作者希望系统梳理这些应用，揭示Git概念的通用性。

Method: 采用Daniel Jackson提出的"概念设计"方法进行讨论：综述相关应用，展示应用概念如何映射到Git概念；分析Git提交历史与分布式算法、收敛复制数据结构理论之间的联系；并与当前点对点框架进行比较。

Result: 展示了Git对象模型对多种应用概念的映射能力，揭示了Git提交历史与分布式算法及收敛复制数据结构的关联，并通过与现有P2P框架对比，评估了Git对象模型的抽象层次。

Conclusion: Git对象模型具有较强的通用性和抽象层次，其设计接近分布式算法的本质，具备潜在的普适性。

Abstract: Git is most commonly known as a decentralized version control system. Underneath the surface, the design of Git exhibits an elegant object model and incremental replication protocols that have been used to build a variety of applications that go much beyond version control. In this paper, we survey those applications and show how application concepts have been mapped to Git concepts to show the latter's generality. We also show the connection between the Git commit history and the theory of distributed algorithms and convergent replicated data structures, suggesting that it approaches their essence. We finally compare to current peer-to-peer frameworks to establish the level of abstraction of the Git object model and peek at its potential universality. Throughout, we frame our discussion using the \textit{conceptual design} approach proposed by Daniel Jackson.

</details>


### [4] [mKernel: Fast Multi-GPU, Multi-Node Fused Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13585)
*Ziming Mao,Yihan Zhang,Shawn Wei Chew,Shuang Ma,Costin Raiciu,Yang Zhou,Scott Shenker,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: mKernel 是一个多 GPU、多节点的融合算子库，将计算、节点内 NVLink 通信与节点间 RDMA 通信在 tile 粒度上重叠，通过 SM 角色划分与运行时自适应调度，在两个 16-GPU H200 集群上取得最高 1.72x（GEMM+AllReduce）和 1.88x（Ring Attention）加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型分布式训练与推理中通信已成为瓶颈。现有做法（在独立流上以 kernel 粒度重叠通信与计算）只能缓解部分开销；而融合 kernel 虽可通过"tile 一产出即传输"获得更好性能，却大多局限于单个 NVLink 域内。因此需要一种能跨节点、在 tile 粒度上统一重叠计算、节点内 NVLink 与节点间 RDMA 的融合算子方案。

Method: mKernel 将持久化 kernel 的 SM 划分为计算角色与通信角色，并在 GPU 上设置控制器，根据 kernel 与输入形状在运行时自适应调整 SM 划分；采用层次化数据搬移结构以最小化跨节点网络流量；通过直接构建在 RDMA verbs 上的轻量命令队列与主机代理，由 GPU 驱动网络，使同一 kernel 可运行于任意网络后端（如 InfiniBand 与 AWS EFA）。

Result: 实现了覆盖张量并行、序列并行与专家并行共五个 kernel。在两个 16-GPU H200 集群上，GEMM+AllReduce 最高加速 1.72x，Ring Attention 最高加速 1.88x。此外观察到 GPUDirect Async（IBGDA）相比主机辅助的 GPU 发起通信几乎没有额外收益。

Conclusion: 以 tile 粒度统一重叠计算、NVLink 与 RDMA 的多节点融合算子可有效突破分布式通信瓶颈，且网络后端可移植性良好；同时表明 GPU 直接驱动网络的复杂机制（如 IBGDA）在当前场景下收益有限。

Abstract: Communication has become a bottleneck in distributed training and inference of large models. Overlapping communication with computation at the granularity of kernels, on separate streams, reduces only part of this communication cost. Fused kernels often have better performance by transmitting each output tile as soon as it is produced, but existing fused kernels are largely confined to a single NVLink domain. We present mKernel, a library of multi-GPU, multi-node fused kernels that overlap computation, intra-node NVLink communication, and inter-node RDMA at tile granularity. mKernel partitions the streaming multiprocessors (SMs) of a persistent kernel into compute and communication roles, and an on-GPU controller tunes the SM partition adaptively at run time, since the best SM partition varies with the kernel and the input shape. It structures data movement hierarchically so that data traversing the inter-node network is minimized. Finally, it drives the network from the GPU through a lightweight command queue and host proxy implemented directly on RDMA verbs, which allows the same kernels to run on any network backend (e.g. InfiniBand and on AWS EFA); we observe, surprisingly, that GPUDirect Async (IBGDA) yields little additional benefit over host-assisted GPU-initiated communication. We implement five kernels spanning tensor, sequence, and expert parallelism. On two 16-GPU H200 clusters, mKernel achieves speedups of up to 1.72x on GEMM+AllReduce and $1.88\times$ on Ring Attention.

</details>


### [5] [Real-World Deployment and Performance Characterisation of Fog-Based Deep Learning for Cold-Chain Temperature Prediction over LoRaWAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14036)
*Jeremiah Taguta,Jean Frederic Isingizwe Nturambirwe,Clement Nthambazale Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 首个雾计算FFV冷链温度预测的真实部署：树莓派上LSTM-GRU+条件SHAP，MAE 0.2°C，555ms延迟，日耗约0.2 kWh。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生鲜果蔬易腐，冷链断链导致大量食物浪费；云推理存在延迟与数据丢失，雾计算此前仅在仿真中验证，缺乏真实部署证据。

Method: 在南非苹果冷库诱导冷链断裂，用LoRaWAN传感器采集数据；在Raspberry Pi 4上完全本地部署LSTM-GRU预测冷库温度，无云依赖，仅在预测到断链时生成条件SHAP解释。

Result: MAE 0.2°C，约0.2 kWh/天（0.7 Wh/次预测），预测延迟<1秒（555 ms，主要来自网络与消息传递），SHAP增加28% CPU但仍在硬件能力内；模型主要依据温度、湿度及其交互。部署还暴露了仿真无法发现的传感器触发的单点故障，以及真实韧性：基础设施故障自主恢复与断网持续运行。

Conclusion: 给出首批雾基FFV冷链温度预测部署基准，证明可解释温度预测在资源受限边缘硬件上可行；未来工作含异步传感器融合、商业冷链部署、替代模型架构与因果分析。

Abstract: Fresh fruits and vegetables (FFVs) are highly perishable, and cold-chain breaks contribute significantly to global food waste. While Machine Learning (ML) can enable proactive intervention, cloud-based inference faces challenges such as latency and data loss. Fog computing addresses these issues but has been tested only in simulation for FFV cold-chain temperature prediction. To the best of the authors' knowledge, this paper presents its first real-world deployment. A fog-deployed LSTM-GRU model predicted cold-room temperature using LoRaWAN sensor data collected from a South African apple cold-storage facility with induced cold-chain breaks. Running entirely on a Raspberry Pi 4 with no cloud dependency, the system generated conditional SHAP explanations only when a break is predicted. The deployed system predicts cold-room temperature with an MAE of 0.2°C at roughly 0.2 kWh per day (0.7 Wh per prediction). Predictions were delivered in under one second (555 ms), dominated by network and messaging rather than computation, with conditional explanations adding modest cost. SHAP consumes 28% more CPU but is well within the hardware's capacity. The model attributes its predictions primarily to temperature, humidity, and their interaction. Critically, the deployment surfaced what simulation cannot: a sensor-triggered single point of failure, alongside genuine resilience, autonomous recovery from infrastructure faults and continued operation through internet loss. These are the first published deployment benchmarks for fog-based temperature prediction in FFV cold chains, establishing that explainable temperature forecasting is feasible on resource-constrained edge hardware. Future work includes asynchronous sensor fusion, commercial cold chain deployment, alternative model architectures, and causal analysis.

</details>


### [6] [Reducing Cold-Start Latency in Serverless Applications via Dynamic Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14040)
*Georgios Alexopoulos,Konstantinos Karakatsanis,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Dimitris Mitropoulos,Thodoris Sotiropoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PyXtrim 通过动态切片实现 Python 无服务器应用去膨胀，中位数冷启动延迟降低 21.7%，峰值内存降低 17.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器应用存在冷启动延迟问题，而 Python 因其动态特性和大量原生扩展，使传统静态去膨胀方法难以奏效。

Method: 将去膨胀建模为动态切片问题，以应用外部可见行为为切片准则；通过跨语言动态依赖引擎追踪 Python 与原生代码间的数据和控制依赖，并识别与操作系统交互的操作。切片之外的内容（包括依赖）均被移除。

Result: 在 AWS Lambda 的 31 个应用上，冷启动延迟中位数降低 21.7%，峰值内存降低 17.1%，效果是当前最优方法的两倍以上，且每个应用去膨胀仅需数分钟。

Conclusion: PyXtrim 能有效处理依赖反射、原生代码互操作和系统资源交互的真实应用，显著优于现有技术。

Abstract: We present PyXtrim, a system that reduces the cold-start latency of serverless applications through debloating. We focus on Python, a dominant language for serverless applications whose dynamic features and extensive use of native extensions make traditional static debloating particularly challenging. PyXtrim frames debloating as a dynamic slicing problem, using the application's externally visible behavior as the slicing criterion. Everything outside the resulting slice is removed, both from the application and its dependencies. Our key technical contribution is that the slice is computed by a cross-language dynamic dependence engine that tracks data and control dependences across Python and native code and identifies operations that interact with the operating system, which form the slicing criterion.
  As a result, PyXtrim can effectively handle real-world applications that rely on dynamic features such as reflection, interoperate with native code and interact with system resources. Across 31 applications on AWS Lambda, PyXtrim reduces cold-start latency by 21.7% and peak memory usage by 17.1% at the median. This is more than double the reduction achieved by the state of the art, while debloating each application in minutes.

</details>


### [7] [Partition-Aware Scheduling for Mobile Heterogeneous Inference Co-Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14213)
*Zhuojin Li,Marco Paolieri,Leana Golubchik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向移动异构平台的分区感知DAG调度方法，联合考虑算子间与算子内并行，通过在线迭代搜索实现接近离线最优的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代移动推理运行在GPU与多CPU核心簇组成的异构平台上。现有优化要么只利用算子间并行（将整个算子分配给CPU或GPU），要么只利用算子内并行（将每个算子分区以实现CPU-GPU协同执行），二者未联合考虑。此外，最优策略取决于推理DAG的结构，因此需要一种联合建模的方法来提升推理延迟。

Method: 论文定义了面向移动异构推理的分区感知DAG调度问题，将算子分区选择、设备分配与执行顺序纳入统一公式。提出在线迭代搜索框架：将大DAG分解为多个阶段，把搜索聚焦于关键算子，并利用延迟预测器估计分区执行时间，避免穷举式性能剖析。

Result: 在多个代表性移动推理工作负载上，该方法实现了接近离线最优解的延迟，同时将调度开销控制在模型初始化成本的一小部分，从而支持在部署时进行平台特定调度。

Conclusion: 联合考虑算子间与算子内并行可有效提升移动异构平台上的推理延迟表现；所提出的在线迭代搜索框架在保证低调度开销的同时达到近离线最优性能，使部署时平台自适应调度成为可能。

Abstract: Modern mobile inference runs on heterogeneous platforms combining mobile GPUs with multiple CPU core clusters. Existing optimizations typically exploit either inter-operator parallelism, by assigning entire operators to CPU cores or to the GPU, or intra-operator parallelism, by partitioning each operator for CPU-GPU co-execution. We consider these two forms of parallelism together, to improve inference latency of tasks that can be represented by a static DAG of operators with predefined input/output tensor shapes (e.g., CNNs or vision transformers). We define the problem of partition-aware DAG scheduling for mobile heterogeneous inference, illustrating that the best strategy depends on the structure of the inference DAG, thus motivating a joint formulation capturing operator partition choices, device assignment, and execution order. We propose an online iterative search framework, which decomposes large DAGs into stages, focuses search on critical operators, and uses latency predictors to estimate partitioned execution without exhaustive profiling. Across representative mobile inference workloads, our approach achieves latency close to an offline solution while keeping scheduling overhead to a fraction of the model initialization cost, allowing platform-specific scheduling at deployment time.

</details>


### [8] [OpWeave: Flexible Operator Disaggregation for Heterogeneous LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14237)
*Zikun Li,Yixuan Mei,Shiqi Pan,Zixuan Chen,Xiaowen Zhang,Mengdi Wu,Shuhuai Lin,Yutong Yang,Zhihao Zhang,Xupeng Miao,Rashmi Vinayak,Zhihao Jia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OpWeave 是一个面向异构算子级解耦服务（ODS）的端到端框架，通过分析成本模型与规则感知规划器联合优化算子划分与部署配置，并在 vLLM 运行时执行，可将服务成本最高降低 1.78×（同构）与 1.89×（异构）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务系统正朝向更细粒度的阶段解耦发展，例如在解码阶段将注意力与 FFN 或 MoE 执行分离。这种算子级解耦服务（ODS）可提升硬件匹配度并支持独立扩展，尤其在异构设备上。但现有系统固定了算子边界，且缺乏对"何时解耦能降低服务成本"的统一刻画。

Method: 提出 OpWeave 框架，包含三个部分：（1）一个分析成本模型，用于界定同构与异构 ODS 相较共置服务的收益上界；（2）一个规则感知规划器，联合优化算子划分与部署配置，使搜索在混合注意力模型下仍可处理；（3）基于 vLLM 的运行时，在异构设备组上以灵活的算子阶段执行生成的方案。

Result: 在评估中，相比最佳可行基线，OpWeave 在同构 GPU 集群上最多降低服务成本 1.78×，在异构 GPU 集群上最多降低 1.89×，同时满足延迟 SLO。

Conclusion: OpWeave 为异构算子级解耦服务提供了统一的分析刻画与联合优化方法，在满足延迟 SLO 的前提下显著降低服务成本，验证了算子级解耦在异构集群上的实际收益。

Abstract: LLM serving systems increasingly disaggregate inference into finer-grained stages, with recent approaches separating attention from FFN or MoE execution during decode. This operator-level disaggregated serving (ODS) can improve hardware matching and enable independent scaling, particularly across heterogeneous devices. However, existing systems fix operator boundaries and lack a unified characterization of when disaggregation reduces serving cost. We present OpWeave, an end-to-end framework for heterogeneous ODS. OpWeave provides an analytical cost model that bounds the gains of homogeneous and heterogeneous ODS over colocated serving. It jointly optimizes operator partitioning and deployment configuration through a regularity-aware planner that keeps the search tractable even for hybrid-attention models. A vLLM-based runtime executes the synthesized plans with flexible operator stages across heterogeneous device groups. In our evaluation, OpWeave reduces serving cost by up to $1.78\times$ on homogeneous and $1.89\times$ on heterogeneous GPU clusters relative to the best feasible baseline, while meeting latency SLOs.

</details>


### [9] [MANE: A Multi-Path Adaptive Network for Edge Onloading of Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14660)
*Sokratis Nikolaidis,Stylianos I. Venieris,Leonidas Malachias,Iakovos S. Venieris*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MANE是一个面向多设备共享边缘服务器的分布式推理框架，通过多路径尾部模型架构实现在线精度-吞吐权衡，在40设备并发下维持80%以上SLO满足率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能办公等室内环境中，大量IoT设备共享同一边缘服务器进行拆分计算（split computing）。缺乏合理的共享负载管理机制时，服务器会迅速过载，导致时延SLO违约，使服务器辅助推理失效。

Method: MANE采用服务器端多路径尾部（multi-path tail）架构，实现运行时动态的精度-吞吐权衡。具体包括：新颖的多路径模型架构；三阶段训练方案（含Joint Head Network Distillation损失）；基于滞后的调度器与公平的设备回退（device-fallback）策略。

Result: 在多达40个并发设备场景下，MANE保持超过80%的SLO满足率，而现有最优onloading方法完全失效；同时精度比纯设备端方案高6个百分点。

Conclusion: MANE通过多路径尾部架构与配套训练、调度机制，有效解决多设备共享边缘推理资源下的过载与SLO违约问题，兼顾时延与精度。

Abstract: Split computing constitutes a widely used distributed inference approach, where a lightweight head model is onloaded onto the device and a heavier tail model resides on an edge server, leveraging the growing computational capabilities of modern System-on-Chips while alleviating server load. As intelligent indoor environments such as smart offices grow increasingly populated with diverse IoT devices, a single edge server must simultaneously assist multiple devices, each competing for the same shared inference resources. Without a principled mechanism to manage this shared load, the server is quickly overwhelmed, causing latency SLO violations and rendering server-assisted inference ineffective. In this work, we present MANE, a distributed inference framework that equips the server with a multi-path tail architecture, enabling a dynamic accuracy--throughput trade-off at runtime. By introducing a novel multi-path model architecture, a three-stage training scheme featuring a Joint Head Network Distillation loss and a hysteresis-based scheduler with an equitable device-fallback policy, MANE maintains over 80% SLO satisfaction rate where state-of-the-art onloading methods fail completely, while preserving accuracy 6pp higher than on-device alternatives, across up to 40 concurrent devices.

</details>


### [10] [CATS: A Carbon-Aware Task Simulator for Reducing AI Data Center Emissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14775)
*Dayuan Chen,Ziliang Zong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文构建全球云区域与电网碳强度对齐数据集并开发碳感知任务模拟器CATS，量化了时空调度在AI推理负载下可实现的碳减排潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI推动云数据中心快速扩张，预计2026年电力需求翻倍；由于电网碳强度在区域和时段间差异超过5.5倍，推理任务在何地何时执行对运营碳排放影响显著，但当前站点布局与碳强度存在错配，减排潜力未被释放。

Method: 1) 编制全球对齐数据集，统一8家主要云厂商的140个运营及规划区域与145个电网区域2022–2024年五分钟粒度碳强度轨迹；2) 开发CATS（碳感知任务模拟器），基于真实trace刻画6类AI推理任务与多种GPU，合成日变化曲线及地理、任务混合与SLA约束，并评估时空调度器（含两个基线），报告碳排放、能耗、运行时、排队延迟与硬件利用率等指标。

Result: 当前50%的站点位于中高碳强度电网，存在选址—碳错配。在包含60万任务、集群利用率0.37的24小时trace中，空间迁移相对速度优先基线降低38.4%的CO2，时间迁移降低16%，SLA违规率控制在3.27%。

Conclusion: 建议未来数据中心选址于低碳强度电网，并证明在现有集群上实施碳感知调度即可实现显著的运营碳排放削减。

Abstract: The rapid rise of generative AI is accelerating cloud data center expansion, with electricity demand projected to double by 2026. Because carbon-intensity varies by more than 5.5x across grids and times of day, where and when inference tasks execute significantly affects operational emissions. We address this issue with three aspects in this paper. First, we compile a global alignment dataset unifying 140 operational and planned cloud regions across 8 major providers with five-minute carbon-intensity traces for 145 grid regions from 2022 to 2024, revealing that 50% of current sites lie in medium-to-high carbon-intensity grids, indicating a siting-carbon mismatch and unrealized carbon reduction potential. Second, we develop CATS (Carbon-Aware Task Simulator), a flexible trace-driven framework that profiles six AI inference tasks across multiple GPU types, synthesizes realistic diurnal curve, geographical and task mixes, and SLA constraints, and evaluates spatial and temporal schedulers against two baselines while reporting comprehensive metrics including carbon emissions, energy consumption, runtime, queue delay, and hardware utilization. Third, we quantify achievable CO2 savings under realistic constraints: in a 24-hour trace with 600,000 tasks at fleet utilization of 0.37, spatial shifting reduces CO2 by 38.4% versus speed-first baseline, while temporal shifting yields 16% savings with bounded SLA violations at 3.27%. These results advocate locating future data centers in low carbon-intensity grids and demonstrate that carbon-aware scheduling on today's fleets can achieve substantial operational emissions reduction.

</details>


### [11] [Stability-Aware Proactive Autoscaling Using a Double Deep Q-Network in Cloud Computing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14894)
*Bablu Kumar,Anshul Verma,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出基于双深度Q网络（DDQN）的主动式自动扩缩容方法DDQN-Proactive，并结合资源移除策略（RRS），以提升云环境下动态负载的扩缩容效率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态工作负载与延迟敏感型应用要求云环境具备高效自动扩缩容能力，但现有方法多依赖静态阈值的被动响应机制，在工作负载不确定时导致响应延迟与扩缩容振荡。因此需要一种更稳定、自适应的主动式扩缩容方案。

Method: 提出DDQN-Proactive主动式自动扩缩容方法与资源移除策略（RRS）。DDQN+RRS通过将动作选择与价值评估解耦，增强决策能力，实现更稳定、自适应的扩缩容。

Result: DDQN+RRS优于被动式与现有主动式方法：SLA违约率更低（11.81%），CPU利用率更高（52.23%），扩缩容更稳定，扩缩容事件更少（2,488次），Pod重启更少（1,246次）；并在0-60秒内显著减少振荡，在15-30秒、40-45秒、55-60秒区间表现最稳定平滑，而被动方法波动明显。

Conclusion: DDQN+RRS能有效降低SLA违约、提升资源利用率并减少扩缩容振荡，实现更平滑稳定的自动扩缩容行为。

Abstract: Dynamic workloads and latency-sensitive applications require efficient autoscaling in cloud computing environments. However, most existing approaches rely on reactive mechanisms based on static thresholds, resulting in delayed responses and scaling oscillations under workload uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose a double deep Q-Network-based proactive autoscaling approach (DDQN-Proactive) along with Resource Removal Strategy (RRS). The proposed (DDQN+RRS) enhances decision-making by decoupling action selection from value evaluation, enabling more stable and adaptive scaling. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms both reactive and existing proactive approaches. Specifically, DDQN+RRS achieves a lower Service Level Agreement (SLA) violation rate (11.81%), higher CPU utilization (52.23%), improved scaling stability, fewer scaling events (2,488), and reduced pod restarts (1,246). Furthermore, the approach ensures smoother autoscaling behavior by significantly reducing oscillations over time (0-60 s). While reactive methods exhibit substantial fluctuations in pod allocation, Reactive reduces these variations, and DDQN+RRS achieves the most stable and smooth scaling, particularly during the 15-30 s, 40-45 s, and 55-60 s intervals.

</details>


### [12] [Agentic Autoscaling through Worker-Pool Orchestration for LLM-driven Text Classification in Cloud Computing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14898)
*Bablu Kumar,Anshul Verma,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向LLM文本分类的智能体自动扩缩容框架，通过工作池编排实现弹性资源调度，在保持高分类精度的同时提升资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文本处理中，基于LLM的系统面临高延迟、突发性和计算密集的负载，亟需动态自动扩缩容来管理资源。

Method: 框架包含优先任务队列、动态智能体工作池、实时指标采集器和应用层自动扩缩器，且与分类器无关，支持零样本与微调模型而不改动扩缩逻辑。以Autoscaling+BART和Autoscaling+DeBERTa进行评估，对比静态分配及独立RoBERTa、DistilBERT基线。

Result: AG News数据集上，Autoscaling+BART达84.5%准确率，Autoscaling+DeBERTa提升至90.5%；SMS Spam数据集上，Autoscaling+DeBERTa达99.5%，Autoscaling+BART为84.5%且执行时间更短。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持高分类性能的同时，资源效率持续优于基线，表明弹性工作池编排是云环境下可扩展LLM文本分类的有效且低成本的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing adoption of large language model (LLM)-based systems for large-scale text processing has created a critical need for dynamic autoscaling to manage high-latency, bursty, and computationally intensive workloads. This paper proposes an agentic autoscaling framework through worker-pool orchestration for LLM-driven text classification. The framework integrates a priority task queue, a dynamic pool of agent workers, a real-time metrics collector, and an application-layer autoscaler. Its classifier-agnostic design supports both zero-shot and fine-tuned language models without modifying the autoscaling logic. The framework is evaluated using Autoscaling+BART and Autoscaling+DeBERTa against static allocation and standalone RoBERTa and DistilBERT baselines. On the AG News dataset, Autoscaling+BART achieves 84.5% accuracy, while Autoscaling+DeBERTa improves it to 90.5%. On the SMS Spam Collection dataset, Autoscaling+DeBERTa achieves 99.5% accuracy, whereas Autoscaling+BART attains 84.5% accuracy with lower execution time. Overall, the proposed framework consistently outperforms the baseline approaches in resource efficiency while maintaining high classification performance, demonstrating that elastic worker-pool orchestration provides an effective and cost-efficient solution for scalable LLM-driven text classification in cloud environments.

</details>


### [13] [Shared KV Caching for Replicated 27B Inference: Correctness Failures and Performance Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15021)
*Frank Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 共享主机内存缓存可避免请求跨推理副本迁移时重复prefill，但有效性取决于正确的状态传输与丢失的前缀局部性。研究两个共享256 GiB LMCache池的单GPU 27B vLLM副本，发现打包页补丁中裸指针回退缺少当前CUDA流依赖，修复后经字节测试与全池分配校验验证；跨副本首token时间大幅下降，合成会话提升约35%–45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨推理副本迁移请求时反复执行prefill造成浪费，共享主机内存缓存理论上可复用KV/前缀状态，但实际收益同时受两个条件制约：状态能否正确传输，以及前缀局部性是否会在迁移中丢失。现有打包页补丁可能隐藏正确性隐患，需要定位并验证。

Method: 在两个单GPU 27B vLLM副本上共享256 GiB LMCache池。采用已有打包页补丁后，定位到一个裸指针回退路径，该路径遗漏了对当前CUDA流的依赖。通过受控字节测试在施加延迟下复现失败、恢复依赖后通过；使用现有混合分配器作为可用部署路径，并辅以全池分配检查与服务回归。设计四段OFF-ON-ON-OFF对比，包含两个块对内的768个实测请求；另用六轮合成会话交替副本评估不同初始上下文下的收益。

Result: 跨副本首内容token中位时间在128k输入时从31.715秒降至0.605秒，在256k时从92.047秒降至0.790秒。交替副本的六轮合成会话在32k与128k初始上下文下分别提升约35%和45%；固定放置则收益甚微。

Conclusion: 该工程案例研究给出共享主机内存缓存的实用验证步骤（流依赖修正确认、字节级测试、全池分配检查、服务回归），并明确了其收益依赖的局部性条件：只有当请求在副本间迁移且前缀可复用时，共享缓存才显著生效；固定放置下收益有限。

Abstract: Shared host-memory caching can avoid repeated prefill when a request moves between inference replicas. Its usefulness depends on both correct state transfer and lost prefix locality. We study two single-GPU 27B vLLM replicas sharing a 256 GiB LMCache pool. After adopting an existing packed-page patch, we isolate a raw-pointer fallback that omits the dependency on the current CUDA stream. Controlled byte tests fail under an imposed delay and pass when the dependency is restored; the existing mixed allocator provides a working deployment path. Full-pool allocation checks and service regression complete the validation. A four-block OFF-ON-ON-OFF comparison contains 768 measured requests within two block pairs. Median cross-replica time to first content token falls from 31.715 to 0.605 seconds at 128k input and from 92.047 to 0.790 seconds at 256k. Six-turn synthetic sessions alternating replicas improve by approximately 35% and 45% at initial contexts of 32k and 128k, while fixed placement shows little benefit. This engineering case study identifies practical validation steps and the locality conditions in which shared caching pays off.

</details>


### [14] [Validating Hybrid-State Cache Recovery for GLM-5.3-Flash with vLLM and LMCache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15030)
*Frank Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对 GLM-5.3-Flash 混合语言模型在外部缓存传输成功但从状态恢复时出现不一致的问题，提出了一种基于严格前缀查找与检查点对齐的集成修复方法，并在受控实验中验证了恢复一致性并带来性能收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部缓存传输可能"成功"返回，但模型从恢复的状态却是不一致的：缓存命中恢复完整提示词状态时，调度器却少记了一个 token。这种缓存与调度状态之间的错位会导致恢复不一致，需要修复以保障正确性与可用性。

Method: 在完整 45 层 GLM-5.3-Flash 模型上，使用 RedHatAI/GLM-5.3-Flash-NVFP4 量化检查点，结合 vLLM 与 LMCache，在四路张量并行下运行。通过严格前缀查找对齐恢复，并采用共享计算修正、匹配检查点调度与固定的每 rank 内核配置建立数值比较基准。

Result: 在九长度串行负载中，与修改后的重计算对照相比，一致性从 34/36 提升到 36/36 生成结果（每个含 64 个 token ID）。另一组插桩运行通过了传输页、有效尾部与延迟保存检查。三个额外合成模板在两个全新容器运行中通过了 72 组 256-token 配对续写。后续串行性能研究在 120 个请求中保持了输出相等；CPU 重载将首 token 时间降低 46–64%，总请求时间降低 1.9–7.0%。

Conclusion: 本工作贡献是一项经实验验证的集成修复，应用了已有的检查点对齐原则。证据仅限于单一模型版本与受控配置，不能推广到通用确定性、任务质量等价性、并发服务收益或超出 GPU 显存的容量。

Abstract: External cache transfers can succeed while a hybrid language model resumes from an inconsistent state. We examine the full 45-layer GLM-5.3-Flash model, using the RedHatAI/ GLM-5.3-Flash-NVFP4 quantized checkpoint with vLLM and LMCache under four-way tensor parallelism. A complete-hit recovery mismatch restored state for the full prompt while the scheduler credited one fewer token. We aligned recovery through strict-prefix lookup and established a numerical comparison using shared computation corrections, matched checkpoint scheduling, and fixed per-rank kernel configurations. In a nine-length serial workload, agreement with the modified recomputation control improved from 34/36 to 36/36 generations, each containing 64 token IDs. A separate instrumented run passed recorded transfer-page, effective-tail, and delayed-save checks. Three additional synthetic templates passed 72 paired 256-token continuations across two fresh-container runs. A subsequent serial performance study preserved output equality across 120 requests; among the measured trials, CPU reload reduced time to first token by 46-64% and total request time by 1.9-7.0% relative to modified cold recomputation. The contribution is an experimentally validated integration repair applying an existing checkpoint-alignment principle. The evidence is confined to one model revision and controlled configuration; it does not establish general determinism, task-quality equivalence, concurrent-serving gains, or capacity beyond GPU memory.

</details>


### [15] [Accelerating the Solving of Many Tiny General Linear Systems on GPUs: Application to Constitutive Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15217)
*Tristan Chenaille,Francesca Cuteri,Rapha{ë}l Prat,Guillaume Latu,Thomas Helfer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对GPU上求解维度小于32的微小型通用方阵线性系统（LUpp）难题，在TFEL/MFront材料本构评估场景中，比较了GPU线性代数库实现与自定义CUDA核函数，发现最优策略取决于系统规模与应用场景，自定义求解器在H100上相较cuSolverDx和MAGMA分别最高加速6.5倍与17.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用需要在GPU上求解大量相互独立的线性系统。虽然中大型系统已有成熟方案，但维度低于32的微小型系统仍具挑战性。该需求在本构律评估中尤为突出：需独立处理数百万个积分点，每个本构更新依赖牛顿迭代，每次迭代都需用带部分主元的LU分解（LUpp）对微小型通用方阵进行双精度求解。本研究旨在为将TFEL/MFront（CPU上的开源材料知识代码生成工具）的本构律评估移植到NVIDIA GPU提供演示与选型依据。

Method: 研究在闭源原型中开展，比较了多种双精度LUpp求解器，包括GPU线性代数库中的实现以及自定义设计的CUDA核函数。评估分两阶段：先在大批量独立线性系统上测试，再放入完整的本构律评估工作流中（每个积分点需在一系列不同线性系统上按其自身牛顿循环逐次求解）。重点分析了寄存器压力、占用率、能否直接从设备端代码调用LUpp求解器，以及为每个系统分配多个线程是否为最高效策略等关键因素。

Result: 实验表明最优LUpp求解策略强烈依赖系统规模和应用场景。在NVIDIA H100 GPU上，本工作提出的专用LUpp求解器在此类工作负载下可超越现有最先进方法，相比cuSolverDx最高加速6.5倍，相比MAGMA最高加速17.7倍。

Conclusion: 针对微小型线性系统的LUpp求解不存在通用最优方案，需结合系统规模与应用上下文（如本构律评估流程）进行选型；通过自定义CUDA核函数并优化寄存器压力、占用率、设备端调用能力及线程分配方式，可显著提升性能，为TFEL/MFront向GPU移植提供了可行且高效的求解方案。

Abstract: Many applications require solving large numbers of independent linear systems on GPUs. While this need is well addressed for small to large systems, tiny ones, understood here as systems of dimension below 32, remain challenging. This is especially relevant in constitutive law evaluation, where millions of integration points are handled independently, and where each constitutive update generally relies on a Newton iterative method. Each iteration then requires the double-precision solution of a tiny general square linear system using LU factorization with partial pivoting (LUpp). The present study was conducted within a closed-source prototype, which serves as a demonstrator for porting to NVIDIA GPUs constitutive law evaluations currently provided on CPUs by TFEL/MFront, an open-source code generation tool for material knowledge. We compare several double-precision LUpp solvers, including implementations from GPU linear algebra libraries as well as custom-designed CUDA kernels. The comparison is performed first on large batches of standalone linear systems, and then within the full constitutive-law evaluation workflow, where each integration point requires a sequence of distinct linear systems, one per iteration of its own Newton loop. The study shows that the best LUpp solving strategy strongly depends on several factors including system size and application context. We discuss several key aspects, including register pressure, occupancy, the ability to invoke the LUpp solver directly from device code, and whether assigning several threads to each system is the most efficient strategy. Experiments on an NVIDIA H100 GPU show that the specialized LUpp solvers proposed in this work can outperform existing state-of-the-art approaches for this class of workloads, with speedups of up to 6.5x over cuSolverDx, and up to 17.7x over MAGMA.

</details>


### [16] [ETCInfer: An Energy-efficient Thermal-aware Cooling-joint Scheduler for LLM Inference in AI Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15230)
*Rui Lu,Rui Ge,Huanghuang Liang,Xiaobo Zhou,Dan Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ETCInfer，一种热感知的LLM推理调度器，通过联合优化CRAC设定点、GPU频率与微批大小，在保证热安全与延迟SLO的前提下最小化每作业能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理在AI数据中心中形成GPU服务与设施冷却之间的耦合控制问题。提高环境温度设定点可降低冷却能耗与碳排放，但会压缩热裕度、引发GPU降频并导致SLO违规，因此需要联合冷却-计算控制。

Method: ETCInfer选择作业前的CRAC设定点，并在执行中自适应调整每GPU频率与微批大小。其通过遥测数据校准GPU发热、机箱散热、CRAC功耗及预填充/解码延迟关系，构建紧凑的物理信息控制模型，估计隐藏热状态与到达降频的时间。将联合设定点-频率-微批控制问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，并设计学习型控制器ETCAdapter在热安全与SLO约束下最小化每作业能耗，实现为推理与集群管理栈之上的协调层。

Result: 在真实轨迹仿真与验证实验中，ETCInfer将总作业能耗最多降低33.1%，热降频暴露最多降低92.9%，并在环境温度高达48°C时仍将SLO违规率保持在0.7%以下。

Conclusion: ETCInfer通过联合冷却-计算控制，在高温环境下有效降低能耗与热降频风险，同时维持低SLO违规率，验证了热感知调度在LLM推理场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference in AI datacenters creates a coupled control problem between GPU serving and facility cooling. Raising ambient temperature setpoints can reduce cooling energy and carbon, but also shrinks thermal headroom, induces GPU throttling, and leads to Service-Level-Objective (SLO) violations. In this paper, we study joint cooling--computing control for LLM inference: minimizing per-job GPU-plus-cooling energy while satisfying thermal safety and latency SLO constraints. We present ETCInfer, an energy-efficient, thermal-aware scheduler that selects a pre-job Computer Room Air Conditioner (CRAC) setpoint and adapts per-GPU frequency and micro-batch size during execution. ETCInfer builds compact physics-informed control models by calibrating GPU heat generation, chassis heat dissipation, CRAC power, and prefill/decode latency relations from telemetry. These models estimate hidden thermal states and time-to-throttle, enabling the scheduler to evaluate energy, temperature, and latency before applying an action. We formulate this joint setpoint--frequency--micro-batch control problem as a partially observable Markov decision process and design ETCAdapter, a learning-based controller that minimizes per-job energy under thermal safety and SLO constraints. We implement ETCInfer as a coordination layer over typical inference and cluster management stacks. Evaluation across real-trace simulation and validation experiments shows that ETCInfer reduces total job energy by up to 33.1%, thermal throttle exposure by up to 92.9%, and keeps SLO violation rates below 0.7% even at ambient temperatures up to $48^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$.

</details>


### [17] [CIDERS: Cloud-Edge LLM Collaborative Learning via Accelerating Personalized Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15664)
*Victor H. Chen,Hairui Yu,Stella K. Chung,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次将云边协同大语言模型形式化为个性化双层优化框架，并提出求解器CIDERS，在压缩边缘路径上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界智能快速发展背景下，云边协同LLM部署成为实用化路线，但现有云边范式难以兼顾全局共识与本地个性化，无法同时满足云端统一知识基础和边缘领域适配的需求。

Method: 提出个性化双层优化框架，将云边LLM协同建模为双重结构：上层优化边缘侧个性化，下层管理云端知识迁移。并设计高效求解器CIDERS，将模型分解为可学习骨干和信使，云端对骨干进行知识迁移，通过共识变量修正将全局轨迹嵌入每个本地个性化步骤。同时提供理论分析，包括局部轨迹的几何刻画和完整收敛性保证。

Result: CIDERS在压缩边缘路径上持续优于竞争基线，数学推理和代码生成分别提升3.1倍和1.7倍，指令指标相对提升10%。机制实验表明增益源于早期共识修正协调与任务感知蒸馏。

Conclusion: CIDERS为云边LLM系统中共识引导的持续个性化提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Amid the rapid advancement of physical-world intelligence, cloud-edge collaborative large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a promising roadmap for practical LLM deployment. However, existing cloud-edge paradigms struggle to balance global consensus with local personalization, which fails to satisfy the need for a unified knowledge foundation on the cloud and domain-specific adaptation at the edge. To address this, we introduce, for the first time, a personalized bilevel optimization framework that formalizes cloud-edge LLM collaboration as a dual structure: the upper level optimizes edge-side personalization, while the lower level governs cloud-side knowledge transfer, reaching cloud-edge evolving in coordination. We then propose CIDERS, an efficient solver that decomposes the model into a learnable backbone and a messenger. While the cloud performs knowledge transfer to the learnable backbone, the key lies in embedding global trajectories into each local personalization step via consensus-variate correction to reconcile personalization with consensus. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis, including a geometric characterization of the local trajectory and a full convergence guarantee, revealing an explicit trade-off structure between personalization and global convergence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CIDERS consistently outperforms competitive baselines on the compressed edge path, with 3.1x and 1.7x gains on mathematical reasoning and code generation, respectively, and a 10\% relative gain on instruction metrics. Mechanism experiments attribute these gains to early consensus-corrected coordination and task-aware distillation. Overall, CIDERS offers a viable path toward consensus-guided continuous personalization in cloud-edge LLM systems.

</details>


### [18] [Scalability and Performance Evaluation of Federated Learning Frameworks: A Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15681)
*Bassel Soudan,Sohail Abbas,Ahmed Kubba,Manar Wasif Abu Talib,Qassim Nasir*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对FedML、Flower、Substra和OpenFL四个联邦学习框架进行了系统的实验对比，重点考察可扩展性及性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习框架众多，用户在多样化的客户端规模下部署时缺乏对其相对性能的系统性认识，因此有必要进行实验对比以指导选型与部署。

Method: 在不同数量的客户端上实现并运行联邦学习，评估客户端数量增加对总训练时间、损失与准确率值、CPU和RAM使用率的影响。

Result: 各框架性能特征差异明显：Flower损失异常偏高；FedML准确率偏低（66%–79%）；Substra资源效率好但总训练时间呈指数增长；OpenFL最具可扩展性，在不同客户端数量下准确率、损失、训练时间均保持稳定，且CPU与RAM稳定。

Conclusion: 该分析揭示了各联邦学习框架的相对性能与能力，为在不同用户规模下有效部署提供了指导，其中OpenFL在真实场景中表现出较高的可靠性与可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper presents a systematic examination and experimental comparison of the prominent Federated Learning (FL) frameworks FedML, Flower, Substra, and OpenFL. The frameworks are evaluated experimentally by implementing Federated Learning over a varying number of clients, emphasizing a thorough analysis of scalability and key performance metrics. The study assesses the impact of increasing client counts on total training time, loss and accuracy values, and CPU and RAM usage. Results indicate distinct performance characteristics among the frameworks, with Flower displaying an unusually high loss, FedML achieving a notably low accuracy range of 66% to 79%, and Substra demonstrating good resource efficiency, albeit with an exponential growth in total training time. Notably, OpenFL emerges as the most scalable platform, demonstrating consistent accuracy, loss, and training time across different client counts. OpenFL's stable CPU and RAM underscore its reliability in real-world scenarios. This comprehensive analysis provides valuable insights into the relative performance of FL frameworks, offering good understanding of their capabilities and providing guidance for their effective deployment across diverse user bases.

</details>


### [19] [Cnuas: A Software-Defined AI/HPC Rack-scale Emulation Platform and Hyperscale Data Center Facility Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15889)
*Weqaar Janjua,Eoin OConnell,Mihai Penica*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cnuas 是一个开源、实验性的机架级仿真平台，遵循 OCP Open Rack v3 规范，通过功能仿真支持在商品化主机上进行 AI/HPC 基础设施的软件开发、学习与研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 与 HPC 系统在机架规模上集成了加速器、高速网络与管理控制器，但为其开发软件通常需要获取稀缺且昂贵的硬件，而且现有软件抽象会掩盖工作负载对跨服务器与加速器资源的依赖。因此需要一个可访问、可实验的机架级仿真环境。

Method: 作者提出 Cnuas 平台：基线架构遵循 OCP Open Rack v3 规范；提供基于 Web 的用户界面以可视化机架、设备及其互连；核心组件 CnuasNIC 与 CnuasSwitch 实现客户机可见的 RDMA 适配器和支持 RoCEv2 与原生 InfiniBand 的宿主驻留混合软件交换机；并提供专用 AI/ML 加速器（GPU）对等结构、基于 OpenBMC 的机架管理及基于 RS-485 的可执行电源与电池备份固件。

Result: 平台以功能仿真为目标，不追求匹配物理硬件的吞吐量或时延；可支持设备、驱动与固件接口的研究。加速器软件栈仍为早期研究原型，基于 OpenUSD 的设施建模属探索性扩展。论文给出了架构、接口以及有界的原型结果。

Conclusion: Cnuas 作为跨核心平台及其扩展的社区协作基础，为机架级系统的实验、教学与软件开发提供了开放的开源解决方案。

Abstract: Modern AI and HPC systems integrate accelerators, high-speed networks, and management controllers at rack scale. Developing software for this infrastructure typically requires access to scarce, costly hardware, while software abstractions can obscure how workloads depend on resources across servers and accelerators. This paper presents Cnuas, an open-source, experimental rack-scale emulation platform whose baseline architecture follows the Open Compute Project (OCP) Open Rack v3 specifications. Through functional emulation, it supports experimentation, learning and software development within academic and industrial research and development, rather than matching the throughput or latency of physical hardware. Its web-based user interface visualizes racks, devices and their interconnections to help developers build a system-level mental model of the infrastructure supporting their workloads. At its core, CnuasNIC and CnuasSwitch implement a guest-visible remote direct memory access (RDMA) adapter and a host-resident hybrid software switch supporting both RoCEv2 and native InfiniBand. The platform also provides a dedicated AI/ML accelerator (GPU) peer fabric and OpenBMC-based rack management with executable power supply and battery backup firmware over RS-485. These components support the study of device, driver, and firmware interfaces on commodity hosts. The accelerator software stack remains an early research prototype, and facility modeling with OpenUSD is an exploratory extension. The paper presents the architecture, interfaces, and bounded prototype results as a basis for community collaboration across the core platform and its extensions.

</details>


### [20] [Physically Partitioned KVCache Format for CPU--GPU Load Balancing in MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14507)
*Enda Yu,Dezun Dong,Xiangke Liao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出InplaceKVCache抽象与WriteScope运行时，通过在写入时固定每个字节的物理驻留状态，实现KV缓存的CPU-GPU动态负载均衡，在单GPU上支持MoE模型的长上下文推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单GPU运行MoE模型的长上下文推理时，KV缓存需溢出到CPU内存。溢出的KV有两种互补用途：传给GPU做注意力计算，或在CPU上就地计算，二者所需的物理状态相反。最优划分随工作负载变化，但现有KVCache抽象只提供单一物理状态的存储语义，无法表达动态负载均衡。

Method: 提出InplaceKVCache：首个在写入时即固定每个字节物理驻留位置的KVCache抽象，放置后无需移动数据即可调整CPU-GPU负载均衡。其采用两维度（设备亲和性与访问模式）的四区域布局，将负载均衡转化为纯调度问题。基于此构建WriteScope，沿序列维度切分CPU-GPU份额，并用可移植的roofline性能模型确定最优CPU份额，配合在线反馈跟踪CPU开销漂移。

Result: 在32GB显存预算下，三个MoE模型（DeepSeek-V2-Lite、Qwen3-30B-A3B、Mixtral-8×7B）支持1M token聚合规模的端到端推理。长上下文（≥8K）场景下，在A100上取得1.5×–2.5×、V100上1.4×–1.7×的几何平均加速，优于四个复现基线；而vLLM、SGLang、KTransformers即使KV预算翻倍仍失败。DeepSeek-V4-Flash案例验证了与原生稀疏注意力的组合能力。

Conclusion: InplaceKVCache通过写入时固定物理驻留的抽象，将KV负载均衡转为纯调度问题，WriteScope据此在单GPU长上下文MoE推理中实现显著加速并支持百万token规模，且可与原生稀疏注意力组合。

Abstract: Single-GPU long-context inference with Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models requires spilling the key-value cache (KVCache) to CPU memory. The spilled KV serves two complementary purposes---transferring to the GPU for attention computation, or computing in-place on the CPU---which demand opposing physical states. The optimal split between them varies with workload, yet existing KVCache abstractions offer only storage semantics over a monolithic object of a single physical state, and cannot express dynamic load balancing. We propose InplaceKVCache, the first KVCache abstraction whose format fixes each byte's physical residency at write time, so that the CPU--GPU load balance can be adjusted without moving data after placement. It realizes this as a four-region layout along two dimensions---device affinity and access pattern---turning load balancing into pure scheduling. Built on this abstraction, WriteScope splits CPU--GPU shares along the sequence dimension, and a portable roofline performance model determines the optimal CPU share as sequence length evolves, with online feedback tracking CPU cost drift. On three MoE models (DeepSeek-V2-Lite, Qwen3-30B-A3B, Mixtral-8$\times$7B) with a 32~GB VRAM budget, WriteScope supports end-to-end inference at the 1M-token aggregate scale. In the long-context regime ($\ge$8K), it achieves geometric-mean speedups of $1.5\times$--$2.5\times$ on A100 and $1.4\times$--$1.7\times$ on V100 over four reproduced baselines, while vLLM, SGLang, and KTransformers fail even with a doubled KV budget. A DeepSeek-V4-Flash case study validates composition with native sparse attention.

</details>


### [21] [TriCalRAG: A Three-Strategy, Retrieval-Augmented Benchmark for On-Premise LLM-Based Root Cause Analysis in AIOps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14762)
*Rohit Patel,Susil Kumar Mohanty,Jeenal Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TriCalRAG：本地开源LLM与DeepLog在AIOps根因分析中的基准评测，RAG显著提升F1并稳定校准，批处理与4-bit量化带来高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端托管LLM用于AIOps的根因分析存在数据隐私风险、网络延迟和按查询计费成本随日志量扩展性差的问题，因此需要评估可本地部署的开源权重方案。

Method: 构建TriCalRAG基准，在单张96GB高显存工作站GPU（NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000）上通过vLLM本地服务开源模型（Qwen2.5-14B、Mistral-Small），对比经典LSTM检测器DeepLog；在BGL、HDFS、Thunderbird、OpenStack四个公开日志数据集上，采用零样本、少样本、基于标注事件历史的RAG三种提示策略，报告准确率、精确率/召回率、F1及bootstrap 95%置信区间（3个随机种子），并测量吞吐与显存占用。

Result: RAG相比零样本使平均F1提升0.10-0.27，并大幅稳定模型校准：零样本使模型趋于退化（部分数据集上预测“异常”比例高达100%），RAG则使预测正例率在多数配置下接近真实类别分布。Mistral-Small宏平均F1高于Qwen2.5-14B（0.644 vs. 0.560），但校准失败配置更多（12个中7个 vs. 5个），吞吐约为后者一半。消融显示单卡批处理吞吐提升41倍，4-bit量化降低延迟20%且无明显精度损失。

Conclusion: 模型选择取决于部署是优先峰值准确率还是跨提示条件的可预测行为；本地部署配合RAG、批处理与量化可支撑可复现的本地化AIOps研究，并已开源基准框架、数据划分与评测代码。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for root cause analysis (RCA) in AIOps pipelines, but they introduce data privacy risk, network latency, and per-query cost that scale poorly with production log volumes. We present TriCalRAG, a benchmark evaluating open-weight LLMs served locally via vLLM on a single high-memory workstation GPU (NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000, 96GB) against a classical LSTM-based log anomaly detector (DeepLog), across four real, publicly available log datasets (BGL, HDFS, Thunderbird, OpenStack). We evaluate two open-weight models (Qwen2.5-14B, Mistral-Small) under three prompting strategies: zero-shot, few-shot, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over a labeled incident history, reporting accuracy, precision/recall, and F1 with bootstrap 95% confidence intervals across 3 random seeds, alongside throughput and VRAM footprint. Our results show that RAG not only improves mean F1 by 0.10-0.27 over zero-shot prompting but, more importantly, substantially stabilizes model calibration: zero-shot prompting drives both models toward near-degenerate behavior (predicting "anomaly" on up to 100% of incidents on some datasets), while RAG keeps predicted-positive rates close to the true class balance in the majority of configurations. Mistral-Small achieves higher macro-averaged F1 than Qwen2.5-14B (0.644 vs. 0.560) but exhibits calibration failures in more configurations (7 vs. 5 of 12), while running at roughly half the throughput - indicating the better model choice depends on whether a deployment prioritizes peak accuracy or predictable behavior across prompting conditions. Ablations show batching scales throughput 41 times on a single card and that 4-bit quantization reduces latency 20% with no measurable accuracy loss. We release our benchmark harness, dataset splits, and evaluation code to support reproducible on-premise AIOps research.

</details>

### [22] [Numerical Stability of Linear Algebra Operations over Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13802)
*Andrei Draghici,Yuchen He,Dan Olteanu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文首次对数据库连接矩阵上的线性代数与机器学习计算进行数值稳定性研究，提出投影后向稳定性和数据库条件数两个新概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库文献中有大量针对关系连接所定义矩阵的线性代数和机器学习高效算法，但这些计算的数值稳定性此前未受关注。连接矩阵可能远大于输入数据库，其中输入值的重复拷贝会放大浮点误差，这是一个实际且重要的隐患。

Method: 作者首先分析经典后向稳定性在该场景下的失效原因，指出连接矩阵构成环境矩阵空间的结构化子空间，使得解释计算结果的扰动可能不对应任何扰动后的输入数据库，并以矩阵-向量乘法为例说明；随后引入投影后向稳定性，将其定义为从单函数计算推广到两函数复合计算的稳定性概念，并建立其与经典后向稳定性的联系，在数据库场景中这两个函数分别为连接查询和数值操作；此外定义数据库条件数为输入数据值拷贝数量最大值与最小值之比的平方根。

Result: 研究发现经典后向稳定性在连接矩阵场景下失效，即使对矩阵-向量乘法这类简单操作也会发生；提出的投影后向稳定性作为两函数复合的推广概念，与经典后向稳定性建立了联系；数据库条件数能够量化连接矩阵扰动如何放大为输入数据库扰动，且该量化与具体计算无关。作者还证明数据库条件数与将输入值复制到连接矩阵的扩展矩阵的经典条件数一致。

Conclusion: 本文开创了数据库连接上线性代数数值稳定性的形式化研究，指出现有后向稳定性标准的不足，提出投影后向稳定性和数据库条件数两个新工具，为这一方向奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: A large body of work in the database literature develops efficient algorithms for linear algebra and machine learning over matrices defined by relational joins, yet the numerical stability of such computations has so far received no attention. This is a practical concern: a join matrix can be much larger than the input database, and the repeated copies of input values it contains compound the floating-point errors incurred by the numerical operations performed over it.
  This paper initiates a formal investigation of numerical stability for linear algebra over database joins. We first show that backward stability, the standard yardstick of numerical stability, loses its effectiveness in this setting: join matrices form a structured subspace of the ambient matrix space, so a perturbation explaining a computed result need not correspond to any perturbed input database. This failure already occurs for operations as simple as matrix-vector multiplication.
  To overcome this limitation, we introduce projected backward stability, a generalization of backward stability from the computation of one function to that of a composition of two functions, and establish its connection to classical backward stability. In our database setting, the two functions are the join query and the numerical operation.
  We further introduce the database condition number as the square root of the ratio of maximal to minimal number of copies of input data values into the join matrix, and show that it quantifies how a perturbation of the join matrix is amplified into a perturbation of the input database, independently of the computation used. The database condition number coincides with the classical condition number of the expansion matrix that replicates input values into the join matrix.

</details>


### [23] [FastPair: GPU-Optimized String Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15034)
*Joseph Isaacs,Francesco Gargiulo,Peter Boncz,Robert Kruszewski,Nicholas Gates,Rossano Venturini,Will Manning,Martin Prammer*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FastPair是一种GPU字典解码器，通过重组查找并将解码子串组装为连续写出来优化解码过程，在B300上比DE快2.4-4.2倍，最高达1.6 TB/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据系统在存储时压缩数据、仅在需要时解压以保护互连带宽，但这种设计在GPU平台上往往效率低下。传统压缩技术存在串行数据依赖，限制了GPU并行性，导致资源闲置。NVIDIA GPU通过解压引擎（DE）硬件加速器解决解码缺陷，支持Deflate、LZ4、Snappy等格式。近期提出的字符串编解码将频繁子串替换为小型训练字典中的固定宽度代码，使每次代码查找独立、可并行，但由此产生的分散读取和短输出写入与GPU硬件不匹配，因为GPU更擅长处理连续内存访问。

Method: 提出FastPair，一种GPU解码器。它优化现有字典解码过程，通过重组查找并将解码后的子串组装起来，以实现连续输出写入。

Result: 在B300 GPU上，FastPair对十个真实世界列的解码速度比DE快2.4至4.2倍，吞吐量最高达到1.6 TB/s。

Conclusion: FastPair通过将分散的字典查找重组并组装为连续输出写入，有效解决了GPU上字典解码与硬件访问模式不匹配的问题，显著提升了真实数据列的解码性能。

Abstract: Modern data systems compress data at rest and decompress it only when needed to preserve interconnect bandwidth. This design is often inefficient on GPU-based compute platforms because many conventional compression techniques exhibit serial data dependencies that limit GPU parallelism, leaving resources idle. Recent NVIDIA GPUs address this decoding deficiency through the Decompression Engine (DE), an on-die, fixed-function decompression accelerator for general-purpose compression formats such as Deflate, LZ4, and Snappy. Recent work has proposed string codecs that replace frequent substrings with fixed-width codes from a small, trained dictionary, making each code's lookup independent. While these lookups can run in parallel, the resulting scattered reads and short output writes still do not align well with GPU hardware, which handles contiguous memory accesses more efficiently. We present FastPair, a GPU decoder that optimizes the existing dictionary decoding process by reorganizing lookups and assembling decoded substrings for contiguous output writes. On a B300, FastPair decodes ten real-world columns 2.4 to 4.2x faster than the DE, reaching up to 1.6 TB/s.

</details>


### [24] [COMPASS: Steering Distributed Vector Search with Scientific Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13452)
*Song Young Oh,Amal Gueroudji,Seth Ockerman,Rob Latham,Orcun Yildiz,Ian Foster,Kyle Chard,Robert Ross*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: COMPASS 利用知识图谱结构进行向量数据的放置与查询时分片选择，在生物医学图谱上仅搜索少量语料即可保持召回，并显著提升多跳证据获取能力和系统吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库用哈希分片会破坏语义局部性，导致每条查询需 scatter-gather 且受最慢分片拖累。向量空间聚类虽有帮助，但科学证据常由事实关系连接，这种关系与嵌入距离并不一致。

Method: COMPASS 使用知识图谱决定数据放置与查询时分片选择：检测社区、拆分过大社区、按主体实体插入嵌入，并将查询路由到少量分片。

Result: 在四个生物医学知识图谱上，方法仅搜索语料的 13-18%，同时保持广播召回，并比基于嵌入的基线多恢复至多 2.6 倍多跳证据。在 15 个 HPC 节点上，吞吐比哈希广播高 7.9 倍，且尾延迟更低。

Conclusion: 知识图谱结构为可扩展向量搜索提供了对嵌入几何的紧凑补充。

Abstract: Vector databases use hashing to partition data across "shards," logical units for distributed execution. This placement, however, destroys semantic locality, forcing each query into scatter-gather limited by the slowest shard. Vector-space clustering can help, but scientific evidence is often connected by factual relations that do not align with embedding distance. We present COMPASS, a framework that uses a knowledge graph (KG) to determine data placement and query-time shard selection. COMPASS detects communities, splits oversized communities, inserts embeddings by subject entity, and routes queries to a small set of shards. Across four biomedical KGs, our method searches only 13-18% of the corpus while preserving broadcast recall and recovering up to 2.6x more multi-hop evidence than an embedding-based baseline. On 15 HPC nodes, COMPASS sustains 7.9x higher throughput with lower tail latency than hash-based broadcast. These results show that KG structure provides a compact complement to embedding geometry for scalable vector search.

</details>
