### [25] [Abstention Errors and Segment Support in CUAD: An Auditable Contract-Extraction Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13274)
*Zeki Emre Tekin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对固定公开RoBERTa检查点在CUAD 102份合同测试集上进行回顾性可复现评估，修正匹配错误并重选类别阈值后，发现参考跨度召回82.2%但CUAD精度仅8.2%，返回字符串中大量出现在无标注答案问题上，45.4%为假阳性，凸显参考精度与实际复核负担需用不同分母衡量，且多数类别缺少足够的正负样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合同条款提取基准主要衡量参考恢复（recall），但用于复核辅助的系统评估还需衡量不必要的输出以及各类别内可用证据。论文动机在于揭示参考精度与候选级复核负担的差异，并对现有评估指标与实际使用场景的错位进行可复现的再评估。

Method: 在CUAD公开的102份合同测试集上，使用固定公开RoBERTa检查点进行回顾性、可复现评估。候选生成沿用此前冻结运行；在62份训练集合同上修正匹配错误，并以原始90%召回目标重选类别阈值。分析返回候选字符串的分布、无标注答案问题上的假阳性率（含合同bootstrap置信区间），并统计满足正负样本最少数要求的类别数量。

Result: 修正后的工作点恢复82.2%的参考跨度，CUAD精度8.2%。30,464个返回候选字符串中有19,562个出现在无标注答案的问题上；系统回答了2,938个此类问题中的1,335个，假阳性率45.4%（95%合同bootstrap区间43.3–47.9%）。匹配候选仅占返回字符串的20.3%。41个类别中仅9个拥有至少30个正样本和30个负样本合同；在支持度下限为20和40时，该数量分别变为18和2。两个类别没有负样本合同，其无答案假阳性率无法定义。

Conclusion: 这些发现仅描述单一检查点、解码器和阈值策略。此前的测试集访问以及检查点训练重叠的不确定性限制了确认性解读。论文提供了原始与修正分析、预测和软件检查作为附属产物；既不建立新的发布标准，也不代表已达到生产就绪。

Abstract: Contract-clause extraction benchmarks measure reference recovery, but interpreting a system for review assistance also requires measuring unnecessary output and the evidence available within categories. This paper presents a retrospective, reproducible evaluation of a fixed public RoBERTa checkpoint on CUAD's published 102-contract test split. Candidate generation is preserved from an earlier frozen run; matching errors are corrected and category thresholds are reselected on 62 training-split contracts using the original 90% recall target. The corrected operating point recovers 82.2% of reference spans at 8.2% CUAD precision. Of 30,464 returned candidate strings, 19,562 occur on questions with no annotated answer. The system answers 1,335 of 2,938 such questions, a 45.4% false-positive rate (95% contract-bootstrap interval: 43.3-47.9%). Matching candidates constitute 20.3% of returned strings, illustrating why reference-based precision and candidate-level review burden require different denominators. Only nine of 41 categories have at least 30 positive and 30 negative contracts; this count changes to 18 and two under support minima of 20 and 40. Two categories have no negative contracts, making their no-answer false-positive rates undefined. These findings describe one checkpoint, decoder, and threshold policy. Earlier test access and uncertain checkpoint training overlap limit confirmatory interpretation. The paper supplies the original and corrected analyses, predictions, and software checks as ancillary artifacts; it establishes neither a new release criterion nor production readiness.

</details>


### [26] [SkillSeam: Six Principles for Auditing Agent Skill Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13321)
*Kang Ruiyuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 SkillSeam 方法，将技能库的六个设计原则转化为可测试的系统属性，通过扰动实验检验技能集合"接缝处"的失败机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单独看技能都很胜任，但组合成系统后技能常在"接缝"处失效：随着技能库增长，程序互相争夺注意力、别名重复加载、边界模糊、粒度过大或过小，使路由错误演变为任务失败。现有评估多只看准确率，无法捕捉这些集合层面的失败。

Method: SkillSeam 审计技能文件彼此关系如何构成系统。它将每个集合级原则映射到失败机制、最强可观测信号和受控扰动测试。在一个封闭技能系统上扰动六个设计原则：持久性梯度、系统一致性、状态门控、正交覆盖、流动、粒度纪律。关键点在于按失败机制所预测的通道来评估，而非仅看准确率。

Result: 压平持久性层级使加载技能 token 增加 60%；悬空锚点使总 token 增加 64%、准确率变化 -3.1pp；在技能数和上下文固定时，用同义别名替换无关对照使非规范路由从 0/32 升至 15/32，并翻转一半匹配释义对；候选归属审计中重叠车道使报告的所有权冲突从 0/16 升至 14/16；平淡触发器使路由冲突从 3/32 升至 30/32，加载 token 膨胀 3.7 倍；一种粒度错配造成最大准确率下降 -12.5pp。

Conclusion: 这些结果把六条写作建议变成可测试的系统属性，同时不把每个探测都当作确认。作者发布字节差分变体、任务切片、汇总和单屏设计清单，供其他技能系统测量相同失败通道。

Abstract: A folder of competent skills is not yet a reliable system. Skills rarely fail alone; they
  fail at the seams of a collection. As an agent's skill library grows, procedures compete for
  attention, aliases double-load, boundaries blur, and poorly sized skills turn routing errors
  into task failures. We introduce SkillSeam, a method that audits the relationships through
  which individual skill files become a system. It maps each collection-level principle to a
  failure mechanism, its strongest observable, and a controlled perturbation test. From one
  sealed skill system, SkillSeam perturbs six design principles: persistence gradient, system
  coherence, regime gating, orthogonal coverage, flow, and granularity discipline. Crucially,
  each principle is evaluated through the channel its failure mechanism predicts rather than
  through accuracy alone. Flattening the persistence hierarchy increases loaded-skill tokens
  by 60%; a dangling anchor raises total tokens by 64% and shifts accuracy by -3.1pp; with
  skill count and context size held fixed, replacing an unrelated control with a synonymous
  alias raises noncanonical routes from 0/32 to 15/32 and flips half of matched paraphrase
  pairs; in a candidate-ownership audit, overlapping lanes raise reported ownership conflicts
  from 0/16 to 14/16; bland triggers drive routing conflicts from 3/32 to 30/32 and inflate
  loaded-skill tokens 3.7x; and one granularity mis-mix produces the largest accuracy drop,
  -12.5pp. These outcomes turn six pieces of authoring advice into testable system properties
  without treating every probe as confirmation. We release the byte-differenced variants, task
  slices, rollups, and a one-screen design checklist so that other skill systems can measure
  the same failure channels.

</details>


### [27] [The Agentic Company OS: Substrate Inversion for Sustained Enterprise Agent Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13334)
*Oliver Aleksander Larsen,Mahyar T. Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该立场论文提出，企业AI智能体难以长期稳定运行的根本原因在于其推理所依赖的数据结构是为人类操作者和传统应用设计的，而非为语言模型设计。作者主张围绕"认知基底"重建共享环境，将模式转换隔离到行动边界，并以四层框架（数据、知识、智能、治理）加以操作化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI智能体常在演示中成功，但在日复一日的持续运行中却停滞不前。行业报告估计多数试点无法进入生产，已部署系统很少保留反馈或随时间改进，而智能体基准测试显示单次成功掩盖了重复运行的不稳定性。作者认为这些失败模式共享一个共同的架构根源：智能体在为人设计的结构化数据上进行推理。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文，通过两个机制来支撑其论点：(1) 上下文带宽不对称——单次阅读连贯文本与逐字段类型化访问（后者剥离了关系）之间的差距；(2) 跨回路耦合——行动、技能和政策回路的复合效应仅在共享同一基底时才会发生。作者提出四层框架（数据、知识、智能、治理），由同步智能体（Sync Agent）强制实施行动边界，并采用逐技能的信任梯度。

Result: 该立场在LLM时代的经济学下复兴了经典多智能体系统的共享基底传统。作者分析了主要反对意见和风险，包括编译路径上的间接提示注入。

Conclusion: 作者主张部署持续运行智能体的企业应重建其认知基底，围绕与推理表面匹配的表示构建共享环境，将模式转换隔离到行动边界。Markdown是当下可用的实例，但并非已证实的智能体原生原语。作者还提出了直接评估基底的研究议程。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents often succeed in a demonstration and then stall once they must operate day after day. An industry report estimates that most pilots never reach production and that deployed systems rarely retain feedback or improve over time, while agent benchmarks show single-run successes masking unreliable repetition. We argue that these failure modes share a common architectural root: agents reason over data structured for human operators and traditional applications, not for the language models that power them. This position paper proposes that companies deploying agents in sustained operation should rebuild their cognitive substrate, the shared environment agents read as working context, around representations matched to that reasoning surface, isolating schema translation to the action boundary. Markdown is the instantiation available today, not a proven agent-native primitive. Two mechanisms ground the argument: context-bandwidth asymmetry, the gap between one-pass reading of connected prose and field-by-field typed access that strips relations; and cross-loop coupling, the claim that action, skill, and policy loops compound only if they share one substrate. A four-layer framework (Data, Knowledge, Intelligence, Governance) operationalizes the position, with a Sync Agent enforcing the action boundary and a per-skill trust gradient, making governance and auditability structural properties of the substrate. The position revives the shared-substrate tradition of classical multi-agent systems under LLM-era economics. We analyze the main objections and risks, including indirect prompt injection on the compile path, and outline a research agenda for evaluating substrates directly.

</details>


### [28] [Usage Control in Public Data Exchange Infrastructures for Future Digital Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13450)
*Oleksandr Kosenkov,Fathiyeh Faghih,Eric Jackson,Theo Dimitrakos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文从跨学科视角分析公共数据交换基础设施（以爱沙尼亚X-Road为例）中数据使用控制的实际挑战与需求，并提出概念性愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共数据交换基础设施（如X-Road）对数据密集型数字公共服务和欧盟数字经济至关重要。其下一代需要先进的数据使用控制机制以满足隐私设计等新兴需求，缓解大规模自动化数据交换带来的隐私威胁。虽然各单一学科已有研究，但在学科交叉处出现显著的实际挑战。

Method: 采用跨学科视角，整合使用控制的形式化验证与监控、需求工程以及数字治理研究，对爱沙尼亚X-Road系统及新兴用例进行分析性考察。

Result: 提炼出公共数据交换基础设施中数据使用控制的关键需求，并识别出在学科交叉处的实际挑战。

Conclusion: 提出一个概念性愿景，用于发展公共数据交换基础设施中的使用控制，使未来数字治理系统能够通过设计满足隐私要求。

Abstract: Public data exchange infrastructures, such as X-Road, are essential to data-intensive digital public services and the EU digital economy. Their next generation requires advanced data usage control (DUC) mechanisms that address emerging requirements, including privacy by design, to mitigate privacy threats inherent in large-scale automated data exchange. Although research within the individual disciplines already addresses many of the emerging requirements, significant practical challenges arise at the intersection of the disciplines.
  This paper analytically examines the practical challenges and requirements of data usage control in public data exchange infrastructures. We examine the Estonian X-Road system and emerging use cases through an interdisciplinary lens that integrates formal verification and monitoring of usage control, requirements engineering, and digital governance research. We formulate key requirements and propose a conceptual vision for developing usage control in public data-exchange infrastructures to enable future digital-governance systems to comply with privacy requirements by design.

</details>


### [29] [Rethinking Software Development as a Self-Adaptive Socio-Technical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13461)
*Javier Cámara*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将软件开发视为自适应社会技术系统，软件项目与开发配置（参与者、职责、权限、信息、验证）随目标、证据、不确定性和风险共同演化，从而产生软件演化与工程执行方式重构两种耦合的自适应形式，并通过概念验证进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI 正扩展到各类软件工程活动中，但人-智能体组织通常被视为固定的，这在开发情境演变时可能导致配置次优，因此需要重新思考开发配置的动态调整问题。

Method: 提出将软件开发视为自适应社会技术系统的概念框架，其中软件项目与开发配置（参与者、职责、权限、信息、验证）随目标、证据、不确定性和风险的变化而调整；并用概念验证（proof of concept）进行说明。

Result: 提出了两种耦合的自适应形式：一是软件本身的演化，二是对后续工程执行方式的重构；通过概念验证展示了该思路的可行性，并概述了相关挑战与未来计划。

Conclusion: 人-智能体组织不应被视为固定不变，而应作为自适应社会技术系统的一部分进行动态重构；该方向尚处概念验证阶段，仍面临挑战并有待未来研究推进。

Abstract: Agentic AI is expanding across various software engineering activities, yet human-agent organization is typically treated as fixed, which is problematic because development configurations may become suboptimal as development context evolves. We propose viewing software development as a self-adaptive socio-technical system in which both the software project and the development configuration (participants, responsibilities, authority, information, and verification) adapt as goals, evidence, uncertainty, and risk evolve. This yields two coupled forms of adaptation: evolving the software and reconfiguring how subsequent engineering is performed. We illustrate the idea with a proof of concept, outlining challenges and future plans.

</details>


### [30] [Deterministic Temporal Reconciliation for Effective-Dated Identity Lifecycle Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14017)
*Pramod Ubbala*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对生效日期身份事件的确定性时间对账方法，分离逻辑事实与物理修订，去重、按事务时间取权威修订、剔除取消事实，再按有效时间顺序应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份生命周期自动化涉及两种时间概念：业务事实的生效时间与权威源的修订时间。纠错、取消、未来生效变更、重复投递和乱序到达会使仅依赖传输顺序或生效时间的处理器产生状态分歧，因而需要一种确定性对账方法。

Method: 方法将逻辑业务事实与其物理修订分离；对完全重复投递去重；依据事务时间新鲜度选择每个事实的权威修订；移除已取消事实；将存活事实按有效时间顺序应用。并给出排列不变性、重放幂等性、修订支配性和确定性快照构造等性质。

Result: 在250,000条合成身份历史、1,585,589条投递记录的可复现故障注入基准中，所提方法在所有测试案例中重建出权威最终状态。75%投递乱序时，精确状态准确率为：到达顺序42.35%、有效时间排序85.38%、事务时间排序76.61%、所提方法100%。另在40,000次排列实验中，每种排列均得到相同最终状态。

Conclusion: 该时间对账契约在明确源假设下具备确定性与鲁棒性，显著优于到达顺序、有效时间和事务时间处理方式。

Abstract: Identity lifecycle automation processes workforce changes that carry two distinct notions of time: when a business fact becomes effective and when an authoritative source last revised it. Corrections, cancellations, future-dated changes, duplicate delivery, and out-of-order arrival can therefore produce divergent account states when processors rely on transport order or effective time alone. This paper presents a deterministic temporal reconciliation method for effective-dated identity events. The method separates logical business facts from their physical revisions, deduplicates exact replays, selects the authoritative revision of each fact using transaction-time freshness, removes canceled facts, and then applies surviving facts in valid-time order. We establish permutation invariance, replay idempotence, revision dominance, and deterministic snapshot construction under explicit source assumptions. A reproducible synthetic fault-injection benchmark compares the method with arrival-order, effective-time, and transaction-time processing. Across 250,000 generated identity histories and 1,585,589 delivered records, the proposed method reconstructed the separately retained authoritative final state in all tested cases. At 75% delivery disorder, exact-state accuracy was 42.35% for arrival-order processing, 85.38% for effective-time sorting, 76.61% for transaction-time sorting, and 100% for the proposed method. A separate 40,000-permutation experiment produced the same final state for every tested permutation under the proposed reconciliation contract.

</details>


### [31] [EchoFuzz: Empowering Smart Contract Fuzzing with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14475)
*Juanen Li,Peng Qian,Guanyan Li,Rui Wang,Peixin Wang,Zhiqing Tang,Fuchen Ma,Yuanliang Chen,Lun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 EchoFuzz，一个 LLM 引导的智能合约模糊测试框架，通过引入漏洞函数调用序列（VFCS）来消除状态转移的组合冗余并引导深度漏洞路径探索。实验表明其分支覆盖率提升 29%，漏洞检测数提升 62%，并在真实合约中发现 37 个未知漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约管理着巨额数字资产，是攻击的重要目标。现有模糊测试方法面临两大挑战：（1）状态转移的逻辑断层与组合冗余，导致难以在缺陷检测效率与状态空间探索成本之间取得有效平衡，关键执行路径被忽视；（2）基于规则的序列变异策略存在路径冗余，且缺乏合约逻辑的有效引导，造成性能瓶颈，阻碍了面向深度漏洞路径的探索。

Method: EchoFuzz 引入"漏洞函数调用序列（VFCS）"概念——即暴露缺陷的最小、保持行为的执行路径，通过关键状态转移来触发漏洞。该框架包含两个关键步骤：第一，设计链引导的 LLM 方法，结合静态分析与逻辑理解，生成合约特定的 VFCS 候选，从而消除组合冗余；第二，采用迭代模糊测试策略，利用带实时反馈的 LLM 自适应地将模糊器引导向未覆盖分支。

Result: 实验显示 EchoFuzz 优于当前最先进方法：分支覆盖率提升 29%，检测到的漏洞数量增加 62%；在真实合约中还发现 37 个此前未知的漏洞，展现出很强的实用性。

Conclusion: EchoFuzz 通过 LLM 引导的 VFCS 生成与迭代反馈式模糊测试策略，有效缓解了状态空间冗余与序列变异引导不足的问题，在智能合约漏洞检测的覆盖率与发现能力上均取得显著提升，并具备真实场景的落地价值。

Abstract: Smart contracts, serving as the cornerstone of decentralized applications, autonomously manage trillion-dollar digital assets, making them attractive targets for attacks. Fuzzing has emerged as a promising technique for detecting vulnerabilities in smart contracts, yet existing methods face two main challenges. (1) The logical gap in state transitions and combinatorial redundancy hinders effective tradeoffs between bug detection efficiency and state space exploration cost, leading to critical execution paths to be overlooked. (2) Rule-based sequence mutation strategies suffer from path redundancy and inadequate guidance from contract logic, resulting in performance bottlenecks that stall the exploration of in-depth vulnerability-oriented paths.
  To tackle these challenges, we propose EchoFuzz, an LLM-guided fuzzing framework introducing Vulnerable Function Call Sequences (VFCS) - minimal, behavior-preserving execution paths that expose bugs through key state transitions. EchoFuzz consists of two key procedures. First, we develop a chain-guided LLM approach, that combines static analysis with logical understanding to generate contract-specific VFCS candidates that eliminate combinatorial redundancy. Second, we adopt an iterative fuzzing strategy that uses LLMs with real-time feedback to adaptively steer fuzzer toward uncovered branches. Experiments show EchoFuzz outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving 29\% higher branch coverage and detecting 62\% more vulnerabilities. It also found 37 previously unknown vulnerabilities in real contracts, showing strong practicality.

</details>


### [32] [Enhancing Automated Unit Test Generation for NLP Libraries Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14784)
*Amirhossein Deljouyi,Annibale Panichella,Andy Zaidman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMSuite是一种混合测试生成框架，将自精炼提示与类级LLM推理融入基于搜索的测试流程，针对Java NLP库提升覆盖率与变异得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化单元测试工具（如EvoSuite）在通用软件上表现良好，但在NLP等需要满足语义、句法和结构约束的领域特定软件上效果不佳；而纯LLM生成的测试常无法编译或覆盖不足，因此需要结合两者优势。

Method: 提出LLMSuite混合框架：LLM基于前几代反馈迭代精炼测试片段，生成更精确、符合领域约束的代码；当进化算法多代无目标提升时，将精炼片段解析并注入EvoSuite种群以扩展搜索空间。同时构建含5个Java NLP项目、100个类的新数据集，并用Java重实现CodaMOSA进行对比。

Result: 相比CodaMOSA-J，分支和行覆盖率约提升10%和8%，变异得分高11%；相比EvoSuite，分支和行覆盖率约高15%，变异得分高5%；相比纯LLM基线，结构覆盖率提升36%，变异得分提升约24.7个百分点；还能补充人工测试未覆盖的领域特定行为。

Conclusion: 将自精炼LLM推理与基于搜索的测试相结合，可有效应对领域特定软件的测试难题，在覆盖率和缺陷检测能力上显著优于现有方法与基线。

Abstract: Automated unit test generation tools like EvoSuite perform well on general-purpose software but often struggle with domain-specific software such as Natural Language Processing (NLP) libraries, where inputs must follow semantic, syntactic, and structural constraints. Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate domain-relevant test code, but tests produced by LLMs alone often fail to compile or achieve sufficient coverage. We propose LLMSuite, a hybrid test generation framework that integrates self-refinement prompting with class-level LLM reasoning into the search-based testing process. In this mechanism, the LLM iteratively improves its test snippets based on feedback from previous generations. This enables the model to produce increasingly precise, domain-consistent code fragments that steer the evolutionary search toward exercising complex and otherwise hard-to-reach behaviors. When no objective improves over multiple generations in the underlying evolutionary algorithm, these refined snippets are parsed and injected into EvoSuite's population to expand the search space. To support our evaluation, we constructed a new dataset comprising 100 classes drawn from five widely used Java NLP projects. We also re-implemented CodaMOSA, a recent hybrid SBST-LLM technique, in Java to enable a direct comparison. Across this dataset, LLMSuite improves branch and line coverage by approximately 10% and 8%, respectively, and achieves an 11% higher mutation score than CodaMOSA-J. Compared to EvoSuite, LLMSuite yields roughly 15% higher branch and line coverage and 5% higher mutation score. Against an LLM-only baseline, it improves structural coverage by 36% and mutation score by about 24.7 percentage points. Finally, LLMSuite complements manually written test suites by exercising domain-specific behaviors that are often left untested.

</details>


### [33] [Trust by Design: Trust Calibration Through Non-Advisory Socratic Dialogue in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14818)
*Roba Hassan,Nahla Aboromi,Naomi Unkelos-Shpigel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CASELy——一个通过非建议式苏格拉底对话主动限制自身权威的对话智能体，以实现信任校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式AI进入敏感领域，核心挑战从可用性转向信任校准，即避免用户过度依赖或依赖不足。提供建议或解读的系统易引发不当依赖，尤其当用户视AI输出为权威时。

Method: 设计CASELy智能体，通过非建议式苏格拉底对话约束自身能动性：仅基于用户输入提出反思性问题，拒绝给出建议、推荐或解读。随后以高等教育学生为对象开展试点随机对照研究。

Result: 苏格拉底对话组的用户体验显著更高（UEQ-S总分=1.50），非对话对照组为0。质性分析识别出三种支持信任校准的机制：通过可见依据实现透明性、保留用户决策权、降低被评判的恐惧。

Conclusion: 适当的依赖可通过交互性约束实现，为敏感情境下可信对话式AI提供了一种设计范式。

Abstract: As conversational AI systems increasingly operate in sensitive domains, the central challenge shifts from usability to trust calibration, ensuring that users rely on systems neither too much nor too little. Systems that provide advice or interpretations risk encouraging inappropriate reliance, particularly when users perceive AI outputs as authoritative. We present CASELy, a conversational agent explicitly designed to limit its own authority through non-advisory Socratic dialogue. The agent asks reflective questions grounded exclusively in user input and refuses to provide advice, recommendations, or interpretations. This design operationalizes trust calibration by constraining agent agency rather than optimizing capability. In a pilot randomized controlled study with higher education students, participants interacting with the Socratic dialogue reported substantially higher user experience (UEQ-S overall = 1.50) compared to a non-dialogue control (0). Qualitative findings identify three mechanisms supporting calibrated trust: transparency through visible grounding, preservation of user decision authority, and reduced fear of judgment. We argue that appropriate reliance can be achieved through interactional constraints, offering a design pattern for trustworthy conversational AI in sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [34] [Toward Sustainable AI Deployment: A Carbon-Aware Decision Framework for Enterprise Supply Chain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14881)
*Haoran Yu,Lifei Liu,Danping Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对6个大语言模型在520个供应链任务上同时测评决策质量与碳排放，提出碳感知AI采购框架（CAAPF），表明"先基准测试、后绿色选择"可实现质量与环保双赢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业采购AI时习惯默认选用最大参数模型，这一启发式忽视了实际性能与环境成本，缺乏对可持续AI治理的实证依据与设计工具。

Method: 基于TOE框架开发CAAPF（绿色IS设计artifact）；对6个LLM在520个供应链任务上做基准测试，同步测量决策质量与生成相关运营碳估算；并构建类别-层级校准的GreenRoute概念验证。

Result: 质量区间0.497–0.723，参数量最大者未获最高分；GreenRoute达0.733平均样本外质量、约0.402 gCO2/任务；静态Haiku为0.699、0.022 gCO2/任务，Sonnet为0.723、0.401 gCO2/任务，说明最优策略取决于组织的质量要求。研究未分离规模、供应商、架构或基准构建效应。

Conclusion: "先基准测试、后绿色选择"原则显示环境责任与决策质量可相互促进，为企业可持续数字基础设施治理及SDG 12、SDG 13提供支撑。

Abstract: Enterprises deploying AI for supply chain decisions commonly default to the largest available language model, a procurement heuristic that neglects both empirical performance and environmental cost. We benchmark six large language models across 520 supply chain tasks, simultaneously measuring decision quality and estimated generation-related operational carbon. Drawing on the Technology-Organization-Environment (TOE) framework, we develop a Carbon-Aware AI Procurement Framework (CAAPF), a Green IS design artifact that operationalizes sustainable AI governance for enterprise procurement. Within this bounded sample, quality spans 0.497-0.723, and the models with the largest disclosed parameter totals do not achieve the highest scores. The design does not isolate size, provider, architecture, or benchmark-construction effects. A category-by-tier calibrated GreenRoute proof of concept reaches 0.733 mean out-of-sample quality at an estimated 0.402 gCO2/task. Static Haiku reaches 0.699 at 0.022 gCO2/task, while Sonnet reaches 0.723 at 0.401 gCO2/task, demonstrating that the preferred strategy depends on the organization's quality requirement. Our "benchmark first, select green" principle suggests that environmental responsibility and decision quality can be mutually reinforcing, contributing to sustainable digital infrastructure governance aligned with SDG 12 and SDG 13.

</details>


### [35] [Failure-Guided Co-Evolution of Prompts and Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15209)
*Tianyu Yuan,Zhuzhong Qian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FORGE 提出一种失败引导的框架，让提示词与训练数据协同演化：把每次失败同时视为修正提示词的信号和合成新训练数据的信号。在 8 个异构基准上，聚合得分较未优化基线提升 16.52 个百分点，并优于所有对比的 APO 基线；合成数据还能迁移到其他方法，带来 2–9 分（APO）和 4–8 分（GRPO）的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动提示词优化依赖固定的训练数据，反复针对同一批样例优化，只能暴露并修补这批数据中已有的弱点，相关的失败情形无法被探索，反馈信息受限。

Method: 将每次失败视为双重信号：既指示提示词应如何修改，也指示应合成哪些新训练证据。FORGE 把不完美的执行抽象为可复用的失败模式，通过四种互补的变异策略合成新训练数据；经校验的实例被回馈到提示词搜索中，使更新后的提示词暴露下一轮数据需求，从而让提示词与数据协同演化。

Result: 在 8 个异构基准上，聚合得分较未优化基线提升 16.52 个百分点，优于所有评估的 APO 基线；在迁移研究中，合成数据在相同优化预算下使全部 9 个 APO 对比提升 2–9 分，全部 3 个 GRPO 对比提升 4–8 分。

Conclusion: 失败可作为提示词优化与数据合成之间的共享接口，联合调整"模型被指示去做什么"与"模型从中学习什么"能带来收益。

Abstract: Automatic prompt optimization (APO) improves language-model programs by revising prompts from task feedback, yet it typically holds its training data fixed. Repeatedly optimizing against the same instances confines feedback to weaknesses already represented in those data, leaving related failure conditions unexplored. We therefore view each failure as a dual signal: it indicates both how the prompt should be revised and what new training evidence should be synthesized. We introduce FORGE, a failure-guided framework that co-evolves prompts and training data. FORGE abstracts imperfect executions into reusable failure modes and synthesizes new training data through four complementary mutation strategies. Verified instances are fed back into prompt search, allowing updated prompts to expose the next data needs. Across eight heterogeneous benchmarks, FORGE improves the aggregate score over the unoptimized baseline by 16.52 percentage points and outperforms all evaluated APO baselines. The synthesized data also transfer beyond FORGE: in a transfer study, they improve all nine APO comparisons by 2--9 points and all three GRPO comparisons by 4--8 points under matched optimization budgets. These results establish failures as a shared interface between prompt optimization and data synthesis, and show the benefit of jointly adapting what a model is instructed to do and what it learns from.

</details>


### [36] [When Digitalization Transforms Itself: AI, Software, and the Next Technical Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15350)
*Ina K. Schieferdecker*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 智能体式AI使数字化进入"递归"阶段：数字系统开始自我开发与演化，软件工程（SE）因此从支撑性工程学科转变为社会价值创造与数字主权的核心基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化此前的阶段主要聚焦于将模拟信息数字化、流程自动化以及构建数字价值网络。而智能体式AI的出现，使数字化开始反作用于其自身的技术生产基础——即在软件工程领域，AI系统能够自主规划、执行、检查并迭代优化开发任务。这一变化引发了对人机分工以及SE学科整体角色的重新审视，作者希望分析这一社会技术变迁。

Method: 本文采用概念性/理论分析的方法，以"递归数字化"为核心概念框架，梳理数字化发展的阶段性演进，并基于智能体式AI在软件工程中已具备的多步自主开发能力，分析其对SE领域中人与机器分工、以及SE学科定位带来的社会技术层面变化。

Result: 数字化因此呈现递归特征：数字系统不再仅服务于其他行业的数字化，而是日益由数字系统自身进行开发与演化，数字化自身的技术生产基础成为其自我变革的对象。这既改变了软件工程中人与机器之间的分工，也改变了软件工程作为一个整体的角色。

Conclusion: 软件工程正从一门主要为其他领域提供支撑的工程学科，演变为社会价值创造以及技术与数字主权的核心基础设施。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) marks a new phase of digitalization: digitalization is beginning to act back upon its own technical production base. Whereas earlier phases aimed at digitizing analog information, automating processes, and building digital value networks, AI is increasingly taking over tasks in the development of digital systems themselves. In software engineering (SE) in particular, agentic AI systems can already plan, execute, check, and iteratively refine development tasks across multiple steps. Digitalization thus becomes recursive: digital systems no longer merely support the digitalization of other sectors, but are increasingly developed and evolved by digital systems themselves. The technical production base of digitalization thereby becomes the object of its own transformation. This changes not only the division of labor between humans and machines in SE, but also the role of SE as a whole. This chapter analyzes this socio-technical change and argues that SE is evolving from a primarily supporting engineering discipline into a central infrastructure of societal value creation as well as of technological and digital sovereignty.

</details>


### [37] [Translator vs. Challenger: Adversarial Agentic Learning for C-to-Rust Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15381)
*Chaofan Wang,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Chao Hu,Beijun Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出TRAIL对抗式智能体学习框架，通过Translator与Challenger双智能体协作，将C-to-Rust翻译中的经验提炼为鲁棒可复用的翻译知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C到Rust翻译存在巨大语义鸿沟。现有经验增强的LLM翻译器虽能从历史失败与修复中学习洞察，但这些洞察源于稀疏、特定程序的痕迹，常含缺失条件、狭窄适用边界或忽略的边界情况，难以构成可复用知识，鲁棒性与泛化性受限。

Method: TRAIL采用两个协作智能体：Translator从翻译失败与被接受的修复中提炼候选洞察，Challenger通过对抗性挑战主动搜索弱点、缺口与边界情况。框架在两个层面进行对抗学习：个体洞察对抗学习反复压力测试单个洞察以细化适用条件与约束；组合洞察对抗学习通过暴露冲突、缺口与未覆盖的边角案例来强化相关洞察组。通过可执行反例挑战洞察及其组合，将特定痕迹经验转化为鲁棒、可复用、可泛化的翻译知识。

Result: 在CRUST-Bench与SmartC2Rust-Bench两个项目级基准上，相比最强LLM基线，TRAIL在语法准确率上平均相对提升23.1%，语义准确率提升15.9%。对抗式精炼后的洞察可有效跨基准迁移，展现对多样C-to-Rust翻译任务的强泛化性。

Conclusion: TRAIL通过对抗式智能体学习框架，将稀疏的程序特定经验转化为鲁棒、可复用、可泛化的翻译知识，显著提升C-to-Rust翻译质量并具备良好跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: C-to-Rust translation remains challenging due to the substantial semantic gap between the two languages. Recent experience-enhanced LLM translators improve translation quality by learning reusable insights from prior failures and repairs. Yet learned insights do not automatically constitute reusable translation knowledge: derived from sparse, program-specific traces, they often contain missing conditions, narrow applicability boundaries, or overlooked corner cases. This limits their robustness and generalizability in new translation scenarios. We present TRAIL, an adversarial agentic learning framework for robust C-to-Rust translation. TRAIL employs two collaborating agents: a Translator that derives candidate insights from translation failures and accepted repairs, and a Challenger that actively searches for weaknesses, gaps, and boundary cases through adversarial challenges. To improve the robustness of individual insights and the completeness of insight collections, TRAIL performs adversarial learning at two levels. Individual-insight adversarial learning repeatedly stress-tests each insight to refine its applicability conditions and constraints, while compositional insight adversarial learning strengthens groups of related insights by exposing conflicts, gaps, and uncovered corner cases. By challenging insights and their compositions with executable counterexamples, TRAIL transforms trace-specific experience into robust, reusable, and generalizable translation knowledge. We evaluate TRAIL on two project-level benchmarks, CRUST-Bench and SmartC2Rust-Bench. Compared with the strongest LLM-based baseline, TRAIL achieves average relative improvements of 23.1% in syntax accuracy and 15.9% in semantic accuracy. Furthermore, the adversarially refined insights transfer effectively across benchmarks, demonstrating strong generalizability across diverse C-to-Rust translation tasks.

</details>


### [38] [IWC-Bench: Evaluating Web Application Generation from a Software Testing Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15387)
*Chenxu Liu,Zilu Zou,Peizhong Gao,Jiawen Tao,Zhexin Zhang,Guang Chen,Haowei Lin,Ying Zhou,Tianyi Bai,Dolly Deng,Suncong Zheng,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 IWC-Bench，一个从软件测试视角评估 LLM 生成 Web 应用的交互式基准，利用代码覆盖率引导智能体仿真用户探索功能，并以状态转移图评估视觉美感、可用性与需求对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工评估虽能直接衡量 LLM 生成 Web 应用的质量，但难以自动化。静态基准可能认可源码中存在却运行不可达的功能；交互式基准则因探索不完整而漏检已实现功能，并将应用缺陷与智能体执行失败相混淆。

Method: IWC-Bench 对每个生成应用进行插桩，用代码覆盖率引导智能体通过模拟用户交互探索功能；将交互轨迹抽象为状态转移图，从视觉美感、可用性、需求对齐三个维度评估。通过将探索与评分分离，可在不将探索限制于预设验收标准的情况下收集运行时证据。基准包含 369 条真实需求与 5,088 条验收标准。

Result: 对 16 个前沿 LLM 的评估显示，各模型在三个维度上各有优势，无模型全面领先。在内部竞技场抽样的 197 个已验证会话中，IWC-Bench 与人类偏好的一致率达 85.3%，且分数差距越大一致性通常越高。进一步实验表明，覆盖率引导提升了探索覆盖率，替换评测模型后模型排序保持稳定。

Conclusion: IWC-Bench 通过覆盖率引导的交互式探索与多维度评分，有效缓解静态与交互式基准的局限，并在与人类偏好一致性和排序稳定性上表现良好，为 Web 应用生成提供可靠的自动化评估方案。

Abstract: Human evaluation provides a direct measure of the quality of LLM-generated web applications. However, fitting human judgments through automated evaluation remains challenging. Static benchmarks can credit functionality that exists in source code but is unreachable at runtime. Interactive benchmarks exercise the application, yet incomplete exploration can cause them to miss implemented functionality and confound application defects with agent execution failures. To address these limitations, we propose IWC-Bench, an interactive benchmark for evaluating web application generation from a software testing perspective. IWC-Bench instruments each generated application and uses code coverage to guide an agent in exploring its functionality through user-simulated interactions. It then abstracts the interaction trace into a state-transition graph and evaluates the application along three dimensions: visual aesthetics, usability, and requirement alignment. By separating exploration from scoring, IWC-Bench collects runtime evidence without constraining exploration to predefined acceptance criteria. IWC-Bench comprises 369 real-world user requirements and 5,088 acceptance criteria. Evaluation of 16 frontier LLMs reveals distinct strengths across the three dimensions, with no model leading on every dimension. On 197 validated sessions sampled from an internal arena, IWC-Bench achieves 85.3\% agreement with human preferences, with agreement generally increasing as the score difference between paired applications grows. Further experiments show that coverage guidance improves exploration coverage and the model rankings remain stable when the judge model is replaced.

</details>


### [39] [The Future of Safety for SaMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15438)
*Rhea Malhotra,Tanya Sharma,Krisha Patel,Satvika Sharma,Heena Purkait,Mehak Nehal Makhija,Aellison Cassimiro,Everett Hildenbrandt,Palina Tolmach,Jaidev Shastri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何将形式化验证逐阶段应用于人工器官软件，以弥补传统测试无法覆盖未预见失效模式的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工器官的失效后果堪比飞机或反应堆，但其驱动软件却很少被要求达到同等标准。团队依赖测试，而测试只能覆盖事先想到的失效模式；对于在患者体内运行数月的水泵或控制器而言，真正危险的是无人预料到的情况。

Method: 将形式化验证应用于设备软件：证明代码在规范允许的所有执行路径下均满足规范，并在证明失败时返回导致破坏的确切输入序列；沿用并扩展制造商已遵循的 IEC 62304 生命周期，而非取而代之。

Result: 形式化验证方法已在轨道交通、航空和核控制系统中得到保护性应用，并可与 IEC 62304 生命周期相衔接。

Conclusion: 本文逐阶段探索形式化验证在人工器官上的应用方式，并阐明每种技术对设备实际能保证什么。

Abstract: An artificial organ carries failure consequences on the scale of an aircraft or a reactor, but the software driving it is rarely held to the same standard. Teams building them rely on testing, which only reaches the failure modes someone thought of in advance. In a pump or controller that runs inside a patient for months, the dangerous cases are the ones nobody anticipated. Formal verification closes that gap. Applied to the device's software, it proves the code meets its specification for every execution that specification allows, and where a proof fails, it returns the exact input sequence that breaks it. The same methods already protect rail, aviation, and nuclear control systems, and they extend the IEC 62304 lifecycle that a manufacturer already follows rather than replacing it. In this paper, we explore how to apply formal verification to artificial organs, stage by stage, and what each technique actually guarantees about the device.

</details>


### [40] [Technostress in the Age of AI: A Preliminary Study with Software Professionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15686)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Zixuan Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过调查121名来自26个国家的软件从业者，探讨AI相关的技术压力（technostress）。研究发现，AI技术压力不仅源于适应快速变化的技术，还源于需管理工作、技术职责及采用AI带来的职业变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI的快速采用与演进正在改变软件工程工作，要求从业者反复调整其知识、实践与技能。尽管技术适应一直是软件开发的长期特征，但对于这些新的、反复出现的压力如何表现为技术压力，人们了解仍较少。

Method: 开展了一项初步探索性研究：对来自26个国家的121名软件从业者进行问卷调查，并就其在工作中使用AI的近期经历进行主题分析。

Result: AI相关的技术压力不仅来自适应快速变化的技术，还来自必须管理与AI采用相伴的工作、技术职责和职业变化。

Conclusion: AI带来的压力超出学习与使用新工具的范畴，影响软件从业者如何执行并对技术工作保持责任，以及如何为其职业生涯的未来做准备。

Abstract: The rapid adoption and evolution of AI are changing software engineering work and requiring professionals to repeatedly adapt their knowledge, practices, and skills. Although technological adaptation has long characterized software development, less is known about how these new and recurring pressures manifest as technostress. This preliminary exploratory study investigates AI related technostress among software professionals. We conducted a survey and performed a thematic analysis of responses from 121 software professionals across 26 countries reporting their recent experiences with AI at work. Our findings suggest that AI related technostress emerges not only from adapting to rapidly changing technologies, but also from having to manage the work, technical responsibilities, and professional changes that accompany their adoption. This characterization shows that AI introduces pressures beyond learning and using new tools, affecting how software professionals perform and remain accountable for technical work and how they prepare for the future of their careers.

</details>


### [41] [ForgeTrain: Forging Production-Grade Training Frameworks via Harness-Driven AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13645)
*Qingfeng He,Zhui Zhu,Shangzhan Li,Yaojian Chen,Haojun Sun,Xu Chen,Leshan Li,Yifei Shen,Changjingxing Zhao,Mengyuan Fan,Wenyu Guan,Yiyun Zheng,Yuxuan Zuo,Zhen Li,Zhenghang Luo,Yuxuan Li,Xu Han,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"Forge Engineering"范式，为每个场景从零构建并迭代优化专用实现，实例化为训练框架ForgeTrain，MFU提升4.7–33.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用框架（如Megatron-LM）存在"通用性税"：抽象层积累带来运行时开销，且限制场景特定优化。AI代码生成降低了构建框架的成本，使得"每场景一个框架"变得可行。

Method: 从零为每个场景构建专用实现，在正确性与可用性约束下迭代优化至峰值性能。ForgeTrain以可信框架为黄金参考，将等价性要求从Bit-for-Bit单调放宽至Surpass，从而不受抽象边界限制、可跨栈整合优化。

Result: 在多种模型-硬件配置下，ForgeTrain均生成正确的训练引擎，MFU相比成熟训练框架提升4.7–33.2%。

Conclusion: 这是首个由AI端到端锻造、性能匹配或超越人类参考实现的生产级训练框架，验证了按场景定制专用框架的可行性。

Abstract: Training large models still relies on general-purpose frameworks such as Megatron-LM, whose generality tax constrains scenario-specific optimization and adds runtime overhead through accumulated abstraction. AI code generation reduces the cost of building a framework, and makes it affordable to forge one per scenario. We propose Forge Engineering: building a dedicated implementation from scratch for each scenario and iteratively optimizing it toward peak performance under correctness and usability constraints. Dedicated implementations inherit no abstraction boundaries, so they can integrate optimizations across the stack and reach a higher performance ceiling. We instantiate this paradigm for training frameworks as ForgeTrain, which holds a trusted framework as a golden reference and relaxes equivalence monotonically from Bit-for-Bit to Surpass. Experiments across multiple model--hardware configurations show that ForgeTrain consistently produces correct training engines and improves MFU over established training frameworks by 4.7--33.2%. To our knowledge this is the first production-grade training framework forged end-to-end by AI to match or surpass its human reference.

</details>

### [42] [Self-Indexing Attention for Compression-Compatible Sparse Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13205)
*Xu Yang,Jiapeng Zhang,Zhangke,Changjian Chen,Yuxin Chen,Feiqiang Sun,Chengguang Xu,Feng Jin,Zhuo Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Self-Indexing Attention，一种免训练框架，基于共享变换域符号-幅值表示，用1-bit符号索引同时支持prefill分组选择与decode检索，并兼容外部KV-cache压缩，在低注意力密度下接近稠密注意力并显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏长上下文推理需要在prefill和decode两个阶段高效检索token，但现有方法对两个阶段采用不同的检索策略，导致检索表示无法在整个推理过程中复用。

Method: 构建免训练框架Self-Indexing Attention，基于共享的变换域符号-幅值（sign-magnitude）表示；关键符号提供可复用的token级索引，用于分组prefill选择和decode检索，同一表示无需额外indexer元数据即可兼容外部KV-cache压缩，1-bit索引通过现代加速器广泛支持的位运算实现高效检索。

Result: 在5%注意力密度下，LongBench和RULER上接近稠密注意力，prefill注意力算子最高加速6.1倍，decode最高加速10.3倍；与TurboQuant及DeepSeekV4-Flash的实验进一步证明其兼容低比特KV-cache压缩和预训练稀疏注意力indexer。

Conclusion: 统一的共享检索表示可在prefill与decode阶段复用，实现高效稀疏长上下文推理，并具备对低比特KV-cache压缩与预训练稀疏注意力indexer的良好兼容性。

Abstract: Sparse long-context inference requires efficient token retrieval in both prefill and decode. Existing methods often use different retrieval strategies for the two stages, preventing one retrieval representation from being reused throughout inference. We propose Self-Indexing Attention, a training-free framework built on a shared transform-domain sign-magnitude representation. The key signs provide a reusable token-level index for grouped prefill selection and decode retrieval, while the same representation remains compatible with external KV-cache compression without separate indexer metadata. This 1-bit index enables efficient retrieval through bitwise operations widely supported by modern accelerators. At 5% attention density, Self-Indexing Attention remains close to dense attention on LongBench and RULER and achieves up to 6.1x prefill and 10.3x decode attention-operator speedups. Experiments with TurboQuant and DeepSeekV4-Flash further demonstrate compatibility with low-bit KV-cache compression and pretrained sparse-attention indexers.

</details>


### [43] [Decoupling Error Attribution in Cloud-Native Graph-RAG: A Data Integrity Diagnostic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13324)
*Shuai Yan,Yuhang Wu,Xiaodong Huang,Ke Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出三层解耦诊断框架，将Graph-RAG系统错误归因于推理损失、知识图谱缺陷和Cypher生成错误，发现数据完整性而非算法推理是主要性能瓶颈，并揭示"参数化知识掩蔽效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Graph-RAG系统通常假设数据质量完好，忽视了云原生数据库中数据扰动带来的严重影响，缺乏对系统错误来源的正交归因分析。

Method: 提出三层解耦诊断框架，将系统错误正交归因于推理损失、知识图谱（KG）缺陷和Cypher生成错误三类来源；在含八种缺陷类型的藏东南地区时空生态知识图谱上进行评估。

Result: 数据完整性而非算法推理是主导性能瓶颈，结构缺陷使系统准确率从0.93降至0.39；观察到类似掩蔽现象，命名为参数化知识掩蔽效应（PKME），大模型用内部记忆补偿断裂的检索路径，使表观查询生成错误减少超过70%，掩盖了真实的存储退化并增加自动监控的假阴性风险。

Conclusion: 该工作为云环境下信息融合系统的数据完整性审计与优化提供了定量基础。

Abstract: Graph-RAG systems often assume pristine data quality, overlooking the severe impact of perturbations in cloud-native databases. This paper proposes a three-layer decoupled diagnostic framework to orthogonally attribute system errors to reasoning loss, Knowledge Graph (KG) defects, and Cypher generation errors. Evaluated on a spatio-temporal ecological KG of the Southeastern Tibet region with eight defect types, results reveal that data integrity, rather than algorithmic reasoning, is the dominant performance bottleneck, with structural defects degrading system accuracy from 0.93 to 0.39. Crucially, we observe a masking-like phenomenon termed the Parametric Knowledge Masking Effect (PKME), suggesting LLMs compensate for broken retrieval paths using internal memory. This shrinks apparent query generation errors by over 70 percent, obscuring actual storage deterioration and increasing the risk of false negatives for automated monitoring. This work provides a quantitative foundation for auditing and optimizing data integrity in cloud-based information fusion systems.

</details>


### [44] [EviQE: Evidence Selection for LLM-Based Query Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14875)
*Hai Son Le,Amin Bigdeli,Shirin Seyedsalehi,Morteza Zihayat,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出EviQE框架，将基于LLM的查询扩展中"证据选择"与"扩展生成"分离，通过汇总多个改写器检索到的文档并精选紧凑证据集，再进行一次有据扩展，在TREC DL和BEIR上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的查询扩展研究多聚焦于"如何生成扩展"，而忽视了"模型应阅读哪些文档"这一问题；本文认为读取的证据质量才是关键，因此需要显式地选择高质量证据。

Method: EviQE汇总来自多个改写器（reformulator）检索到的文档，将其视为互补的检索视角；随后从中筛选出一个紧凑的证据集（其中基于相关性的LLM-Score选择效果最佳），并用该证据集执行一次有据的扩展生成步骤，从而将证据选择与生成解耦。

Result: 在三个TREC DL和五个BEIR基准上，不同改写器经常检索到不同的相关文档，汇总候选的相关文档覆盖率高于任何单一来源；基于相关性的证据选择（LLM-Score）始终优于直接改写、冷启动扩展和单源种子扩展；一旦选到强条件证据，额外的检索-生成轮次收益甚微甚至降低效果。

Conclusion: 证据选择而非扩展生成是LLM查询扩展的关键；将改写器视作互补检索视角并采用相关性驱动的紧凑证据选择，可稳定提升检索效果，而多轮迭代并无必要。

Abstract: LLM-based query expansion increasingly conditions reformulation on documents retrieved from the target corpus, yet most work focuses on how to generate expansions rather than which documents the model should read. We propose EviQE, which aggregates documents retrieved by multiple reformulators, selects a compact evidence set, and uses it for one grounded expansion step. This separates evidence selection from generation and treats reformulators as complementary retrieval perspectives. Across three TREC DL and five BEIR benchmarks, reformulators frequently retrieve distinct relevant documents, so pooled candidates provide higher relevant-document coverage than any individual source. The strongest gains come from relevance-based evidence selection: LLM-Score consistently outperforms direct reformulation, cold-start expansion, and single-source seeded expansion. Additional retrieval-generation rounds provide little benefit once strong conditioning evidence has been selected and can reduce effectiveness.

</details>


### [45] [Benchmarking Embedding Models for ESG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15434)
*Motaz Saad,Veronica Cretì,Ivan Gentile,Kianna Kazemi,Antonella Longo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建了面向ESG领域的基准数据集，评测了14个开源与闭源嵌入模型在检索和RAG任务上的表现，发现Qwen3系列模型整体性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESG数据对现代企业问责、可持续性报告和金融决策至关重要，嵌入模型是将非结构化ESG文本转化为可用于下游NLP任务的数值表示的强大方法，但其在ESG特定任务中的有效性尚未被系统研究。

Method: 构建了专门针对ESG领域的基准数据集，并对14个嵌入模型（涵盖开源与闭源）进行基准评测，比较它们在检索（retrieval）和检索增强生成（RAG）任务上的性能。

Result: 不同模型的性能存在差异，其中基于Qwen3的模型取得了最高的整体性能。

Conclusion: 本研究为ESG RAG任务中哪些嵌入模型更为适用提供了实践性见解。

Abstract: The use of Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) data is fundamental for modern corporate accountability, sustainability reporting, and financial decision-making. Embedding models have emerged as a powerful approach for transforming unstructured ESG text into numerical representations suitable for downstream natural language processing (NLP) tasks. However, their effectiveness in these ESG-specific tasks has not been systematically studied. In this paper, we construct a benchmark dataset specifically tailored to the ESG domain. We benchmark fourteen models, both open-source and closed-source embedding models, comparing their performance with respect to retrieval, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). The results demonstrate performance variations across different models, with Qwen3-based models achieving the highest overall performance. This study provides practical insights into which models are better suited for ESG RAG tasks.

</details>

*Diletta Micol Tobia,Hrishita Chakrabarti,Maria Soledad Pera,Monica Landoni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文关注儿童使用主要为成人设计的在线信息检索系统时所面临的困难，主张将儿童视为搜索过程的主动参与者，把搜索重构为一种有支架的学习体验，而非简单的检索任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童越来越多地使用主要为主流人群（成人）设计的在线信息获取系统，但他们对系统工作原理了解有限，导致搜索行为缺乏结构化且效率低下，进而阻碍其好奇心与批判性搜索技能的发展。

Method: 基于儿童信息检索（Child-oriented IR）与人机交互（HCI）领域的既有文献，从理论层面进行立场定位与研究框架构建。

Result: 摘要为立场/定位性内容，未报告具体实验或实证结果，主要提出研究视角与框架。

Conclusion: 研究主张将儿童定位为搜索过程中的主动参与者，把搜索视为一种带支架的学习体验，而非单纯的检索任务。

Abstract: Children increasingly turn to online information access systems that are primarily designed for the mainstream population, e.g., adults, but possess a limited understanding of how these systems work, contributing to their unstructured and ineffective search practices. This lack of knowledge can hinder their curiosity and the development of critical search skills. Grounded on the existing literature of both child-oriented Information Retrieval and Human Computer Interaction, our work positions children as active participants in the search process, framing it as a scaffolded learning experience rather than a simple retrieval task.

</details>


### [47] [The Magnitude Mirage: Rethinking Confidence for Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15578)
*Jamie Holdcroft,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了RAG系统中基于相似度分数幅值的检索弃权机制在需要推理的任务上系统性失效（Magnitude Mirage），并提出使用零成本的分数分布型QPP指标（如Score Gap和LSMV）替代幅值信号，可将弃权AUROC提升最多0.16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生产级RAG系统通过阈值化原始相似度分数来实现检索弃权，隐含地将分数幅值视为置信度信号。但当查询需要超越语义匹配的推理（如逻辑、时间推理）时，这一做法会系统性退化，因为神经检索器会给语义相关但违反约束的文档打高分，导致基于幅值的阈值在推理任务上退化为接近随机的弃权性能。

Method: 论文在11种检索架构和28个数据集上验证了幅值失效现象，并对六种零成本查询性能预测（QPP）指标在三个认知层级（语义匹配BEIR、逻辑推理BRIGHT、时间推理TEMPO）上进行了大规模实证研究，比较幅值信号与分数分布信号的效果。

Result: 关键改进来自放弃幅值信号、改用分数分布信号，该转变带来的增益比不同分布替代方案之间的差异大5-10倍。Score Gap（s1-sk）和实用的分数幅值与方差自适应方法（LSMV）在幅值置信度几乎无判别力的场景下，将弃权AUROC提升最多0.16。

Conclusion: 基于分数分布的QPP指标无需额外推理、重训练或延迟，是部署RAG系统中幅值阈值化的实用零成本替代方案。

Abstract: Many production RAG systems implement retrieval abstention by thresholding raw similarity scores, implicitly treating score magnitude as a confidence signal. We demonstrate that this practice degrades systematically as queries require reasoning beyond semantic matching. Across 11 retrieval architectures and 28 datasets, neural retrievers consistently assign high similarity scores to semantically related but constraint-violating documents, causing magnitude-based thresholds to collapse toward near-random abstention performance on logical and temporal reasoning tasks---a failure we term the Magnitude Mirage. To address this without computationally expensive alternatives, we conduct a large-scale empirical study of six zero-cost Query Performance Prediction (QPP) metrics across three cognitive tiers: semantic matching (BEIR), logical reasoning (BRIGHT), and temporal reasoning (TEMPO). Our central finding is that the key improvement comes from abandoning magnitude in favor of score-distribution signals: the gain from this shift exceeds the differences among distributional alternatives by a factor of 5-10$\times$. In particular, Score Gap ($s_1 - s_k$) and a practical adaptation of Score Magnitude and Variance (LSMV) improve abstention AUROC by up to 0.16 in settings where magnitude-based confidence provides little discriminative power. These methods require no additional inference, retraining, or latency, making them a practical zero-cost replacement for magnitude thresholding in deployed RAG systems.

</details>


### [48] [Self-Evolving Memory for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15598)
*Xinyu Lin,Zhuosong Jiang,Zixiao Suo,Siqin Wang,Hanqing Zeng,Hanchao Yu,Yinglong Xia,Jiang Zhang,Aashu Singh,Fei Liu,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Yang Song,Qifan Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文针对生成式推荐中的"演化冲突"问题，提出自演化记忆范式 LION，通过稀疏键值记忆层实现异构行为模式的隔离演化与强化学习，在多种持续演化设置下取得有效结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐是一种有前景的端到端个性化推荐范式，但用户偏好随时间不断演化，因此自演化能力至关重要。现有演化策略（如持续重训练、基于蒸馏的适配）直接使用流式交互更新共享模型参数，会引入"演化冲突"：不同用户的异构偏好变化在完全共享的自回归参数空间中被优化，导致主导行为模式逐渐主导模型演化，而代表性不足的模式被忽视。

Method: 作者提出面向生成式推荐的自演化记忆范式，并总结出三条关键原则：隔离记忆、强化演化、可扩展应用。据此提出 LION 框架，核心是一个稀疏键值（Key-Value）记忆层：利用稀疏记忆激活隔离不同行为模式的演化，并设计整合损失（consolidation loss）在持续适配中强化对代表性不足的偏好动态的学习。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，LION 在多种持续演化设置下（如按周期评估、用户/物品分组评估、演化收敛性分析）均有效。代码已开源。

Conclusion: LION 通过稀疏键值记忆层有效缓解了生成式推荐中的演化冲突，实现了跨异构行为模式的自演化推荐，验证了隔离记忆、强化演化与可扩展应用三条原则的有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation has emerged as a promising end-to-end paradigm for personalized recommendation. However, user preferences continuously evolve over time, making self-evolving an essential capability for generative recommender systems. Existing evolving strategies, such as continual retraining and distillation-based adaptation, directly update the shared model parameters using streaming interactions. Nevertheless, we find that directly applying such strategies to generative recommendation introduces a critical issue, termed evolution conflict. Specifically, heterogeneous preference shifts from different users are optimized within a fully shared autoregressive parameter space, causing dominant behavioral patterns to progressively dominate the model evolution process while underrepresented patterns become increasingly overlooked. To address this issue, we propose a self-evolving memory paradigm for generative recommendation, aiming to enable effective evolution across heterogeneous behavioral patterns. We further identify three key principles for effective self-evolving recommendation systems, including isolated memorization, reinforced evolution, and scalable application. Guided by these principles, we develop LION, a simple yet effective framework centered on a sparse Key-Value memory layer. Specifically, LION introduces sparse memory activation to isolate the evolution of different behavioral patterns, while a consolidation loss is designed to reinforce the learning of underrepresented preference dynamics during continual adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of LION under various continual evolution settings (e.g., per-period evaluation, user/item group evaluation, and evolution convergence analysis). The codes are released at https://github.com/JazyJiang/Self-Evolving-Memory-for-Generative-Recommendation.

</details>


### [49] [IROH: Insightful Ranking Of Humor using Multi-Stage Hybrid Retrieval with Rationale-Distilled LLM Judges for JOKER 2026 Track Task 1 English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15618)
*Ana-Maria Luisa Mocanu,Sebastian Mocanu,Ciprian-Octavian Truică,Elena-Simona Apostol*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VANGUARD团队提出IROH三阶段检索系统用于CLEF 2026 JOKER Task 1英文赛道，以0.6347 MAP位列榜首，其核心发现是理性蒸馏评判器主导排序质量，结构化硬负样本损害泛化，且更轻量、校准更好的模型可与更大模型匹敌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升幽默检索任务的排序质量，通过消融实验厘清各组件（理性生成、硬负样本、模型规模）对检索性能的实际贡献与反直觉现象。

Method: IROH采用三阶段流水线：混合稀疏-稠密检索、交叉编码器重排序、以及LoRA微调的大语言模型评判器集成；使用Gemma 4在通用与类型化两种提示策略下生成查询感知理性，并构建多达四种结构化硬负样本用于训练数据。

Result: 系统以0.6347 MAP获得排行榜第一名；消融覆盖三种交叉编码器架构、四种稠密嵌入器与八种评判器配置。发现理性蒸馏评判器是排序质量的主要驱动力，而将理性附加到一阶段索引贡献微乎其微；结构化硬负样本在几乎所有配置下降低泛化能力，尽管会抬高本地验证分数；通用理性Qwen2.5-7B评判器（0.6055 MAP）优于所有Gemma-4-31B配置，且通用理性相对类型化理性的优势几乎完全集中在较小模型上。

Conclusion: 在幽默检索中，理性蒸馏评判器最为关键，结构化硬负样本易造成过拟合式假象，且更轻量、校准更优的模型在多数消融组件中可与更大模型竞争甚至更优，提示模型规模并非性能的决定性因素。

Abstract: Our team, VANGUARD, presents IROH (Insightful Ranking of Humor), a three-stage retrieval system for JOKER Task 1 English at CLEF 2026, achieving first place on the leaderboard with 0.6347 MAP. Our pipeline combines hybrid sparse-dense retrieval, cross-encoder reranking, and a LoRA-adapted Large Language Model judge ensemble. We employ Gemma 4 to generate query-aware rationales under two prompt strategies, generic and typed, and produce up to four types of structured hard negatives for training data construction. Through an ablation across three cross-encoder architectures, four dense embedders, and eight judge configurations, our key findings are threefold: (1) the rationale-distilled judge is the primary driver of ranking quality, whereas appending rationales to the first-stage index contributes negligibly; (2) structured hard negatives degrade generalisation in nearly all configurations despite inflating local validation scores; and (3) across the components we ablate, the lighter, better-calibrated model is competitive with or stronger than its larger counterpart, with the generic-rationale Qwen2.5-7B judge (0.6055 MAP) outperforming every Gemma-4-31B configuration, and the advantage of generic over typed rationales is concentrated almost entirely in the smaller model.

</details>

### [50] [BudgetBench: A Budget-Tiered Protocol and Pilot Harness for Memory Strategy Evaluation in Local Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13149)
*Aditya Karnam Gururaj Rao,Arjun Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BudgetBench——一个把"每次调用输入 token 预算"当作自变量的评测协议与开源工具，用于在固定模型/任务/采样/解码条件下比较记忆策略，并首次把"预算违规率"作为一等结果指标；用若干试点研究验证协议而非给出最终排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地大模型智能体的活跃上下文是稀缺资源，记忆容量、预填充时延、缓存增长与服务目标共同限制了每次调用的输入 token 数。现有评测多在单一预算下比较记忆策略，掩盖了预算合规失败、非单调质量曲线与真实工作点，因此需要一个可复用、以预算为自变量的测量面。

Method: BudgetBench 将每次调用的输入 token 预算设为自变量，固定模型、任务、采样器与解码，扫描 2K/4K/8K/16K/32K 预算，记录质量、预算利用率、时延以及预算违规率。核心是可复用的测量面：可替换的 MemoryStrategy 契约、显式预算强制、确定性或版本化评分器、提示审计元数据与可复现工件。用三组试点研究验证：本地 qwen2.5:1.5b（SWE-bench Verified 与 LongBench v2 各 89 项）、托管 Qwen3 30B-A3B 的 50 项 LongBench 复现（精确分词）、以及 500 项 LongMemEval oracle 研究（官方 GPT-4o 评分器）。

Result: harness 暴露出预算合规失败、非单调质量曲线以及单预算评测所掩盖的工作点。"预算化 vs 全上下文"方向仍未定论：本地切片接近零效应，托管复现的点估计偏向全上下文。作者透明报告：早期试点的分词器近似会低估部分实际服务模型提示的 token 数，故其违规行仅为分词近似诊断，不构成主张性结果；所有时延数据均为操作性诊断。

Conclusion: 可复用的贡献在于协议、harness 与失败报告纪律，而非最终排名；它为扩展固定预算下的记忆策略评测提供了可复现的测量基础。

Abstract: For local large language model agents, active context is a scarce resource: memory capacity, prefill latency, cache growth, and service objectives all constrain how many input tokens each call can afford. We present BudgetBench, an active-budget protocol and reference harness that treats the per-call input-token budget as the independent variable when comparing memory strategies. Holding the model, task, sampler, and decoding fixed, it sweeps budgets over 2K, 4K, 8K, 16K, and 32K tokens and records quality, budget utilization, latency, and, as a first-class outcome, budget-violation rates. The core contribution is this reusable measurement surface: a swappable MemoryStrategy contract, explicit budget enforcement, deterministic or versioned graders, prompt-audit metadata, and reproducibility artifacts, released at https://github.com/aviskaar/budgetbench.
  We substantiate the protocol with pilot studies rather than final rankings. Across a local qwen2.5:1.5b pilot (89 items each on SWE-bench Verified and LongBench v2), a hosted 50-item Qwen3 30B-A3B LongBench replication with exact tokenization, and a 500-item LongMemEval oracle study scored by the official GPT-4o evaluator, the harness exposes budget-compliance failures, non-monotonic quality curves, and operating points that single-budget evaluation hides. The budgeted-versus-full-context direction remains unresolved: the local slice is near-null and the hosted replication favors full context in point estimate. We report results transparently, including that the early pilot's tokenizer approximation undercounts some served-model prompts, so its violation rows are tokenizer-approximation diagnostics, not claim-bearing results; all timings are operational diagnostics. The reusable contribution is the protocol, harness, and failure-reporting discipline needed to scale fixed-budget memory-strategy evaluation.

</details>


### [51] [A derivative-fidelity failure mode in physics-informed neural networks: strengthened benchmark evidence from function-value training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13171)
*Koji Koyamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"导数保真度"识别为PINN的一种失效模式，通过一维基准测试发现：函数值拟合准确并不代表自动微分得到的二阶导数准确，尤其在高曲率边界附近误差显著增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN依赖自动微分施加微分方程残差，但现有评估多关注函数值拟合，忽视导数的准确性。函数值一致并不必然意味着导数准确，这一潜在失效模式需要被明确界定和检验。

Method: 以sin(x)和exp(x)为一维基准，仅用函数值训练多层感知机，再单独评估自动微分得到的二阶导数；并额外测试训练点密度、激活函数、端点密集评估以及L2与最大误差两种诊断指标。

Result: 实验显示函数逼近在视觉上准确时，二阶导数误差可能显著更大，尤其在高曲率边界区域附近表现突出。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一套区分"函数值精度"与"物理残差可靠性"的诊断协议，提示不应仅凭函数值拟合判断PINN的可靠性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) use automatic differentiation to impose differential-equation residuals, but good agreement in function values does not necessarily imply accurate derivatives. This paper formulates derivative fidelity as a failure mode of PINNs and tests it with one-dimensional benchmarks. Multilayer perceptrons are trained only on function values for sin(x) and exp(x), while second derivatives obtained by automatic differentiation are evaluated separately. The hypothesis is strengthened by additional tests over training-point density, activation functions, endpoint-dense evaluation, and both L2 and maximum-error diagnostics. The results show that visually accurate function approximation can coexist with substantially larger second-derivative errors, especially near high-curvature boundary regions. The experiment provides a diagnostic protocol for distinguishing value accuracy from physics-residual reliability.

</details>


### [52] [Land Art as a Big-Data Climate Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13182)
*Alev Cinbarci,Sean Kalaycioglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过 1984–2025 年 1,744 幅 Landsat/Sentinel-2 影像与气候水文面板数据，构建 14 维复杂度特征，发现影像复杂度可提前约三年预示大盐湖水位变化，将"艺术即温度计"修正为"艺术即水文状态先行指标"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robert Smithson 的《螺旋防波堤》随大盐湖水位涨落而淹没或出露，既往有小样本研究声称其影像复杂性与全球气温正相关，但缺乏大样本与统计稳健性检验。本文旨在用长时序、多源遥感与气候数据系统检验并修正这一"艺术即温度计"隐喻。

Method: 采集 1984–2025 年覆盖各年各月的 1,744 幅配准 Landsat 4-9 与 Sentinel-2 影像块；构建 14 维复杂度特征（香农熵、多尺度排列熵、分形维数、空隙度、灰度共生纹理、强度统计量及 ImageNet 预训练 ResNet50 特征）；与 NASA GISTEMP、USGS NWIS、Open-Meteo、全球碳预算的 42 年月度气候水文面板对比；采用 bootstrap、Spearman/Pearson 相关、主成分分析、滞后分析、STL 分解及控制月份与传感器的偏相关。

Result: 香农熵为弱代理，不支持既往正相关claim；粗尺度排列熵与平均强度强追踪湖面高程（Spearman 0.85–0.88，置信区间不含零）；ResNet50 嵌入第三主成分无监督地表现为"AI 气候轴"，与累积 CO2 相关 0.86、与湖面高程相关 -0.83；影像复杂度领先湖面阶段约 3 年（滞后 +3 时 Pearson r=0.58，95% CI 0.40–0.73）；STL 显示 1984–2015 上升、此后随湖面接近历史低点而急剧下降的非单调趋势；偏相关证实结果对季节与传感器稳健。

Conclusion: 研究表明《螺旋防波堤》影像复杂度可作为水文状态的先行指标，而非单纯的温度计；数据集、特征管线与分析代码作为公开基准发布。

Abstract: Robert Smithson's 1970 land artwork Spiral Jetty, located in the north arm of Utah's Great Salt Lake, has alternated between submergence and exposure during severe lake decline. We analyze 1,744 co-registered Landsat 4-9 and Sentinel-2 image chips spanning every year and calendar month from 1984 to 2025. A 14-feature complexity signature combines Shannon entropy, multiscale permutation entropy, fractal dimension, lacunarity, gray-level co-occurrence texture, intensity statistics, and ImageNet-pretrained ResNet50 features. These measurements are compared with a 42-year monthly climate and hydrology panel from NASA GISTEMP, USGS NWIS, Open-Meteo, and the Global Carbon Budget. Bootstrap analysis shows that Shannon entropy is a weak proxy and does not support an earlier small-sample claim of positive correlation with global temperature. By contrast, coarse-scale permutation entropy and mean intensity track lake elevation strongly, with Spearman correlations of 0.85 to 0.88 and 95 percent confidence intervals excluding zero. The third principal component of the ResNet50 embeddings emerges without supervision as an AI climate axis, correlating 0.86 with cumulative CO2 and -0.83 with lake elevation. Image complexity leads lake stage by about three years, with Pearson r = 0.58 at lag +3 and a 95 percent confidence interval of 0.40 to 0.73. STL decomposition reveals a non-monotonic trend that rises from 1984 to 2015 and declines sharply thereafter as the lake approaches record-low elevations. Partial correlations controlling for month and sensor confirm robustness to seasonal and sensor effects. These results refine the art-as-thermometer metaphor into an art-as-leading-indicator-of-hydrological-state interpretation. The dataset, feature pipeline, and analysis code are released as a public benchmark.

</details>


### [53] [LLMs or Naive Bayes? Old Gems or New Ways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13185)
*Mohammad Firas Sada,Dmitry Mishin,John Graham,Seungmin Kim,Mahidhar Tatineni,Frank Würthwein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过跨4个模型家族、27B至1T参数规模的零样本/少样本LLM与Complement Naive Bayes的文本分类基准测试，论证在有标注数据场景下NB仍是资源受限HPC环境的最优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的兴起引发研究计算领域的一个疑问：像Naive Bayes（NB）这样的经典方法是否应该被淘汰？本文旨在通过系统基准测试回答这一问题。

Method: 在多个文本分类任务上，对Complement Naive Bayes与零样本/少样本LLM（覆盖4个模型家族、规模从27B到1T参数的MoE，跨越37倍范围）进行基准对比；并测量GPU吞吐量、能耗，以及分析Batch size 1下的CPU推理性能，同时提供基于Kubernetes Helm operator的自动化模型选择方案。

Result: LLM仅在零数据场景占优（Amazon Polarity情感任务98.0% vs 88.2%），但该优势易受污染影响（低污染情感任务NB 81.7% vs LLM 73.0%）；有标注数据时（如AG News）NB达89.1%，与零样本27B LLM（89.0%）统计上无差异，优于397B前沿模型（84.8%）；微调DistilBERT达90.6%但吞吐远低于NB；小LLM批量推理比NB CPU推理慢40-486倍，每样本能耗高约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 决策分界线依赖任务：话题分类中NB约在N~10^4标注量时达到LLM水平，而零数据情感任务则始终有利于LLM。对使用标注数据进行文本分类的资源受限HPC从业者，NB仍是最优选择。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) prompt a recurring question in research computing: should classical methods like Naive Bayes (NB) be retired? We benchmark Complement Naive Bayes against zero-shot and few-shot LLMs spanning four model families and a 37x range in scale (27B to a 1T-parameter mixture-of-experts) across text classification tasks. LLMs dominate only in zero-data regimes (98.0% vs 88.2% on Amazon Polarity sentiment), and even that win is contamination-prone: on a low-contamination sentiment task NB beats the zero-shot LLM (81.7% vs 73.0%). However, once labeled data is available (e.g., AG News), NB reaches 89.1% accuracy, statistically indistinguishable from the zero-shot 27B LLM (89.0%) and better than the 397B frontier model (84.8%), at thousands of samples/sec on a commodity CPU. Fine-tuned DistilBERT reaches 90.6% but at far lower throughput than NB at batch size 1 (Table 2). Our measured GPU throughput analysis shows small-LLM batched inference is 40-486x slower than NB CPU inference (the multiplier depends strongly on the host CPU), exposing a structural gap bounded by memory bandwidth, with roughly two orders of magnitude lower energy per sample. For resource-constrained HPC practitioners performing text classification with labeled data, NB remains the optimal choice. We show the decision line is task-dependent (NB reaches LLM parity around $N \sim 10^4$ labels for topic classification, while zero-data sentiment favors the LLM at all N tested) and provide a Kubernetes Helm operator that automates model selection using configurable thresholds and verifiable Prometheus metrics.

</details>


### [54] [Early Prediction of Satellite Collision Probability Using a Hybrid TCN-Transformer Model for a CDM-Based Conjunction Analysis Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13191)
*Rabia Tüylek Tok,Burak Yağlıoğlu,Enes Dağ,Emre Onur Kahya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的卫星交会风险早期预测框架，通过估计同一接近事件中后续CDM更新时的碰撞概率(PoC)，结合UT敏感性分析、PCA特征筛选与TCN-Transformer混合模型，实现更早、更一致的低轨卫星交会风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星与轨道碎片数量快速增长，导致近距离接近事件频繁，加重了卫星运营方的操作负担。对于采用电推进的卫星，低推力机动能力使规避规划受到额外时间约束，问题尤为严峻。现有CDM虽提供相对状态、协方差、最小距离、TCA和PoC等信息，但轨道不确定性的非线性传播以及PoC对协方差演化的敏感性，使得序列CDM的解释十分困难，亟需更早、更可靠的交会风险预测手段。

Method: 提出基于学习的框架，用于估计同一接近事件在后续CDM更新中预期的PoC。首先采用无迹变换(UT)的传播与反向传播框架，评估碰撞风险度量对CDM参数的敏感性；随后对数值化CDM参数应用主成分分析(PCA)，识别与PoC变化最相关的特征。基于敏感性与PCA结果，筛选原始CDM参数并构造表征相对运动、交会几何与协方差不确定性的派生指标。利用构建的序列化增强交会数据集，训练混合时序卷积网络(TCN)-Transformer模型，学习交会风险的时间演化规律。

Result: 敏感性分析与PCA结果为所选原始CDM参数和派生指标的构造提供了依据；基于序列化增强交会数据集训练的TCN-Transformer混合模型能够学习交会风险的时间演化。该框架应用于TÜBİTAK UZAY接收与分析的CDM数据，显示出对低轨卫星交会进行更早、更一致运行风险评估的潜力。

Conclusion: 该学习型框架通过预测后续CDM的PoC，为低轨卫星交会风险提供了更早期且更一致的运行评估能力，在TÜBİTAK UZAY的实际CDM数据上验证了其应用潜力。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of operational satellites and orbital debris has increased the frequency of close approach events in low Earth orbit (LEO), creating a higher operational burden for satellite operators. This problem is especially critical for satellites using electric propulsion, where low-thrust maneuver capability imposes additional time constraints on collision avoidance planning. In current practice, Conjunction Data Messages (CDMs) provide relative state, covariance, miss distance, time of closest approach, and probability of collision (PoC) information for conjunction assessment. However, the nonlinear propagation of orbital uncertainties and the sensitivity of PoC to covariance evolution make the interpretation of sequential CDMs challenging. This study proposes a learning-based framework for early prediction of satellite conjunction risk by estimating the PoC expected in the subsequent CDM update of the same close approach event. In the proposed methodology, an Unscented Transform-based propagation and backpropagation framework is first used to evaluate the sensitivity of the collision risk metric to CDM parameters. In addition, Principal Component Analysis is applied to the numerical CDM parameters to identify the features most relevant to PoC variation. The results obtained from the sensitivity analysis and PCA are then used to justify the selected raw CDM parameters and to construct derived metrics representing relative motion, encounter geometry, and covariance-related uncertainty. Using the resulting sequential enriched conjunction dataset, a hybrid Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN)-Transformer model is trained to learn the temporal evolution of conjunction risk. The framework is applied to CDMs received and analyzed within TÜBİTAK UZAY, demonstrating its potential for earlier and more consistent operational risk evaluation for LEO satellite conjunctions.

</details>


### [55] [Evaluating LLM-Generated Rules for Heart Disease Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13192)
*Feisal Alaswad,Batoul Aljaddouh,Maher Alrahhal,Wafaa Al Nassan,Talal Bonn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了传统机器学习模型与LLM生成规则系统在心脏病预测中的表现，发现传统ML模型预测性能更优，但LLM规则具备更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在医疗人工智能中预测性能与可解释性之间的权衡，评估LLM生成规则系统能否作为传统机器学习模型的可解释替代方案。

Method: 使用UCI心脏病数据集，评估Logistic Regression、KNN、SVM、Naive Bayes、Decision Tree、Random Forest等分类器，并与GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.6生成的规则系统对比，采用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数进行评价。

Result: 传统机器学习模型整体优于LLM规则系统。Random Forest表现最佳，准确率90.2%、精确率0.829、召回率1.0、F1分数0.906；Naive Bayes次之为88.5%准确率和0.881的F1分数。LLM规则系统较弱，Claude Sonnet 4.6准确率80.3%（F1: 0.833），GPT-4o为70.5%（F1: 0.690）。

Conclusion: 传统机器学习在预测性能上占优，而LLM生成的IF-THEN规则提升了可解释性和透明度，体现了医疗AI中性能与可解释性的权衡。全部代码已公开于GitHub仓库。

Abstract: This study compares traditional machine learning models and Large Language Model (LLM)-generated rule-based systems for heart disease prediction using the UCI Heart Disease dataset. Several classifiers, including Logistic Regression, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), Support Vector Machine (SVM), Naive Bayes, Decision Tree, and Random Forest, were evaluated alongside rule-based systems generated using GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet 4.6. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score metrics. Experimental results show that traditional machine learning models consistently outperform LLM-generated rule-based systems in predictive performance. Random Forest achieved the best overall performance with 90.2% accuracy, a precision of 0.829, perfect recall of 1.0, and an F1-score of 0.906. Naive Bayes followed closely with 88.5% accuracy and an F1-score of 0.881. In contrast, the LLM-generated rule models achieved lower performance, with Claude Sonnet 4.6 reaching 80.3% accuracy (F1-score: 0.833) and GPT-4o obtaining 70.5% accuracy (F1-score: 0.690). Despite the performance gap, the LLM-generated rules provide interpretable IF-THEN diagnostic logic that enhances explainability and transparency in clinical decision-making. These findings highlight the trade-off between predictive performance and interpretability in medical artificial intelligence systems. The complete implementation of all experiments, including machine learning models and LLM-derived rule classifiers, is publicly available in the GitHub repository at https://github.com/FeisalAlaswad/LLM-Rule-ML-Heart-Disease-Prediction .

</details>


### [56] [Diagnosing Faults in Reinforcement Learning Simulators and World Models with Canonical Polynomial Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13194)
*Tesfay Zemuy Gebrekidan,Hadush Hailu Gebrerufael*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用精确多项式不变量（化为有理数域上的约化Gröbner基）检验"尊重物理结构的模型预测更好"这一前提，发现精确性对预测帮助甚微，但对诊断（定位被违反的约束与归因故障参数）极具价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量文献将物理结构嵌入学习动力学，前提是尊重物理的模型预测更准。作者要检验这一前提是否成立，并探索精确不变量真正有用的场景。

Method: 从轨迹中恢复精确多项式不变量并规范化为Q上的约化Gröbner基；用一致性正则化器、塑形势做预测实验；提出筛选（定位被违反的物理约束）与归因（恢复错误不变量并识别责任物理参数）两种诊断流程，引入法式收缩与商空间恢复技术；在15个注入故障、350对发布版本、11个RL环境上验证。

Result: Acrobot上精确性对预测几乎无益：一致性正则化降低代数残差但rollout保真度不变，100%质量误差系统恢复的塑形势与正确势加速学习效果相当。诊断方面：筛选在15个注入故障中全部定位且无误报，观测空间基线无法定位任何一个；归因在7个参数故障上全部恢复责任参数；配对差分检验检测所有故障，优势在于定位而非检测。参考生成器扰动1e-4仍保留14–15/15定位，说明筛选不要求精确性；而理想等式判定仅能区分1e-12扰动，说明代数比较需要精确性。应用于350对版本，未发现模拟器动力学改变的证据，反而揭示基准实现自身的性质。

Conclusion: 精确不变量对预测价值有限，但对诊断不可替代：筛选与归因能精确定位约束违反与故障参数，且筛选对扰动鲁棒。该诊断工具在版本对比中未发现动力学变化，转而暴露基准实现特性。

Abstract: A large literature builds physical structure into learned dynamics on the premise that models respecting the underlying physics predict better. We test that premise using exact polynomial invariants recovered from trajectories and canonicalised as reduced Gröbner bases over $\mathbb{Q}$. On Acrobot, exactness provides little benefit for prediction: a consistency regulariser reduces algebraic residual while leaving rollout fidelity essentially unchanged, and a shaping potential recovered from a system with a 100% mass error accelerates learning as effectively as the correct potential. Exact canonical invariants instead prove valuable for diagnosis. We develop two procedures: screening, which identifies the violated physical constraint, and attribution, which recovers the faulty invariant and identifies the responsible physical parameter. To enable this, we introduce normal-form deflation and quotient-space recovery. Across fifteen injected faults, screening localises every broken constraint with no false alarms, whereas observation-space baselines do not localise any; attribution recovers the responsible parameter on all seven parameter faults. Paired difference tests detect all faults, showing that the advantage is localisation rather than detection. Perturbing reference generators by $10^{-4}$ preserves 14--15/15 localisations, showing that screening does not require exactness, whereas ideal-equality decisions distinguish perturbations of only $10^{-12}$, showing that exactness is required for algebraic comparison. Applied to 350 release pairs across eleven RL environments, the diagnostic finds no evidence of changed simulator dynamics, instead revealing properties of the benchmark implementations themselves.

</details>


### [57] [Machine Unlearning for Speech Question Answering in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13195)
*Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大型音频-语言模型（LALMs）在语音问答（QA）中的机器遗忘问题，提出并评估了多种遗忘策略，在降低隐私泄露率的同时保持核心能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LALMs在大规模数据训练下具备强大语音理解与问答能力，但也继承了隐私风险，包括对敏感信息的非预期记忆。相比文本LLM或自动语音识别（ASR）的既有研究，语音问答场景更具挑战性，因为声学感知与事实知识紧密耦合。

Method: 提出并评估多种遗忘策略，包括梯度上升（gradient ascent）、任务算术（task arithmetic），以及基于对齐的微调方法（通过强制安全拒绝响应），以在移除隐私知识的同时保留核心能力。

Result: 在语音问答数据集上的大量实验表明，这些遗忘方法可将隐私泄露率最多降低80%，同时在非隐私语音问答和通用语音理解基准上保持接近中性的性能。

Conclusion: 多种遗忘策略能够有效缓解LALMs在语音问答中的隐私泄露问题，并较好维持模型的核心语音能力，为语音场景下的机器遗忘提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have recently shown strong capabilities in speech understanding and question answering (QA), but they also inherit privacy risks from large-scale training data, including the unintended memorization of sensitive information. In this work, we study machine unlearning for speech QA in LALMs, a setting that is more challenging than prior work on text-based Large Language Models (LLMs) or Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) due to the tight coupling between acoustic perception and factual knowledge. We present and evaluate multiple unlearning strategies, including gradient ascent, task arithmetic, and alignment-based fine-tuning methods that enforce safe refusal responses, to remove private knowledge while still preserving performance on core capabilities. Through extensive experiments on speech QA datasets, we show that these unlearning methods can reduce the privacy leakage rate by up to 80% while maintaining near-neutral performance on non-private speech QA and general speech understanding benchmarks.

</details>


### [58] [Algorithmic Information Dynamics of Learning: A Certified, Differentiable Complexity Controller for Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13197)
*Luan Ozelim,Hector Zenil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用可微的算法复杂度估计器 K^CDM_sF 将算法信息动力学引入学习动力学，通过瞬态损失扰动实现并加速 grokking，并系统分析其机制、边界与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法信息动力学（AID）依赖分块分解法估计算法复杂度，但该方法分段常数，只能做有限差分，无法进行真正的微分运算。作者希望用可认证、可微的估计器把"复杂度微积分"带入学习动力学，特别是 grokking 现象——其中复杂度序参量已知，却尚未被用于实际控制。

Method: 采用可认证可微估计器 K^CDM_sF；对模型施加瞬态损失扰动（kick），使估计器充当控制器，在 Levin 的描述长度—时间意义下加速 grokking；设定数据依赖的 Occam 边界并研究其有限尺度趋势 f_c ~ ln p / p；开展消融实验，比较复杂度门控与训练损失门控、检验认证先验与逐参数 ∇K 归因的可替换性；并进行直接场扰动以考察对 Occam 场的响应。

Result: 复杂度门控在挽救失败种子时与训练损失门控效果相当，但干预量少 27%；被测信号中只有映射复杂度能标记转变的完成；认证先验与 ∇K 归因均可替换（均匀先验传感器给出逐位相同的门控决策，随机支持在稀疏度阈值以上与 ∇K 选中支持相当）；直接场扰动显示对 Occam 场呈类成核响应，探测幅度范围内未分辨出线性区（故不支持涨落—耗散替代），有限场响应在接近转变时增大数个量级；该扰动可迁移到稀疏奇偶校验和 transformer，而持续的权重空间损失则失败。

Conclusion: 这些测量解释了经验调优的阶梯式策略：bang–bang 脉冲，在收益充足时触发、在停滞时释放，其迭代过程很可能构建了它所利用的响应。算法估计器的独特贡献在于时机（何时触发、何时释放），而非归因。

Abstract: Algorithmic Information Dynamics (AID) studies systems by perturbing them and measuring changes in algorithmic complexity, but its usual estimator, the Block Decomposition Method, is piecewise constant, restricting the calculus to finite differences. We use $K^{\mathrm{CDM}}_{\mathrm{s}F}$, a certified, differentiable estimator, to bring the calculus into learning dynamics: grokking, where a complexity order parameter is known but has not been made to act. A\empts a transient loss kick, the estimator becomes a controller that accelerates grokking in Levin's description-length--versus-time sense, within a data-dependent Occam boundary whose finite-size trend, $f_c\sim\ln p/p$, is consistent with a coupon-collector interpretation. Ablations show that a complexity gate matches a train-loss gate in rescuing failing seeds with $27\%$ less intervention; among the tested signals, only map complexity marks the transition's completion; the certified prior and the per-parameter $\nabla K$ attribution are both fungible (a uniform-prior sensor makes bit-identical gate decisions, and random supports match $\nabla K$-selected ones above a sparsity threshold); and direct field perturbation shows a nucleation-like response to the Occam field (no linear regime is resolved over the probed amplitudes, so these measurements do not justify a fluctuation--dissipation surrogate), with a finite-field response growing by orders of magnitude toward the transition. These measurements account for the empirically tuned staircase: bang--bang pulses, stall-fired and released on yield, whose iteration plausibly builds the response it exploits. The kick transfers to sparse parity and to a transformer; a sustained weight-space loss fails. The algorithmic estimator's distinct contribution is timing (when to fire and when to release), not attribution.

</details>


### [59] [SPICE: Simple Polysemantic Feature Interpretation via Clustering-based Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13198)
*Sehyun Lee,Dahee Kwon,Damin Lee,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPICE框架，通过聚类自动确定概念簇数量，实现跨CNN与Transformer的通用多义神经元分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可解释性中的多义性（polysemanticity）问题——单个神经元被多个不相关概念激活——阻碍了功能理解。现有方法存在两大局限：一是依赖特定架构的传播规则，二是依赖人工启发式（如固定簇数K），通用性和可扩展性不足，难以适用于现代Transformer模型。

Method: SPICE（Simple Polysemantic Feature Interpretation via Clustering-based Explanation）框架：避免架构相关的传播规则，采用基于聚类的解释方法，并自动为每个神经元确定概念簇的数量，无需预设K。

Result: 实现了首个在CNN与Transformer之间对多义性进行系统性对比的分析，并支持大规模模型的可扩展分析。

Conclusion: 利用SPICE对多义性的产生方式、随深度和架构的变化规律，以及其通过不同计算路径的形成机制进行了全面研究。

Abstract: One of the pivotal recent challenges in neural network interpretability is polysemanticity, where a single neuron is activated by multiple, often unrelated concepts, hindering clear functional understanding. Although prior work has explored this phenomenon, existing approaches remain architecture-specific and depend on manual heuristics such as a fixed number of concept clusters ($K$), limiting their generality and scalability--especially for modern Transformer-based models. To address these limitations, we introduce SPICE (\textbf{S}imple \textbf{P}olysemantic Feature \textbf{I}nterpretation via \textbf{C}lustering-based \textbf{E}xplanation), a generalizable framework for analyzing polysemanticity in deep vision architectures. SPICE avoids architecture-dependent propagation rules, enabling the first systematic comparison of polysemanticity across both CNNs and Transformers, and automatically determines the number of concept clusters per neuron, eliminating reliance on a preset $K$ and supporting scalable analysis for large models. Using SPICE, we conduct a comprehensive investigation into how polysemanticity emerges, varies across depth and architecture, and forms through distinct computational pathways.

</details>


### [60] [Discovering and Preserving Category Correlation Knowledge via Adaptive Reciprocal Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13199)
*Dawen Jiang,Zhishu Shen,Zeyu Liu,Tiehua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应互惠知识蒸馏（AR-KD），通过简化教师输出分布改善知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师与学生模型间存在较大容量差距，现有方法多采用静态单向的师到生蒸馏范式，忽略学生学习动态性，且难以为困难样本提供针对性指导。

Method: AR-KD对教师进行互惠适应：将教师的类相关矩阵与学生关系表示对齐，重塑教师预测结构以更契合学生能力，从而缓解因教师过度自信导致的类间暗知识坍塌，使学生获得更丰富且兼容的监督信号。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1k分类数据集上优于现有最优蒸馏基线；学生精度最高提升7.13%，平均比vanilla KD高1.42%至4.15%，与其他先进方法结合后还能进一步提升。

Conclusion: AR-KD通过师生互惠关系对齐有效缩小容量差距，提供兼容的监督信号，显著提升同构与异构蒸馏场景下的学生性能。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation aims to improve the performance of lightweight student models by transferring knowledge from larger and more powerful teacher models. However, a substantial size gap between teacher and student models often impedes effective knowledge transfer.
