Most existing approaches adopt a static, one-way teacher-to-student distillation paradigm, which overlooks the dynamic nature of student learning and fails to provide targeted guidance on hard samples. In this paper, we propose adaptive reciprocal knowledge distillation (AR-KD), a novel method that improves knowledge transfer by simplifying the teacher's output distribution. Specifically, AR-KD performs reciprocal adaptation on the teacher by matching its class correlation matrix to the student's relational representation, which reshapes the teacher's prediction structure to better suit the student's capacity. This relational alignment mitigates the collapse of inter-class dark knowledge caused by overconfident teachers, enabling the student to learn from richer and more compatible supervisory signals. We evaluate AR-KD on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1k classification datasets, where it outperforms state-of-the-art knowledge distillation baselines. Specifically, AR-KD improves student performance across homogeneous and heterogeneous setups: up to 7.13% accuracy gain for students, 1.42% to 4.15% higher than vanilla KD on average, and further improvements when integrated with other advanced methods. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ARKD/.

</details>


### [61] [Scalable partial information decomposition for symptom networks via supervised embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13203)
*Cillian Hourican,Eric Dignum,Rick Quax,Debraj Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出embedding-based PID (ePID)，通过将非焦点症状压缩为低基数离散嵌入，实现可扩展的双源部分信息分解，从而在症状网络中区分冗余与协同信息，并发现PHQ-9与人际反应指针量表呈现相反的依赖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统症状网络以标量边权重概括成对关系，无法表达两个症状是否携带关于第三症状的重叠信息，或仅在组合中才出现的信息；而部分信息分解(PID)虽能弥补这一缺陷，却在多于几个源时计算不可行。

Method: 提出ePID流程：将所有非焦点症状压缩为低基数离散嵌入，再计算可处理的双源PID，为每个有序源-目标对输出源独有、剩余独有、冗余和协同分量。在基于PHQ-9校准的合成贝叶斯网络和83个真实数据集上，用5种PID度量对13种候选嵌入进行了基准测试。

Result: 监督式凝聚条件信息瓶颈(ACIB)嵌入在13种嵌入中恢复参考分解最准确，且在压缩4个及以上症状后对所有PID度量均产生非负原子(synergy recovery r = 0.92)。PHQ-9网络(UK Biobank, N=154,291; 新乡学生样本, N=24,292)中周围症状背景通过冗余和剩余独有通道承载了大部分成对依赖，协同贡献6%至9%，且两个队列均无有向边以协同为主；而在28项人际反应指针量表中，45%的源对以协同为主。

Conclusion: ePID通过区分重叠信息与交互依赖信息，提供了可扩展、模型无关的方法，可作为标准症状网络方法学的补充，用于辨别观测相关中的冗余与协同贡献。相同流程无需改动参数即对两种量表返回相反的结构特征，且各自与该量表的构建方式一致。

Abstract: Pairwise relationships among mental-health symptoms are routinely summarised asscalar edge weights, which cannot express whether two symptoms carry overlapping information about a third or information that appears only in combination. Partial information decomposition (PID) addresses this gap but is computationally intractable beyond a few sources.
  We introduce embedding-based PID (ePID), a scalable pipeline that compresses all nonfocal symptoms into a low-cardinality discrete embedding and computes a tractable two-source PID, yielding source-unique, remainder-unique, redundant, and synergistic components for each ordered source-target pair. We benchmarked 13 candidate embeddings on synthetic Bayesian networks calibrated to PHQ-9 and on 83 real-world datasets across five PID measures.
  A supervised Agglomerative Conditional Information Bottleneck (ACIB) embedding recovered the reference decomposition most accurately of the 13 embeddings tested, and did so for every PID measure yielding non-negative atoms once four or more symptoms were compressed (synergy recovery r = 0.92). The two instruments then diverged sharply. In PHQ-9 networks (UK Biobank,N = 154,291; Xinxiang student sample, N = 24,292) the surrounding symptom context carried most pairwise dependence through redundant and remainder-unique channels; synergy contributed 6 to 9%, and no directed edge was synergy-dominated in either cohort. In the 28-item Interpersonal Reactivity Index, 45% of source pairs were. The identical pipeline, applied without parameter changes, therefore returned opposite profiles for the two instruments, each consistent with how that instrument was constructed.
  By separating overlapping from interaction-dependent information, ePID provides a scalable, model-agnostic complement to standard symptom-network methodology to distinguish redundant and synergistic contributions to observed correlations.

</details>


### [62] [ReCAST: Reward Credit Assignment across Timesteps for Online Diffusion Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13425)
*Yihang Chen,Yuanhao Ban,Kuei-Chun Kao,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCAST提出首个在扩散模型多奖励微调中实现逐奖励、逐时间步信用分配的方法，通过奖励×时间步权重矩阵分离用户偏好与时间分配，按各奖励在去噪各步的信息量分配权重，在SD3.5-Medium上验证了泛化性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多奖励扩散模型微调需区分两个问题：用户偏好（决定各奖励对总目标的贡献大小）与奖励信息量（决定其反馈在去噪何时有用）。有的奖励在全局结构刚出现时即可评估样本，有的需接近干净时才有效。现有方法未同时解决这两者的联合分配。

Method: 提出ReCAST，通过奖励×时间步权重矩阵W分离偏好与时间分配：行和等于用户指定奖励预算λ，列和相等（每个去噪步总权重相同）。在此边际约束下，依据各奖励在各步的Rényi判别性增益来分配权重，这些增益可望远镜式累加为奖励诱导正策略与当前策略间的总判别性。

Result: 在SD3.5-Medium上以两种不同四奖励设置、各五种奖励预算λ进行训练：ReCAST在一组设置中提升训练奖励、另一组持平，两种设置下所有留出评判器指标均提升，并被独立LLM-as-a-Judge偏好。

Conclusion: ReCAST产生的改进可泛化到训练奖励之外，支持其核心原则：在去噪时间步中，对反馈最具信息量的步骤赋予该奖励更大权重。

Abstract: Training diffusion models with multiple rewards requires distinguishing user preference from reward informativeness. User preference determines how much each reward should contribute to the overall objective; reward informativeness determines when its feedback is useful during denoising. Some rewards can meaningfully evaluate a sample as soon as global structure emerges, but others become informative only when the sample is nearly clean. To address both questions jointly, we propose ReCAST (Reward Credit ASsignment across T}imesteps), the first method, to our knowledge, for per-reward, timestep-dependent credit assignment in diffusion reward fine-tuning. ReCAST separates user preferences from temporal allocation through a reward-by-timestep weight matrix $W$, whose row sums match the user-specified reward budgets $λ$, while its column sums are equal, assigning the same total weight to each denoising step. Under these marginal constraints, ReCAST allocates weight according to each reward's informativeness, quantified by its Rényi discriminability gain at each step. These gains telescope to the total discriminability between the reward-induced positive policy and the current policy, providing a basis for temporal credit assignment. We evaluate ReCAST by training SD3.5-Medium under two distinct four-reward settings, each across five reward budgets $λ$. ReCAST improves the training rewards in one setting and matches them in the other, improves every held-out judge in both, and is preferred by an independent LLM-as-a-Judge. Together, these results show that ReCAST yields improvements that generalize beyond the training rewards and support its core principle: assigning each reward greater weight at the denoising timesteps where its feedback is most informative.

</details>


### [63] [Certifiably Interpretable Training of ReLU-MLPs for Boolean Tasks with Guaranteed Truth-Table Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13439)
*Hrad Ghoukasian,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MACCHIATO训练算法，联合构造结构化ReLU-MLP与显式布尔电路，为神经网络的可解释性提供证书，并给出统计泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着算力、模型和训练算法的进步，AI系统能力增强但可解释性却在退化，为保障可解释性需要新的训练算法。

Method: MACCHIATO迭代地将布尔函数残差投影到低维{AND,OR,XOR}电路类上，并精确编译为ReLU-MLP，结合ReLU-MLP电路编译、ESPRESSO逻辑最小化和基于影响的变量选择。

Result: 在影响恢复条件下，若m个阶段残差各依赖至多log2(B)位，样本分裂变体用T个观测返回六层、宽度O(mB)的ReLU-MLP，真值表误差为O(√(m(B+log(m/δ))/T))。合成随机junta任务上优于同深度宽度Adam-MLP（数据稀疏或投影对齐情形），PyEDA实现中迭代过程可完成而平面ESPRESSO超过三小时预算。

Conclusion: MACCHIATO通过显式电路与结构化MLP的联合构造，提供可解释性证书与统计保证，并在数据稀疏场景和计算效率上展现实用优势。

Abstract: As compute scales, models evolve, and training algorithms advance, our ability to explain the increasingly powerful AI systems they enable is eroding. To help safeguard interpretability, we introduce a specialized training algorithm (MACCHIATO) that jointly constructs (i) an explicitly structured $\operatorname{ReLU}$-MLP from partial truth-table observations and (ii) an explicit Boolean circuit over signed literals with $\{\operatorname{AND},\operatorname{OR},\operatorname{XOR}\}$ gates certifying what its subnetworks compute and how they compose. Intuitively, we iteratively project the residuals of a Boolean function onto low-dimensional $\{\operatorname{AND},\operatorname{OR},\operatorname{XOR}\}$-circuit classes and exactly compile the resulting circuit into a $\operatorname{ReLU}$-MLP; we combine $\operatorname{ReLU}$-MLP circuit compilation, ESPRESSO logic minimization, and influence-based variable selection.
  Roughly speaking, our interpretability certificate is complemented by a statistical guarantee: under the theorem's influence-recovery conditions, if each of the $m$ stage-wise residuals depends on at most $\log_2(B)$ bits, a sample-splitting variant of our algorithm trained on $T$ observations returns a six-layer $\operatorname{ReLU}$-MLP (counting the input layer) of width $\mathcal{O}(mB)$ with truth-table error $\mathcal{O}\bigl(\sqrt{m(B+\log(m/δ))/T}\bigr)$.
  On synthetic random-junta tasks, our networks outperform depth- and hidden-width-matched Adam-trained MLPs in several data-sparse or projection-aligned regimes, while the trained ReLU-MLPs are stronger in others. Moreover, in our explicit PyEDA truth-table implementation, the iterative procedure completes in regimes where flat ambient-dimensional ESPRESSO exceeds the three-hour computational budget.

</details>


### [64] [Efficient Online Inverse Optimization with $O(d)$ Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13440)
*Yang Cai,Anupam Gupta,Vineet Gupta,Guru Guruganesh,Yanchen Jiang,Christopher Liaw,Aranyak Mehta,Renato Paes Leme,Grigoris Velegkas,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性在线逆线性优化算法，达到$O(d)$的遗憾界，每轮仅需$O(d^2)$时间，是首个多项式时间且proper的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Dewasurendra虽获得了$O(d)$的遗憾界，但其规则是improper的，每轮需枚举各尺度覆盖、耗时$T^{Θ(d)}$，效率极低。因此需要一种既高效又proper的算法。

Method: 基于Sakaue等人的变度量（variable-metric）框架，引入自归一化秩一更新（self-normalized rank-one update），并用迹幂$\tr(H^{-1/2})$替代$\log\det$势函数。

Result: 算法获得$O(d)$遗憾界，对时间域统一；每轮$O(d^2)$时间；$\tr(H^{-1/2})$有界，消除了$\ln T$项；界同样对非优化专家成立；并给出抗腐坏与秩自适应变体，以及凸最小化应用。

Conclusion: 该算法首次在保持$O(d)$遗憾界的同时实现高效（多项式时间）与proper，是对先前工作的显著改进，并具有多个扩展应用。

Abstract: We give a deterministic algorithm for online inverse linear optimization with regret $O(d)$, uniform in the horizon and $O(d^{2})$ time per round. A bound of this order was obtained recently by Dewasurendra, settling a question of Gollapudi et al.\ and of Oki and Sakaue, but by an improper rule that enumerates covers at every scale and costs $T^{Θ(d)}$ a round; ours is the first efficient such bound and the first proper one. We build on the variable-metric framework of Sakaue et al., adding a self-normalized rank-one update, and we replace the $\log\det$ potential by the trace power $\tr(H^{-1/2})$, which is bounded outright and removes the $\ln T$. The bound also holds against an expert that does not optimize, and we give corruption-robust and rank-adaptive variants, and an application to convex minimization.

</details>


### [65] [Learning to Solve Hard Problems in RL for LLMs by Never Giving Up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13443)
*Michael Noukhovitch,Hamish Ivison,Nathan Lambert,Aaron Courville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示LLM强化学习中的"马太效应"，并提出自适应采样方法NGU，通过异步RL将计算资源从简单问题动态转移到困难问题，提升单位计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练LLM时，性能提升在数据集上分布不均：模型本已擅长的简单问题提升大，困难问题提升小，形成"富者愈富"的累积优势现象（马太效应）。朴素解释认为困难问题需要更多计算来求解，但作者认为现代RL方法将过多计算浪费在简单问题上，加剧了这一问题，因此需要动态重分配计算资源。

Method: 提出Never Give Up (NGU)自适应采样方法：对一个问题持续生成样本，直到至少有一个正确为止。借助异步RL，该方法天然地减少用于过滤简单问题的样本量，并将更多计算分配给困难问题。作者还研究了影响NGU的设计选择（如off-policy鲁棒性），并总结出一套最佳实践。

Result: 在数学基准Deepscaler上，NGU提升了单位计算的性能，尤其在困难问题上改善明显。在近期编码任务Manufactoria上，标准GRPO配合逐测试奖励无法完全解决包含易难不同测试的问题；而NGU迭代改进，逐步解决越来越难的测试，最终学会完整解决编码问题。

Conclusion: 现代RL方法应改变计算使用方式，从均匀采样转向自适应重分配。NGU以简单方式实现这一理念，缓解RL训练中的马太效应，在数学与编码任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We demonstrate that training LLMs with RL does not improve performance equally across a dataset. RL shows large improvements on easy problems that an LLM is already good at solving, but small improvements on hard problems. We call this the Matthew Effect in RL for LLMs, after the phenomenon of cumulative advantage from economics and network science summarized as "the rich get richer". The naive explanation is that hard problems require more compute to find a solution. We argue that modern RL methods are exacerbating the issue by wasting too much compute on easy problems and instead should dynamically reallocate how they use compute. We introduce Never Give Up (NGU), a simple adaptive sampling method that keeps generating samples for a problem until one is correct. By leveraging asynchronous RL, this naturally uses fewer samples to filter out easy problems and allocates more compute to solving harder problems. We investigate the design choices that affect NGU, such as off-policy robustness, and develop a set of best practices. On the math benchmark Deepscaler, NGU improves performance per compute, especially on harder problems. On a recent coding task, Manufactoria, standard GRPO with a per-test reward fails to fully solve problems that have a range of easy and difficult tests. NGU iteratively improves, solving harder and harder tests, until it learns to fully solve coding problems.

</details>


### [66] [A Machine Learning API for Earth Observation Data Cubes Based on openEO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13453)
*Brian Pondi,Jonas Hurst,Rolf Simoes,Jonas Starke,Marius Appel,Edzer Pebesma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 openEO 提出一套过程级机器学习规范，涵盖模型初始化、模型动作与模型管理三阶段，支持经典算法与深度学习模型，并通过 R/Python 原型与作物类型制图等用例验证跨后端互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测数据日益以时空数据立方体组织，而机器学习方法通常面向表格特征矩阵或结构化张量输入，二者不匹配导致需进行平台特定的转换，难以复现或跨云基础设施迁移。openEO 规范虽提供统一的数据访问与处理接口，但缺乏标准化的机器学习集成方案。

Method: 提出面向 openEO 的过程级机器学习规范，分为三阶段：模型初始化、模型动作（训练、调参、推理、验证）和模型管理。支持随机森林、SVM 等经典算法，以及 TempCNN、时间注意力编码器和基础模型推理等深度学习架构。开发了 R 和 Python 三个原型实现。

Result: 作物类型制图用例通过向独立的 R 和 Python 后端提交相同过程图，验证了跨后端互操作性，并比较了预测结果与评价指标；另两个用例分别在专用后端上演示了时间序列深度学习和基础模型推理。原型同时揭示，完整跨后端可移植性需要比过程级更深入的序列化格式与执行语义协调。

Conclusion: 后端的库版本与规范边界之外的预处理约定也会影响可复现性。通过显式后端一致性配置文件同时解决这两方面问题，是最重要的近期方向。该规范提升了地球观测数据立方体上机器学习工作流的可复现性、可移植性与可访问性。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) data are increasingly organized as spatio-temporal data cubes, while machine learning (ML) methods operate on tabular feature matrices or structured tensor inputs. This mismatch forces platform-specific transformations that are difficult to reproduce or transfer across cloud infrastructures. The openEO specification provides a unified interface for EO data access and processing across heterogeneous backends, but lacks a standardized approach for ML integration.
  We propose a process-level ML specification for openEO structured into three stages: model initialization, model actions (training, tuning, inference, validation), and model management. It supports classical algorithms such as Random Forest and SVM, as well as deep learning architectures for time-series and spatial patch-based modeling, including TempCNN, Temporal Attention Encoders, and foundation model inference. Three prototype implementations in R and Python demonstrate feasibility across diverse technology stacks. A crop type mapping use case demonstrates cross-backend interoperability by submitting an identical process graph to independent R and Python backends and comparing predictions and evaluation metrics.
  Two further use cases demonstrate deep learning on time series and foundation model inference, each executed on a dedicated backend. The prototypes reveal, however, that full cross-backend portability requires deeper harmonization of serialization formats and execution semantics than the process level alone can enforce; backend library versions and preprocessing conventions outside the specification's boundary also affect reproducibility. Addressing both through explicit backend conformance profiles represents the most important near-term direction. The specification advances the reproducibility, portability, and accessibility of ML workflows on EO data cubes across cloud platforms.

</details>


### [67] [On the Potential of Multi-Task Learning in Predictive Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13477)
*Lukas Kirchdorfer,Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统实证研究多任务学习(PPM)在预测性流程监控中的效果，表明MTL是PPM的强有力范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性流程监控(PPM)中，深度学习虽提升了预测精度，但多数方法采用单任务学习(STL)，每个任务单独训练模型，增加维护成本且忽略任务协同。多任务学习(MTL)是潜在替代方案，但其在PPM中的有效性尚不明确：MTL是否及在何种设置下优于STL、哪些任务受益最大、哪些任务组合协同性强、是否需要及如何平衡任务，均未探明。

Method: 对PPM的MTL进行首次全面实证研究，评估多种任务组合、神经网络架构与优化方法。

Result: MTL在下一活动预测上有显著提升，并天然缓解类别不平衡；任务平衡在低容量模型下尤为关键。

Conclusion: MTL是PPM的强有力范式。

Abstract: Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) forecasts how ongoing organizational processes unfold, enabling information systems to move beyond execution support toward proactive analysis and monitoring. Although deep learning has improved prediction accuracy in PPM, most approaches follow a single-task learning (STL) setup, training a separate model per task. This increases maintenance effort and overlooks potential synergies. Multi-task learning (MTL), which jointly learns multiple prediction targets in one model, offers a promising alternative, yet its effectiveness in PPM remains underexplored. It remains unclear whether and under which settings MTL improves upon STL, which prediction tasks benefit most from joint learning, which task combinations are particularly synergistic, and if and how tasks should be balanced. To fill this gap, we present the first comprehensive empirical study of MTL for PPM, evaluating a variety of task combinations, neural architectures, and optimization methods. Overall, our results position MTL as a strong paradigm for PPM: we see substantial improvements in next-activity prediction and inherent mitigation of class imbalance using MTL, while task balancing is especially critical under low-capacity models.

</details>


### [68] [One Spectrum, Two Resources: Data-Memory Scaling in Autoregressive Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13500)
*Chiwun Yang,Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在正熵自回归检索源中提出"预测-能量谱"，证明数据与记忆两种资源由同一条极小极大律统一支配：数据决定分辨率1/n，记忆决定比特分配达到的水平τ_B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是"需要多少已学记忆，才能从更多数据中获益"。作者希望说明数据量与模型记忆容量并非独立资源，而是被同一能量谱共同约束，并揭示能量-维度配对为何是本质的。

Method: 构建正熵自回归检索源，令每个坐标贡献查询概率乘未知logit半径平方，得到能量谱μ；推导n个预测块与至多2^B值的学习状态下的极小极大律 R*_value(n,B)≍ Φ_μ(n^{-1})+Φ_μ(τ_B)。用掩码query-key注意力头学习路径与取值，显式建模路由、格式与算术误差，并给出指数自适应分配、有限精度实现与双侧算术假设下的计算精度律。

Result: 理论完整恢复正能量谱并证明能量-维度配对的必要性：两个具有相同块能量与块维度边缘分布的因果源，数据与记忆指数却不同。实验复现了数据-记忆崩塌与耦合指数，解释路由与分配机制，并在六个预训练模型规模上检验仅权重量化。

Conclusion: 数据与记忆由统一预测-能量谱支配，遵循极小极大律；能量-维度配对不可舍；该框架为比特分配、有限精度与量化提供理论刻画，并被注意力机制实验与多规模模型验证。

Abstract: How much learned memory is needed to benefit from more data? We show that the two resources are governed by one predictive-energy spectrum in a positive-entropy autoregressive retrieval source. Each coordinate contributes its query probability times the squared radius of its unknown logit. Writing $μ$ for the resulting energy spectrum, we prove the minimax law $\mathfrak R^*_{\rm value}(n,B)\asymp_R Φ_μ(n^{-1})+Φ_μ(τ_B), Φ_μ(t)=\int\min\{x,t\}\,μ(\mathrm dx),$ for $n$ prediction blocks and a learned state with at most $2^B$ values. Data set the resolution $1/n$; memory sets the level $τ_B$ reached by optimal bit allocation. The complete curve also recovers the positive spectrum. Energy-dimension pairing is essential: two causal sources with identical block-energy and block-dimension marginals have different data and memory exponents. A masked query-key attention head learns the route and values, realizing the law with explicit routing, format, and arithmetic errors. Further results give exponent-adaptive allocation, finite-precision realization, and compute-precision laws under two-sided arithmetic assumptions. Experiments recover the data-memory collapse and coupling exponents, explain the routing and allocation mechanisms, and examine weight-only quantization across six pretrained-model scales.

</details>


### [69] [Pretraining for Sample-Efficient Neural Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13507)
*Ben Tang,Zachary Spalding,Gregory B. Cogan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAPA——一种仅增加空间编码（解剖区域嵌入+相对位置编码）的掩码自编码器，使iEEG自监督预训练能够跨受试者、跨任务迁移，并在Neuroprobe三个基准设定中取得新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能神经解码器通常需要为每个新受试者采集大量带标签数据，成本高昂。自监督预训练可利用跨受试者累积的无标签记录学习通用神经表征，从而降低标注需求。但对颅内脑电（iEEG）而言，受试者之间电极接触位置与神经解剖结构差异大，导致自监督学习难以奏效。

Method: MAPA：一个结构上基本为普通掩码自编码器（masked autoencoder）的模型，引入两种空间编码——解剖区域嵌入（anatomical region embedding）与相对位置编码（relative positional encoding），二者共同作用以学习可迁移的神经表征。

Result: 在Neuroprobe基准的三种设定（同会话、跨会话、跨受试者）上均刷新SOTA，且无需微调。跨受试者设定下，MAPA特征的线性探针仅需约164个带标签试次即可达到无预训练时需3,500个试次才能达到的准确率。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练能够扩展到异质的iEEG记录，并减少新受试者实现高精度解码所需的标注数据量。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) decode neural activity to restore lost function. Typically, training a high-performance neural decoder requires a large labeled dataset to be collected from every new subject. One way to reduce the labeled data cost is self-supervised pretraining, which learns general neural representations from unlabeled recordings that accumulate across subjects. However, for intracranial electroencephalography (iEEG) recordings, self-supervised learning has been challenging due to differences in contact placement and neuroanatomy between subjects. We propose MAPA, an otherwise vanilla masked autoencoder with two spatial encodings, an anatomical region embedding and a relative positional encoding, which together enable it to learn neural representations that transfer to unseen subjects and across various tasks. MAPA sets a new state of the art across all three regimes of the Neuroprobe benchmark without fine-tuning: within-session, cross-session, and cross-subject. In the cross-subject regime, a linear probe on MAPA's features needs only ${\sim}164$ labeled trials to reach the accuracy that takes 3,500 without pretraining. Our results show that self-supervised pretraining can scale across heterogeneous iEEG recordings and reduce the labeled data needed for accurate decoding in new subjects.

</details>


### [70] [Adaptive Phase-Switching for Communication-Efficient Federated LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13512)
*Jerry Adams Franklin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文测量了双向B-only联邦LoRA协议的每轮通信开销，提出自适应阶段切换聚合器ReverseAdaptive，在通信-质量前沿的拐点处切换，实现显著通信节省且质量损失小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA协议的通信核算忽略了协议切换聚合模式时的非对称过渡轮，且所报告的节省未考虑分组查询注意力（GQA）的形状，导致通信成本被低估或核算不准确。

Method: 测量双向B-only联邦LoRA协议的每轮上传与下载字节数；将五种方法（含三种已有工作）置于统一的通信-质量前沿上；提出ReverseAdaptive自适应阶段切换聚合器，通过监测全局训练损失的相对改进并与无量纲阈值比较来定位前沿拐点，而非预设阶段边界。

Result: 在TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat与Alpaca上，ReverseAdaptive相比FLoRA实现40.5%的实测往返通信节省，留出集指令跟随损失代价为0.0063；相比FFA-LoRA在留出损失上优0.0182，超过该指标最大单方法种子标准差二十倍以上。同一阈值跨模型规模无需重新调参即可迁移，过渡的质量代价在测试的两个数据集上保持稳定。

Conclusion: 在冻结前先学习LoRA因子可产生更好的适配器；ReverseAdaptive能在通信-质量前沿拐点处自适应切换，实现可迁移、稳定的通信节省与质量平衡。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models with low-rank adaptation reduces per-client trainable parameters, but client-to-server communication remains the dominant cost. Existing accounting for federated LoRA protocols omits the asymmetric transition round when a protocol changes aggregation mode, and reports savings that ignore grouped-query attention shapes. This paper measures per-round upload and download bytes for a bidirectional B-only federated LoRA protocol and places five methods, three from prior work, on a single communication-quality frontier. The frontier has a knee, which an adaptive phase-switching aggregator, ReverseAdaptive, locates by monitoring the relative improvement in global training loss against a dimensionless threshold rather than by fixing a phase boundary in advance. On TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat with Alpaca, ReverseAdaptive attains 40.5 percent measured round-trip savings over FLoRA at a held-out instruction-following loss cost of 0.0063. It outperforms FFA-LoRA, which freezes the first of the two LoRA factors at initialization, by 0.0182 in held-out loss, more than twenty times the largest per-method seed standard deviation on that metric, so learning that factor before freezing it produces better adapters. The same threshold transfers across model scales without retuning, and the quality cost of the transition is stable across the two datasets tested.

</details>


### [71] [Operational Range Bounding in Spectroscopy: A Safety Cage Framework for Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13514)
*Nikki Grens,Luís F. Simões,Kai Hou Yip,Theresa Lueftinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化"安全笼"架构，作为平行监控层，在不修改底层模型的前提下评估预测有效性，用于提升空间任务中黑盒机器学习模型的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全攸关的空间任务中，黑盒机器学习模型难以验证，尤其在缺乏真值的情况下。虽然机器学习可从复杂系外行星光变曲线中提取透射光谱，但其对未建模仪器异常、恒星活动和域偏移的敏感性带来未量化的风险。

Method: 构建模块化安全笼架构，作为平行监控层，通过监测不确定性量化、域外检测和影响函数等多种运行时指标，将模型操作域限制在已验证区域内。利用2019和2021年Ariel数据挑战赛数据集，在域内与跨域条件下进行受控评估。

Result: 模型失败是多方面的，单一指标无法捕捉所有失败模式，需要指标融合。安全驱动的拒绝策略下，数据覆盖率降低20%，可使不同域和评估指标下的误差降低45%至65%。

Conclusion: 安全笼为检测不可靠预测提供了透明机制，是在真值稀少的科学应用（如天体物理学）中安全部署数据驱动模型的关键一步。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of black-box machine learning models in safety-critical space missions remains a significant challenge, particularly when ground-truth is unavailable for validation. Although machine learning models offer a powerful means to augment standard pipelines by extracting transmission spectra from complex exoplanetary light curves, their susceptibility to unmodelled instrument anomalies, stellar activity, and domain shifts introduces unquantified risks. This study evaluates a modular safety cage architecture that operates as a parallel monitoring layer to assess the validity of a prediction without modifying the underlying estimator. By monitoring different runtime indicators, including uncertainty quantification, out-of-domain detection, and influence functions, the framework constrains the model's operational domain to a verified region. A controlled evaluation is conducted under both in-domain and cross-domain conditions, using datasets from the 2019 and 2021 editions of the Ariel Data Challenges. The results reveal that model failure is multifaceted and that no single indicator captures all failure modes, demonstrating the need for indicator fusion. The application of safety-driven rejection strategies shows that a modest 20% reduction in data coverage results in error reductions between 45% and 65% across different domains and evaluation metrics. Using a formalised coverage-risk framework, a systematic analysis of indicator combinations is performed to identify configurations that maximise risk-ranking accuracy and optimise the trade-off between data coverage and scientific performance. Safety cages provide a transparent mechanism for detecting unreliable predictions and represent a critical step towards the safe deployment of data-driven models in scientific applications, such as astrophysics, where ground truth is seldom available.

</details>


### [72] [GeoTTER: Leveraging Local Geometry of Optimal Transport for Zero-Shot Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13518)
*Wei-Yang Alex Lee,Rudrasis Chakraborty,Vishnu Lokhande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoTTER 提出一种将局部几何结构（图-拉普拉斯平滑）与聚类引导代价融合进最优传输的零样本分类框架，缓解标签抖动与角度漂移问题，中位数精度较零样本方法提升 6.82%、较 OTTER 提升 2.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输的零样本分类方法依赖仅由预训练模型嵌入得到的固定代价矩阵，存在标定偏差（miscalibration）与适应性不足的问题，具体表现为两类误差：(1) 高频标签抖动，即样本级流形抖动使相邻嵌入被分到不同类别；(2) 低频的角度漂移，即大群样本相对真实标签原型存在一致的角向偏移。

Method: GeoTTER 包含两项关键技术：一是通过图-拉普拉斯平滑（基于谱图理论）把局部几何结构引入最优传输公式，强制邻域一致性，以缓解高频标签抖动；二是将聚类引导的代价分量与全局调整的传输代价相融合，形成多目标优化，同时兼顾全局分布约束与潜在数据结构，以校正相干角向漂移。

Result: 在多种基准上取得稳健提升，中位数改进为：相较零样本方法 +6.82%，相较 OTTER +2.13%。

Conclusion: 通过将局部几何平滑与聚类引导代价整合进最优传输，GeoTTER 突破了固定代价矩阵的局限，在零样本分类中同时缓解标签抖动与角度漂移，并在多基准上稳定优于现有方法。

Abstract: We present GeoTTER, a novel framework that redefines optimal transport in the realm of zero-shot classification. Conventional methods often suffer from miscalibration and a lack of adaptability, as they rely on fixed cost matrices derived solely from pre-trained model embeddings. In contrast, GeoTTER addresses these limitations by incorporating two key techniques. First, to alleviate high-frequency label jaggedness (sample-level manifold jitter that assigns neighboring embeddings to different classes), GeoTTER integrates local geometric structure into the optimal transport formulation via graph-Laplacian smoothing, a technique grounded in spectral graph theory that enforces neighborhood consistency. Second, to correct coherent angular drift (a low-frequency orientation bias in which large groups of samples share the same angular offset from their true label prototypes), we fuse clustering-guided cost components with a globally adjusted transport cost, achieving a multi-objective optimization that respects both global distribution constraints and latent data structure. With a median improvement of +6.82% compared to zero-shot and +2.13% compared to OTTER, GeoTTER shows robust improvements across a diverse set of benchmarks.

</details>


### [73] [Rolling Day-Wise Mortality Prediction in Critically Ill Patients With AKI on CRRT Utilizing Machine Pressure Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13524)
*Shehan Irteza Pranto,Joanna Yang,Joshua Lambert,Stuart L. Goldstein,Lili Chan,Girish N. Nadkarni,Tiago K. Colicchio,Javier A. Neyra,Jin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次将CRRT机器的分钟级回路压力波形转化为连续死亡风险信号，构建漏检安全的滚动日级预测基准，融合机器与EHR数据使一日死亡率AUROC达0.766。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危重AKI患者接受CRRT时死亡率高，但现有风险评估主要依赖EHR中的临床参数，忽视了CRRT机器产生的分钟级回路压力波形。这一连续记录因共享设备记录污染、非生理分钟和传感器伪影而被丢弃，导致临床医生无法实时察觉恶化，风险仅在抽血化验时被重新评估。

Method: 数据清洗：将机器记录对齐到已记录的疗程区间以防跨患者泄漏，剔除预充与停机分钟，基于合成尖峰注入基准调优去噪，并对未观测区间做掩码而非插补。建模：定义滚动日级任务，采用基于Transformer的堆叠集成，将窗口降采样序列Transformer与使用回路不稳定特征及临床EHR变量的经典模型进行晚期融合。

Result: 在多中心CRRTnet队列（976例患者、4,585个治疗日）的漏检安全基准上，仅机器模型独立预测价值最低（AUROC 0.625），仅EHR模型为0.717，融合EHR与机器流后一日死亡率AUROC达0.766。SHAP归因显示回路不稳定描述符将机器特征在联合模型前15重要特征中的占比从3个提升至7个（20.0%→46.7%），突出滤器压力、跨膜压（TMP）和入-回压差（ARD）的作用。

Conclusion: 这是首个纳入CRRT机器数据的患者级死亡预测研究，将原本被丢弃的床旁数据流转化为连续风险信号。

Abstract: Critically ill patients with acute kidney injury (AKI) on continuous renal replacement therapy (CRRT) face high mortality, yet current risk assessment relies primarily on clinical parameters from electronic health records (EHR) and ignores minute-level circuit pressure waveforms generated by CRRT machines that track the extracorporeal circuit's interaction with the patient. Clinicians therefore cannot see deterioration as it develops. Risk is reassessed only when labs are drawn, while this continuous record is discarded because it is contaminated by shared-device records, non-physiological minutes, and sensor artifacts. To make the stream usable, we aligned machine records to charted therapy intervals to prevent cross-patient leakage, removed priming and downtime minutes, tuned denoising on a synthetic spike-injection benchmark, and masked unobserved intervals rather than imputing them. On this cleaned stream, we define a rolling day-wise task and a transformer-based stacked ensemble that late-fuses a window-reduced sequence transformer with classical models using circuit-instability features and clinical EHR variables. In a leak-safe benchmark on the multi-center CRRTnet cohort (976 patients, 4,585 treatment days), the machine-only model had the lowest standalone prognostic value (AUROC 0.625), followed by the EHR-only model (0.717). Integrating EHR and machine streams reached a one-day mortality AUROC of 0.766. SHAP attribution showed that circuit-instability descriptors raised the machine share of the top 15 combined-model features from 3 to 7 (20.0% to 46.7%), highlighting filter pressure, transmembrane pressure (TMP), and access-to-return difference (ARD). To our knowledge, this is the first patient-level mortality prediction incorporating CRRT machine data, turning a discarded bedside stream into a continuous risk signal.

</details>


### [74] [Generative Interpretability via Scalable Neuro-Symbolic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13529)
*Xiaocong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文主张AI可解释性研究应从"事后解释"转向"生成式可解释性"，即模型推理过程原生暴露可理解、可因果干预的语义检查点，并以神经符号模型作为具体实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从聊天机器人走向智能体系统，输出会转化为对现实产生不可逆后果的行动。现有的事后可解释性范式在结构上不足以支撑安全可信的部署，因为它只能在行为发生后进行解释，无法在推理计算做出输出承诺之前进行审计或干预。

Method: 提出"生成式可解释性"这一架构属性：模型在一次推理前向过程中原生暴露具有语义意义、人类可理解且可进行因果干预的检查点。通过与其他可解释性研究范式进行比较来论证其优势，并提出以神经符号模型作为该理念的具体实例化方案。

Result: 该摘要为立场/观点型论文，未给出实验结果。论点包括：生成式可解释性相较其他范式具备优势，且神经符号模型可作为其可行的具体实现形式。

Conclusion: 面向智能体时代的安全部署需求，可解释性研究需从被动的、事后的事后解释，转向内生于推理过程的生成式可解释性，从而实现推理前的审计与因果干预。

Abstract: As the use of Large Language Models moves from chatbots into agentic systems, where outputs become actions with irreversible consequences on reality, the existing paradigm on AI Interpretability research, post-hoc interpretability, is structurally inadequate for safe and trustworthy model deployment: it explains behavior after the fact but cannot audit or intervene in an inference computation before it commits to an output. We therefore argue for a shift toward \emph{generative interpretability}, an architectural property under which a model's inference pass natively exposes semantically meaningful checkpoints that are human-understandable and amenable to causal intervention. We show the merits of generative interpretability as comparison to other interpretability research paradigms, and propose Neuro-Symbolic Models as a concrete instantiation.

</details>


### [75] [Attention Is All You Need (to Avoid Spurious Oscillations)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13531)
*Jinyoung Jeong,Joseph B. Choi,Xinlun Cheng,H. S. Udaykumar,Sanghun Choi,Stephen S. Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种保守的、固定网格有限体积格式，利用经CFL条件约束的注意力通量，根据当前时间步所需的输运范围自适应选择上游信息，从而在单次更新、较大时间步下实现激波的稳定输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探索注意力机制能否在单次更新中跨越多个网格单元搬运激波而不破坏激波结构。传统显式格式受CFL条件限制，大时间步下激波易失真，作者希望将注意力作为可学习的信息模板（information stencil）来实现保守的大步长激波输运。

Method: 采用一维无粘Burgers输运作为核心机理测试，构建保守固定网格有限体积格式，其中CFL条件约束的注意力通量依据当前时间步所需输运范围选择上游信息。与标准五阶WENO搭配三阶SSP-RK3（WENO-5+SSP-RK3）及受控前向欧拉法对比，以分离通量选择与时间积分的影响。进一步通过推理时干预与重训练消融验证机制，并将方法扩展到二维标量Burgers输运和一维浅水系统。

Result: 所学通量在常规小步长区间保持可靠；当时间步放大四倍时，单阶段每更新即可保持锐利激波。注意力随局部输运范围向上游迁移，并在激波附近更具选择性。推理时干预与重训练消融表明，输运尺度信息与状态相关选择直接贡献于性能。在二维标量Burgers输运与一维浅水系统上，守恒-尺度-选择原则具备可迁移性。

Conclusion: 结果支持将注意力作为一种可学习的信息模板，用于保守的大步长激波输运；同时指出有限的候选感受范围与依赖问题的鲁棒性是当前局限。

Abstract: Can attention move a shock across several cells in one update without breaking it? We develop a conservative, fixed grid finite-volume scheme in which a CFL-conditioned attention flux selects upstream information according to the transport required by the current time step. One-dimensional inviscid Burgers transport is used as the central mechanism test: the same learned flux remains reliable in the conventional small-step regime and, with a time step four times larger, preserves sharp shocks while using one stage per update. A standard fifth-order WENO scheme with third-order strong-stability-preserving Runge-Kutta time integration (WENO-5+SSP-RK3) is included alongside controlled Forward Euler comparisons to separate flux selection from time integration. The learned attention shifts upstream with the local transport reach and becomes more selective near shocks; inference-time interventions and retrained ablations show that transport-scale information and state-dependent selection contribute directly to performance. Directional two-dimensional scalar Burgers transport and the one-dimensional shallow-water system then test whether the conservation-scale-selection principle transfers beyond the original scalar setting. The results support attention as a learnable information stencil for conservative large-step shock transport, while identifying finite candidate reach and problem-dependent robustness as the present limits.

</details>


### [76] [Toward Optimal Switching Regret for Multi-Armed Bandits with Oblivious Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13547)
*Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性多臂老虎机中的切换遗憾问题，在遗忘型对手下，提出单一算法可对任意未知切换次数 S 达到最优遗憾界，解决了 Auer 等人的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当切换次数 S 已知时，最优期望遗憾界可达；但当 S 未知时，Marinov 和 Zimmert 证明在自适应对手下无法实现该保证。本文旨在解决 S 未知且对手为遗忘型时的公开问题。

Method: 算法结合了一个以较小学习率初始化的固定份额学习器，以及通过随机学习率和隐式探索来搜索局部改进的二元区间子程序。采用非均匀先验偏向跟随主学习器，使维护多个子程序的开销保持较小。当子程序相对主学习器积累足够改进时，其学习率翻倍，从而适应未知的切换次数 S。

Result: 所提单一算法在遗忘型对手下，对每个 S 都能达到 Ã(√((S+1)KT)) 的期望遗憾界。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了 Auer 等人提出的公开问题，证明了单一算法可对任意未知 S 实现最优切换遗憾保证。

Abstract: We study switching regret in adversarial multi-armed bandits, where the learner competes with an arm sequence that changes at most $S$ times. When $S$ is known, an optimal expected regret of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{(S+1)KT})$ is obtainable [Auer et al., 2002]. However, when $S$ is unknown, Marinov and Zimmert [2021] show that this guarantee is impossible under an adaptive adversary. In this paper, we show that a single algorithm achieves $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{(S+1)KT})$ expected regret for every $S$ against an oblivious adversary, resolving an open problem of Auer et al. [2019b]. Our algorithm combines a fixed-share learner initialized with a small learning rate and dyadic-interval subroutines that search for local improvements using randomized learning rates and implicit exploration. Importantly, a non-uniform prior favors following the main learner, keeping the cost of maintaining many subroutines small. When the subroutines accumulate sufficient improvement over the main learner, its learning rate doubles, allowing adaptation to the unknown number of comparator switches $S$.

</details>


### [77] [When Greedy Sampling Explores: KL-Regularized Contextual Bandits without Eluder-Dimension Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13564)
*Zichen Wang,Haoyang Hong,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究KL正则化上下文赌博机，在奖励与偏好两种反馈下，证明贪心采样无需显式依赖eluder维度即可实现对数遗憾，并揭示了贪心采样与UCB式探索之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文赌博机算法通常依赖eluder维度等复杂度度量来刻画遗憾界，且探索策略（如UCB式）较为复杂。作者希望探究在KL正则化下，简单贪心采样能否获得更强的、与eluder维度无关的遗憾保证。

Method: 针对奖励反馈，提出直接从估计奖励诱导的Gibbs策略中采样的简单贪心算法；并将结果扩展到偏好反馈，涵盖一般偏好模型与Bradley-Terry模型，同时改进已有的维度相关保证。

Result: 证明贪心采样可实现对数遗憾，且界不显式依赖eluder维度；在偏好反馈下同样成立，并锐化了现有维度相关遗憾界。

Conclusion: 分析揭示贪心采样与UCB式探索的权衡：当KL正则化足够强时贪心采样保证更优，而正则化减弱时引入额外探索更有利。

Abstract: We study KL-regularized contextual bandits under both reward and preference feedback. We show that greedy sampling can achieve logarithmic regret without explicit dependence on the eluder dimension. For reward feedback, we establish an eluder-dimension-independent regret bound for a simple greedy algorithm that directly samples from the Gibbs policy induced by the estimated reward. We further extend this result to preference feedback under both the general preference and Bradley--Terry models, while also sharpening existing dimension-dependent guarantees. Our analysis reveals a trade-off between greedy sampling and upper confidence bound-style exploration: greedy sampling enjoys stronger guarantees when KL regularization is sufficiently strong, whereas additional exploration becomes preferable as the regularization weakens.

</details>


### [78] [AttnFuse: A Composable DSL for Compiling Attentions to Fused GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13612)
*Varun Kumar Dasoju,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AttnFuse：一个面向注意力机制的领域专用语言及其编译器，将 RoPE 等乘法前变换作为一等操作，可为整个注意力计算生成单个融合 GPU 内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 是主流 AI 系统的基础，其核心注意力操作占大部分计算与内存开销。研究者不断提出新的注意力变体，但每个变体都需要专家手写 GPU 代码才能达到可用速度。PyTorch 的 flex_attention 允许用 Python 描述自定义注意力模式并编译为融合内核，但其设计仅支持中心矩阵乘法之后的修改，无法涵盖所有主流 LLM 使用的 RoPE 位置编码，导致灵活性受限。

Method: 提出 AttnFuse，一种用于注意力的轻量级 DSL，将 RoPE 等乘法前变换作为一等操作。研究者通过组合十个高层构建块来描述注意力变体，AttnFuse 编译器为整个计算生成单个融合 GPU 内核。同时提出 Rotation Calculus（旋转微积分）理论，分析是否应融合 RoPE 还是单独应用，取决于 GPU 的计算带宽比。

Result: 在 RTX 3090 上，AttnFuse 在 RoPE+因果模式相比 flex_attention 取得 2.10× 加速。在 H100 上，运行完整 Llama-3-8B 训练步骤的性能与 PyTorch 手工调优后端差距在 5% 以内。推导出的计算带宽比交叉点与实测结果一致。

Conclusion: AttnFuse 表明，一个小型、注意力专用的编译器可以弥合灵活研究代码与生产内核之间的差距，同时 Rotation Calculus 为是否融合 RoPE 提供了与实测吻合的理论判据。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are built on the Transformer architecture, whose core operation, attention, accounts for the majority of computation and memory cost. Researchers continually propose new attention variants to improve quality, efficiency, or context length, but each variant currently requires expert-written GPU code to run at usable speeds. PyTorch's recent flex\_attention lets researchers describe custom attention patterns in Python and compile them to fused kernels, but its design is limited to modifications applied after the central matrix multiplication, excluding Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE), the positional encoding used by every major LLM.
  We introduce AttnFuse, a small DSL for attention that makes pre-multiplication transformations like RoPE first-class operations. Researchers compose ten high-level building blocks to describe a variant, and AttnFuse's compiler emits a single fused GPU kernel for the entire computation. On an RTX 3090, AttnFuse achieves a 2.10$\times$ speedup over flex\_attention on the RoPE+causal pattern. On an H100, it runs a full Llama-3-8B training step within 5\% of PyTorch's hand-tuned backend. Our investigation reveals the Rotation Calculus: whether to fuse RoPE or apply it separately depends on the GPU's compute-to-bandwidth ratio, with a derived crossover that matches measurement. AttnFuse demonstrates that a small, attention-specific compiler can close the gap between flexible research code and production kernels.

</details>


### [79] [Affinity-Aware Sharding for Delayed Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13846)
*Eloi de Reynal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 延迟张量并行（DTP）打破了FFN神经元与注意力KV头的置换对称性，使分片本身成为建模决策；通过置换密集模型最大化同设备上KV头与FFN神经元的亲和度，可加速DTP适配的蒸馏或重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTP去除了张量并行Transformer推理中阻塞式的all-reduce，但需要设备立即将自身部分输出加入残差流，延迟接收其他设备的部分输出，这构成真实的架构变更，导致密集Transformer模型必须重训练或蒸馏。作者希望找到一种方法加速这一适配过程。

Method: 作者指出DTP打破了FFN内神经元与注意力模块内KV头的置换对称性，从而分片成为建模决策。为此，他们用一阶近似衡量失去某个头对每个神经元输出的损害，以此定义亲和度；采用坐标上升优化器，交替进行神经元的精确平衡分配和KV头划分的穷举搜索，在分片前置换密集模型以最大化同设备上KV头与FFN神经元的共置亲和度。整个流程在单GPU上对Qwen3-0.6B和Danube3-500M耗时不到两分钟。

Result: 在δ=1时，亲和度优化布局在10k步测试范围内，达到任意蒸馏目标所需步数约为朴素连续布局的一半到三分之二；每个优化种子均优于每个连续种子，且优于十六个随机布局中的十五个。此外，初始化时的共置亲和度分数能预测训练后相对于基础模型的KL散度，在十七种从反优化到优化的布局中，皮尔逊相关系数为-0.81和-0.89。

Conclusion: 置换密集模型以最大化同设备KV头与FFN神经元的共置亲和度，可以显著加速DTP适配的蒸馏或重训练，且初始亲和度分数可作为最终性能的有效预测指标。

Abstract: Delayed Tensor Parallelism (DTP) removes the blocking all-reduce of tensor-parallel Transformer inference. Every device adds its own partial output to its residual stream (and broadcasts it) immediately, but only gathers (receives) the other devices' partials $δ$ modules later. A TP to DTP change therefore amounts to a real architecture change, and dense Transformer models need to be retrained or distilled after adaptation. We show that DTP breaks the permutation symmetry of neurons inside FFNs and of KV heads inside attention modules, and that this symmetry breakage makes the sharding itself a modelling decision. We show that maximising the affinity between the KV heads and the FFN neurons co-located on a device, by permuting the dense model before sharding, speeds up the distillation or retraining process. The affinity is measured with a first-order approximation of the damage that losing a head's contribution does to each neuron's output, and the co-located affinity is maximised with a coordinate-ascent optimiser that alternates an exact balanced assignment of neurons with an exhaustive search over the KV head partitions. The whole procedure takes under two minutes on one GPU for Qwen3-0.6B and Danube3-500M. On these models, at $δ=1$, the affinity-optimised layouts reach any distillation target in about half to two thirds of the steps needed by the naive contiguous layouts, over the whole 10k-step range we tested, and every optimised seed beats every contiguous seed and all but one of the sixteen random layouts. We also show that the co-located affinity score at initialisation predicts the KL to the base model after training, across seventeen layouts ranging from anti-optimised to optimised (Pearson $-0.81$ and $-0.89$).

</details>


### [80] [An Efficient and Modular Framework for Targeted Harm Mitigation in LLMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13624)
*Roberto Campbell,Momin Abbass,Muneeza Azmat,Michal Ulewicz,Raya Horesh,Kristjan Greenewald,Rogério Abreu de Paula,Nathalie Baracaldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模块化纠正框架，用Activated LoRA适配器与上下文感知路由机制，在生成中期低延迟地激活专家适配器，修正LLM的偏见、毒性与有害输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽为零样本学习强者，但易与人类偏好错位，产生偏见、毒性等有害输出；现有对齐方法成本高且与模型紧耦合，灵活性与可扩展性受限。

Method: 在预训练LLM上增加Activated LoRA (aLoRA)适配器与上下文感知路由机制。专家适配器可在序列中期激活且不使KV缓存失效，实现生成中低延迟、针对性纠正；每个专家负责检测并缓解特定危害（如偏见、毒性）；学习型路由器依据模型中间输出动态选择合适专家。

Result: 在标准安全基准上提升了对齐效果，同时保持任务性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为更安全、更可控的LLM部署提供了轻量高效的路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful zero-shot learners but remain prone to misalignment with human preferences, often producing biased, toxic, or otherwise harmful outputs. Existing alignment methods, while effective, are costly and tightly coupled to the model, limiting flexibility and scalability. We propose a modular correction framework that augments pretrained LLMs with Activated LoRA (aLoRA) adapters and a context-aware routing mechanism to eliminate harms from misaligned model responses. Our approach enables expert adapters to activate mid-sequence without invalidating the KV cache, allowing low-latency, targeted correction during generation. Each expert is trained to detect and mitigate specific harms, such as bias or toxicity. A learned router dynamically selects appropriate experts based on the models intermediate outputs. We demonstrate that our system improves alignment on standard safety benchmarks while preserving task performance, offering a lightweight and efficient path toward safer and more controllable LLM deployments.

</details>


### [81] [Minibatch persistency, eight years later: what batch reuse costs in steps and joules, and what it saves in data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13922)
*Matteo Fischetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册实验系统检验了minibatch persistency（小批量复用）的真实收益，发现在大batch下它节省的是数据而非算力，且效果无法与学习率调度位置区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小批量复用自2019年被并入data echoing以来，一直存在两个问题：一是被质疑其效果只是模仿了更大的学习率，二是从未有与该方法同样精心调参的基线对照。本文旨在填补这一缺失的严格测试。

Method: 预注册研究：在FineWeb-Edu上训练49M参数Transformer，minibatch size B∈{32,128,512}，每格8个随机种子；对每个batch size和每个实验臂分别调优学习率；对照臂仅改变采样方式，不引入复用。主要指标为达到固定loss的成本，从四个维度衡量：优化步数、新token量、秒数、插座端焦耳数。随后在新种子和更新一代GPU上复现，并将三个实验臂置于同一以步数为单位的调度中比较。

Result: 小批量复用带来的是数据节省而非速度或能耗节省，且仅在大minibatch size下成立：B=32时它比基线读取更多新token而非更少；在步数、秒数和焦耳上至多是不亏；在表现最好的B=512下，n=8种子时注册对照臂无法将复用效果与学习率调度位置区分开。

Conclusion: 该技术适用于新数据而非算力成为瓶颈成本的场景：语料会耗尽、流水线按样本付费、数据流无法回退。在数据可重复读取的场景下，间隔式epoch效果同等或更好。

Abstract: Minibatch persistency reuses data instead of reading it: rather than drawing a fresh minibatch at every optimizer step, it takes K consecutive steps on the same one. Absorbed into data echoing in 2019, it has carried one objection -- that reuse merely imitates a larger learning rate -- and no baseline tuned as carefully as the method itself. This paper runs the missing test. A pre-registered study trains a 49M-parameter Transformer on FineWeb-Edu at minibatch size B in {32, 128, 512}, 8 seeds per cell, tuning the learning rate separately for every batch size and every arm, against a reuse-free control that changes the sampling and nothing else. Each headline claim is a cost to reach a fixed loss, read on four axes: optimizer steps, fresh tokens, seconds, and joules at the socket. We then replicate on new seeds and a newer GPU generation, and put the three arms on one schedule in steps. The outcome of our study is that what minibatch reuse buys is neither speed nor energy but data, and only at large minibatch size: at B = 32 it reads more fresh tokens than the baseline, not fewer. On steps, seconds and joules it is at best free; and at B = 512, where it looks best, a registered control cannot separate the effect of reuse from the position on the learning-rate schedule at n = 8 seeds. The technique is therefore worth using where fresh data rather than compute is the binding cost: a corpus that runs out, a pipeline that pays per sample, a stream that cannot be rewound. Where the data can simply be read again, spaced epochs do as well or better.

</details>


### [82] [FlowTSFM: Turning Encoder Depth into Quantile Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13640)
*Bahaeddine Abdessalem,Shifeng Xie,Zehao Xiao,Youssef Attia El Hili,Ambroise Odonnat,Jianfeng Zhang,Lujia Pan,Keli Zhang,Malik Tiomoko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowTSFM 将编码器深度视为循环传输过程，用共享参数的单一 Transformer 块迭代应用，并以分位数流目标监督中间状态，在参数量约 3 倍更少的情况下取得具有竞争力的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编码器的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）通常堆叠多个独立参数的 Transformer 层，仅对最终预测进行监督，中间表示缺乏显式的预测角色，导致参数效率低、表示结构不明确。

Method: 提出 FlowTSFM：单个 Transformer 块以共享参数循环迭代应用，将深度解释为从先验分布到最终预测的传输过程；采用分位数流目标，结合 pinball 预测损失与路径级位置匹配，对中间状态进行监督。

Result: 模型仅 38.8M 参数，在 GIFT-Eval 和 TIME 上表现具有竞争力，MASE 与更强基线相差 1.8–4.6%，参数量约为 12 层 Chronos-2（119.5M）的 1/3。新提出的尺度无关诊断指标 CosMean 在匹配的中间状态探测协议下达到 0.919，而 Chronos-2 为 0.350。

Conclusion: 循环参数共享结合路径监督，能够在有利的精度—效率权衡下带来结构更清晰的预测轨迹，说明深度作为循环传输过程是一种有效且高效的编码器设计范式。

Abstract: Encoder-based time series foundation models (TSFMs) typically rely on deep stacks of independently parameterized Transformer layers, where only the final forecast is supervised and intermediate representations have no explicit predictive role. We introduce FlowTSFM, an encoder architecture that interprets depth as a recurrent transport process: a single Transformer block is iteratively applied with shared parameters, while a quantile-flow objective supervises intermediate states along a prescribed trajectory from a prior distribution toward the final forecast. The objective combines pinball forecasting loss with path-level position matching. With only 38.8M parameters, FlowTSFM achieves competitive performance on GIFT-Eval and TIME, remaining within 1.8-4.6% MASE of stronger baselines while using approximately $3\times$ fewer parameters than a 12-layer Chronos-2 model (119.5M). Beyond accuracy, we introduce CosMean, a scale-free diagnostic measuring whether recurrent updates consistently align toward the final prediction. Under a matched intermediate-state probing protocol, FlowTSFM achieves a CosMean score of 0.919 compared with 0.350 for Chronos-2, suggesting that recurrent parameter sharing combined with path supervision is associated with substantially more structured predictive trajectories at a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [83] [When Compliance Data Masquerades as Evaluation: Measurement Validity for Deployed AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13642)
*Hung-Yu Lin,Xingran Huang,Qiming Guo,Jinwen Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，将部署AI系统的运营监测数据（如自动驾驶的脱离与碰撞报告）误当作比较评估基准，是一种测量效度问题；并借用自动驾驶安全评估案例，提出"评估契约"以明确比较性结论所需的对齐假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署AI系统评估中存在一种反复出现的失败：为运营监测或合规监管而收集的数据，被当作专为比较评估而设计的数据来解读。自动驾驶领域提供了具体例证：美国的脱离报告与碰撞报告制度产生了有价值的运营证据，但报告范围、暴露量、部署域、事件捕获方式及比较对象的构造存在差异，使这些测量本身只能支撑有限的安全主张。作者希望将这一问题从"交通领域的数据局限"提升为AI评估中具有普遍意义的测量效度问题。

Method: 概念性论证与案例研究。作者将问题形式化为测量效度问题，提出关于已部署AI系统的比较性主张需要六方面对齐：预期能力、测量结果、暴露机会、部署域、数据生成过程、评估比较对象。并以自动驾驶安全评估为案例，提出一个"评估契约"，在把运营数据解读为比较性性能证据之前，先行明确这些假设。

Result: 分析表明，运营监测数据与监管合规数据在报告范围、暴露量、部署域、事件捕获和比较对象构造上的差异，限制了仅凭这些测量所能支持的安全结论；现有制度下的数据不足以直接支撑比较性安全主张，需要显式化的假设框架才能进行有效解读。

Conclusion: 更广泛的启示是：对监测已部署AI系统有用的数据，并不自动构成评估这些系统的有效基准。评估者需区分监测用途与比较评估用途，并通过"评估契约"显式声明对齐假设，以避免测量效度失效。

Abstract: We argue that a recurring failure in the evaluation of deployed AI systems occurs when data collected for operational monitoring or regulatory compliance are interpreted as if they were designed for comparative evaluation. Automated driving provides a concrete example of this problem. U.S. disengagement and crash-reporting regimes produce valuable operational evidence, but differences in reporting scope, exposure, deployment domain, event capture, and comparator construction limit the safety claims that can be supported from these measurements alone. We frame this issue as a measurement-validity problem in AI evaluation rather than as a transportation-specific data limitation. We argue that comparative claims about deployed AI systems require alignment between the intended capability, measured outcome, exposure opportunity, deployment domain, data-generation process, and evaluation comparator. Using automated-driving safety evaluation as a case study, we propose an evaluation contract that makes these assumptions explicit before operational data are interpreted as evidence of comparative performance. The broader implication is that data useful for monitoring deployed AI systems are not automatically valid benchmarks for evaluating them.

</details>


### [84] [OCT-FedSIR: Toward Trustworthy Federated Ophthalmic Learning under Annotation Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14734)
*Sina Gholami,Abdulmoneam Ali,Tania Haghighi,Rashadul H. Badhon,Behafarin Emam,Sally S. Y. Ong,Atalie C. Thompson,Theodore Leng,Ahmed Arafa,Jennifer I. Lim,Minhaj Nur Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OCT-FedSIR，一种可靠性感知的谱框架，用于客户端依赖标注噪声与异构数据分布下的联邦OCT分类，可识别并纠正噪声客户端而非丢弃数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可在不集中患者数据的前提下协作建模，但参与机构的标注可靠性无法保证。在眼科影像中，疾病患病率与类别构成的差异会与监督被破坏（损坏）所产生的变化相似，导致难以区分。

Method: OCT-FedSIR结合类别均衡的谱估计、Stage-I logit调整、互补谱描述子、选择性谱重标注与噪声感知联邦优化。在Kermany、伊利诺伊大学芝加哥分校与Wake Forest数据集上，评估对称与结构化非对称噪声及三种非IID异构程度。

Result: 在117个实验条件下，平均准确率86.73%，优于RoFL的79.94%与FedCorr的78.75%；在所有条件下均能正确区分原始与损坏标注客户端，而原始FedSIR识别流程稳健性较差，尤其非对称噪声下。谱重标注恢复77.2%损坏标注，纠正精确率91.3%，误纠正率3.5%。保留纠正后客户端比谱剪枝平均高9.30个百分点。

Conclusion: 标注噪声通常可被识别并纠正，而无需丢弃有信息量的客户端数据。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model development without centralizing patient data, but annotation reliability at participating institutions cannot always be assumed. In ophthalmic imaging, differences in disease prevalence and class composition can resemble changes caused by corrupted supervision. We introduce OCT-FedSIR, a reliability-aware spectral framework for federated OCT classification under client-dependent annotation noise and heterogeneous data distributions. OCT-FedSIR combines class-balanced spectral estimation, Stage-I logit adjustment, complementary spectral descriptors, selective spectral relabeling, and noise-aware federated optimization. We evaluated the framework on the Kermany, University of Illinois Chicago, and Wake Forest datasets under symmetric and structured asymmetric noise and three levels of non-IID heterogeneity. Across 117 experimental conditions, OCT-FedSIR achieved a mean accuracy of 86.73%, compared with 79.94% for RoFL and 78.75% for FedCorr. It correctly separated clients with original and corrupted annotations across all evaluated conditions, while the original FedSIR identification procedure was less robust, particularly under asymmetric noise. Spectral relabeling recovered 77.2% of corrupted annotations with 91.3% correction precision and a 3.5% false-correction rate. Retaining corrected clients outperformed spectral pruning by 9.30 percentage points on average. These findings show that annotation noise can often be identified and corrected without discarding informative client data.

</details>


### [85] [Curvature-Independent Regret Bounds for Distributed Online Optimization on Hadamard Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13646)
*Zhanyuan Cai,Emre Sahinoglu,Shahin Shahrampour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作研究Hadamard流形上的去中心化在线黎曼优化，针对horospherical凸（h-凸）函数放宽曲率依赖，提出D-ROGD算法并给出曲率无关的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有测地凸（g-凸）下的分析通常需要曲率信息（如截面曲率有限下界），曲率也会进入步长或切空间黎曼共识的收缩因子，限制了方法的适用性与简洁性。作者希望对更窄的h-凸函数类去除这种曲率依赖。

Method: 提出Distributed Riemannian Online Gradient Descent (D-ROGD)，将局部黎曼h-次梯度更新与隐式Fréchet均值共识相结合，并在h-凸与强h-凸局部目标下进行遗憾分析。

Result: 对h-凸和强h-凸局部目标，分别建立O(√T)与O(log T)的静态遗憾，与欧氏对应速率在T上匹配，网络依赖仅由谱隙决定；实验在双曲嵌入上验证了预测速率且未见曲率导致的退化。

Conclusion: 给出了Hadamard流形上去中心化在线优化的首批曲率无关遗憾保证，表明在h-凸设定下曲率不再必要地进入收敛与共识分析。

Abstract: This work addresses decentralized online Riemannian optimization on Hadamard manifolds. Prior work under geodesic convexity (g-convexity) may require curvature information in the optimization analysis, typically through a finite lower bound on the sectional curvature. Curvature may also enter the step size or contraction factor of tangent-space Riemannian consensus schemes. In this work, we relax the curvature dependence for a narrower class of horospherical convex (h-convex) functions. We study Distributed Riemannian Online Gradient Descent (D-ROGD), which combines local Riemannian h-subgradient updates with an implicit Fréchet-mean consensus. For h-convex and strongly h-convex local objectives, we establish $O(\sqrt{T})$ and $O(\log T)$ static regret, respectively, matching the corresponding Euclidean rates with respect to $T$, with network dependence governed solely by the spectral gap. To our knowledge, these are the first curvature-independent regret guarantees for decentralized online optimization on Hadamard manifolds. Experiments on hyperbolic embeddings corroborate the predicted rates, with no observable degradation due to curvature.

</details>


### [86] [Online Bayesian Node Classification on Inductive Graphs under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13655)
*Jinwen Xu,Gonzalo Mateos Buckstein,Qin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在线变分贝叶斯最后一层（online GVBLL）方法，在动态图上同时实现归纳泛化与校准不确定性的节点分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演化图上的节点分类需满足两个要求：一是对分布漂移下新到达节点的归纳泛化，二是面向安全敏感应用的校准不确定性。标准GNN只训练一次且无法满足这两个要求。

Method: 在确定性GNN编码器之上放置随机最后一层参数以量化不确定性。因分类所需的类别softmax似然破坏高斯共轭性，训练后验与测试时流式更新均无闭式解。为此提出变分贝叶斯最后一层（VBLL）目标，通过最大化含蒙特卡洛期望对数似然的证据下界，联合训练编码器与近似最后一层后验。测试时冻结编码器，对最后一层后验施加在线拉普拉斯更新，该更新对应于带指数遗忘和以训练后验为KL锚点的幂先验贝叶斯模型。

Result: 在五个分布漂移下的节点分类基准上，online GVBLL 是唯一在每个数据集上都同时取得最佳准确率与负对数似然的方法。相比最强非GVBLL基线，在 Cora 上准确率提升最多17个百分点，在 ogbn-arxiv 上提升14个百分点，同时在校准性上与 MC Dropout、Deep Ensembles、Temperature Scaling 及高斯过程分类器保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过变分贝叶斯最后一层与在线拉普拉斯更新的结合，online GVBLL 在动态图节点分类中兼顾了归纳泛化与校准不确定性，并在多个基准上优于现有方法。

Abstract: On evolving graphs, node classifiers must satisfy two key requirements: inductive generalization to newly arriving nodes under distribution shift and calibrated uncertainty for safety-sensitive applications. Standard graph neural networks (GNNs) are typically trained once and address neither requirement. We adapt the Bayesian last-layer (BLL) model by placing random last-layer parameters on top of a deterministic GNN encoder for uncertainty quantification. The categorical softmax likelihood required for classification breaks Gaussian conjugacy, so neither the training posterior nor the test-time streaming update has a closed-form solution. To address both challenges, we introduce a variational Bayesian last-layer (VBLL) objective that jointly trains the encoder and an approximate last-layer posterior by maximizing an evidence lower bound with a Monte Carlo expected log-likelihood. At test time, we freeze the encoder and apply an online Laplace update to the last-layer posterior. This update corresponds to a power-prior Bayesian model with exponential forgetting and a Kullback-Leibler anchor to the training posterior. Across five node-classification benchmarks under distribution shift, online GVBLL is the only method to achieve the best accuracy and negative log-likelihood on every dataset. It improves accuracy by up to 17 percentage points on Cora and 14 percentage points on ogbn-arxiv over the strongest non-GVBLL baseline, while remaining competitive in calibration with MC Dropout, Deep Ensembles, Temperature Scaling, and Gaussian-process classifiers.

</details>


### [87] [Leakage-Safe and Scheduler-Aware Machine Learning for Grid Job Runtime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13701)
*Ashfaq Ali Shafin,Khandaker Mamun Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将GWA-T-4 AuverGrid作业运行时间预测重构为无泄漏的执行前预测任务，采用仅提交时刻特征与时序/冷启动评估，CatBoost取得最佳部署导向结果，仿真显示预测辅助SJF较FCFS降低平均等待时间50.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的作业运行时间预测有助于网格与分布式环境中的调度感知资源管理，但预测模型必须在真实部署约束下评估。现有工作常依赖随机交叉验证并可能引入执行后信息泄漏，导致性能被高估，缺乏对时序泛化与冷启动场景的可靠考察。

Method: 基于GWA-T-4 AuverGrid工作负载轨迹，将目标定义为作业级运行时间，仅使用提交时刻属性并排除执行后变量；在时序与冷启动设置下评估，而非仅用随机交叉验证。对比标准回归器、按时间顺序的历史基线、类别编码策略及支持原生类别处理的CatBoost，并加入时序超参数调优、可预测性分析、特征消融、按作业长度的误差分析以及最小调度仿真。

Result: 经时序验证调优后，CatBoost在留出时序测试集上取得R^2=0.239、MAE=27,019、RMSE=46,587、LogMAE=2.646。在全部69,523个留出时序测试作业上的单服务器仿真显示，预测辅助SJF相较FCFS将平均等待时间降低50.92%。

Conclusion: 随机划分评估会高估性能；原生类别增强提升时序泛化能力；长作业低估仍是调度相关的重要挑战。

Abstract: Accurate job runtime prediction can improve scheduling-aware resource management in grid and distributed computing environments, but prediction models must be evaluated under realistic deployment constraints. This paper revisits CPU burst time prediction on the GWA-T-4 AuverGrid workload trace and reformulates it as leakage-safe pre-execution job runtime prediction. We define the target as job-level runtime, use only submission-time attributes, exclude post-execution variables, and evaluate models under temporal and cold-start settings rather than relying only on random cross-validation. We compare standard regressors, chronological historical baselines, categorical encoding strategies, and CatBoost with native categorical handling. We further add temporal hyperparameter tuning, runtime predictability analysis, feature ablation, error analysis by job length, and a minimal scheduling simulation. After temporal-validation tuning, CatBoost achieves the strongest deployment-oriented result with R^2=0.239, MAE=27,019, RMSE=46,587, and LogMAE=2.646 on the held-out temporal test set. A single-server simulation over all 69,523 held-out temporal test jobs shows that prediction-informed SJF reduces average waiting time by 50.92% relative to FCFS. The results show that random-split evaluation overestimates performance, categorical-native boosting improves temporal generalization, and long-job underestimation remains a scheduler-relevant challenge.

</details>


### [88] [Certifying Model Upgrades with Slice-Wise Non-Regression and Incumbent Fallback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13714)
*Shengwei Zhang,Tao Wu,Fei Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究"相对保留基线、带非退化容差"的检查点选择问题，区分"未检出危害"与"证明非劣性"，给出可复现发布流程并分析其有限样本保证与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更新模型可能提升总体指标却损害对下游用户重要的切片。关键区别在于"未检出危害"与"证明非劣性"：在噪声评估下，前者可能高概率发布有害更新。因此需要一套可审计的、相对现有基线的检查点选择与发布协议。

Method: 提出可复现的发布流程：将候选搜索与独立的配对评估分离；当认证失败时返回精确的原基线。基于交集-并（intersection-union）检验与 Learn-then-Test 原则，给出三种情形下的有限样本保证：单个冻结候选、有限候选库、预设检验顺序。并指出联合发布决策无需切片数的 Bonferroni 惩罚，但认证功效仍可能随切片数增加而下降。

Result: 有界分数模拟中，在32切片设置下"未检出危害"门控在99.7%的试验中发布了有害候选，而精确非劣性门控在5%目标下仅2.6%。构造的双块候选族在相同候选数下比标量路径获得更高认证效用。公开 digits 实验中（含后续提升平均总体精度的延续实验），因认证功效不足，每次运行都返回原基线。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一套可审计的协议及其局限性，但不构成在基础模型或多语言翻译升级上的收益证明。

Abstract: An updated model can improve an aggregate metric while degrading a slice that matters to a downstream user. We study checkpoint selection subject to non-regression tolerances relative to a retained incumbent. The central distinction is between failing to detect harm and certifying non-inferiority: the former can release harmful updates with high probability when evaluation is noisy. We give a reproducible release procedure that separates candidate search from independent, paired evaluation and returns the exact incumbent when certification fails. Applying established intersection-union and Learn-then-Test principles, we state finite-sample guarantees for one frozen candidate, a finite candidate library, and a prespecified testing order. A joint release decision does not require a slice-count Bonferroni penalty, although certification power can still decrease with the number of slices. In bounded-score simulations, a no-detected-harm gate releases a harmful candidate in 99.7% of trials in one 32-slice setting, compared with 2.6% for an exact non-inferiority gate at a 5% target. A constructed two-block family yields larger certified utility than a scalar path under matched candidate counts. Public digits experiments, including a subsequent continuation that improves average aggregate accuracy, return the incumbent in every run because certification is underpowered. These results establish an auditable protocol and its limitations; they do not establish benefits on foundation-model or multilingual translation upgrades.

</details>


### [89] [A Variational Optimal Transport Operator on Incompressible Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13729)
*Jinjin He,Shenyifan Lu,Sinan Wang,Zhiqi Li,Duowen Chen,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VIOT 是一种生成式神经算子，用于摊销式不可压缩密度输运，可实时预测无散速度场并生成完整输运轨迹，比逐对优化基线快约 10^4 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不可压缩流体输运依赖伴随流体求解器与可微模拟，对每个新的源-目标密度对都需从头优化，耗时可达小时级，难以复用和交互。

Method: VIOT 由三部分组成：1）流函数或矢量势表示，从构造上保证无散（不可压缩）；2）正则化不可压缩输运目标，平衡端点精度与流场平滑度；3）Fourier 神经算子骨干，将求解摊销到新密度对与不同网格分辨率上。给定源-目标对，前馈推理即可预测无散速度场并生成完整轨迹。

Result: 在 2D 与 3D 密度输运基准上，2D 与 3D rollout 每对均在数秒内完成；2D 对比中逐实例基线需从头优化，耗时约一小时，在线加速约 10^4 倍。系统还支持实时交互，可为用户手绘的源-目标对生成不可压缩输运。

Conclusion: VIOT 将不可压缩输运转化为可复用的神经算子，兼顾精度与平滑性，并具备超出训练分布的生成能力，适用于多种输运过程与实时交互场景。

Abstract: We present the Variational Incompressible Optimal Transport (VIOT) operator, a generative neural operator for amortized incompressible density transport. Given a new source-target density pair, VIOT predicts a divergence-free velocity field and generates the full transport trajectory by feed-forward inference, replacing the hour-scale per-pair optimization used by adjoint fluid solvers and differentiable simulation baselines. The system consists of three components: a stream-function or vector-potential representation that enforces incompressibility by construction, a regularized incompressible transport objective that balances endpoint accuracy and flow smoothness, and a Fourier Neural Operator backbone that amortizes the solve across new pairs and grid resolutions. Together, these components make incompressible transport a reusable neural operator that facilitates various transport processes. Further, the generative capability extends beyond the training distribution, with VIOT producing incompressible transports for user-drawn source-target pairs in a real-time interactive system. We demonstrate VIOT on 2D and 3D density-transport benchmarks. Both 2D and 3D rollouts complete in seconds per pair, while per-instance baselines in our 2D comparisons optimize each new pair from scratch and require on the order of an hour, a roughly $10^4\times$ online speedup.

</details>


### [90] [JumpStart Your Policy Learning with Lessons from 160,000 Training Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13730)
*Nabil Omi,Eric Bae,Chung Yik Edward Yeung,Siddhartha Sen,Ali Farhadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对离线强化学习和模仿学习进行了大规模实证研究，训练了超过16万个策略、覆盖114个数据集，发现没有单一算法占优，超参数调优和基准组成会显著影响结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略学习的可靠进展依赖于谨慎的报告、良好调优的基线和多样条件下的评估，但以往工作虽指出结果对报告选择、超参数调优和数据集属性敏感，这些变异性来源尚未被系统性地联合研究。

Method: 开展大规模实证研究，训练超过160,000个策略，涵盖114个数据集，系统考察报告选择、超参数调优与数据集属性对结论的影响，并研究超参数敏感性及跨环境迁移。

Result: 在该规模下没有算法占据主导：最强方法的总体性能往往接近，但领先者在不同环境中差异很大；恰当的超参数调优经常改变算法排名；基准组成可能产生相互矛盾的结论；并找到一种推导强默认配置的简单策略。

Conclusion: 基于发现开发了数据集条件化的推荐器，为从业者提供任务特定的算法推荐，并发布JumpStart资源套件（包含全部策略、模型分数与超参数、强基线、代码及可扩展网站），以提升离线策略学习研究的可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable progress in offline policy learning depends on careful reporting, well-tuned baselines, and evaluation across diverse conditions. Prior work has shown that results can be sensitive to reporting choices, hyperparameter tuning, and dataset properties, but these sources of variability have not been systematically investigated together at the scale needed to understand how they shape conclusions. To address this gap, we present a large-scale empirical study of offline reinforcement and imitation learning, training over 160,000 policies across 114 datasets. At this scale, no algorithm dominates: aggregate performance among the strongest methods is often close, but the leaders differ substantially across environments. We find that proper hyperparameter tuning frequently reshuffles perceived algorithm rankings and that benchmark composition can produce conflicting conclusions. We also study hyperparameter sensitivity and transfer across environments, identifying a simple strategy for deriving strong default configurations. We use our findings to develop a dataset-conditioned recommender that provides task-specific algorithm recommendations for practitioners. Finally, we release JumpStart: a resource suite containing every trained policy, per-model scores and hyperparameters, strong baselines across all environments, training and evaluation code, and an extensible website for retrieving, analyzing, and contributing results. Together, these resources aim to make offline policy-learning research more reliable and enable future work beyond the scope of this study.

</details>


### [91] [HarnessBandit: Joint Learnability-Transferability Scheduling for Multi-Harness Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13739)
*Hongliang Wei,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Zhengxi Liu,Rongkun Xue,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Debin Zhao,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessBandit，一种在线调度器，在每一步优化器更新时从多个 harness 中选择一个进行 GRPO 训练，利用可学习性与可迁移性信号指导选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体通过不同 harness（系统提示、工具模式、控制循环、轨迹格式各异）部署，同一模型表现不一，因此对 harness 变化的鲁棒性很重要。多 harness 联合训练可提升鲁棒性，但引入调度问题：每步应选择当前能提供有用学习信号、同时其更新又能惠及其余 harness 的那个 harness。

Method: HarnessBandit 在每个优化步骤选择一个 harness。GRPO 更新后观测两个信号：可学习性（批次上的平均绝对优势）与可迁移性（当前 harness 的低维梯度草图与其余 harness 指数移动平均之间的余弦相似度）。两信号经池化滑动窗口 min-max 归一化后融合，并以访问依赖的奖励项和显式探索下限进行采样。

Result: 在 ClawGym 上跨六个 harness 训练 Qwen3.5-2B，并在 PinchBench（留出任务、同分布 OpenClaw）与 ClawEval（留出任务与 harness）上评估。HarnessBandit 在两个基准上均优于混合批次的多 harness 训练，训练诊断显示可学习性与可迁移性提供互补且动态演化的信号。

Conclusion: 将多 harness 训练形式化为调度问题，并以 bandit 式在线调度融合可学习性与可迁移性信号，可有效提升智能体对 harness 变化的鲁棒性与跨 harness 泛化能力。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly deployed through diverse harnesses that differ in system prompts, tool schemas, control loops, and trajectory formats. The same model can perform unevenly across these interfaces, making robustness to harness variation an important objective. A natural approach is to train a shared policy through multiple harnesses, but doing so introduces a scheduling problem: each training step should favor a harness that currently provides a useful learning signal while also producing an update that benefits the other harnesses. We develop HarnessBandit, an online scheduler that selects one harness per optimizer step. After a group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) update, it observes learnability -- the mean absolute advantage on the batch -- and transferability -- the cosine between a low-dimensional gradient sketch of the current harness and exponential moving averages of the remaining harnesses. The two signals are fused after pooled sliding-window min-max normalization and sampled with a visit-dependent bonus and an explicit exploration floor. We train Qwen3.5-2B across six harnesses on ClawGym and evaluate on PinchBench (held-out tasks, in-distribution OpenClaw) and ClawEval (held-out tasks and harness). HarnessBandit improves over mixed-batch multi-harness training on both benchmarks, while training diagnostics indicate that learnability and transferability provide distinct, evolving signals.

</details>


### [92] [Training Specialist Models without Reasoning Trajectories for Domain Expert Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13770)
*Yilei Tu,Zihao Li,Shaoxiong Ji,Jörg Tiedemann,Fei Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当专家模型仅用问答对训练、缺少显式推理监督时，其生成的推理轨迹由优化过程隐式选择的潜在轨迹分布决定，并可通过学生蒸馏作为探针被观测和调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家蒸馏通常依赖教师生成的推理轨迹来传递领域知识，但当专家模型只接受问答对训练、没有显式推理监督时，其生成轨迹的支配因素尚不清楚。作者希望弄清这种"隐式监督"的来源及其对下游学生模型的影响。

Method: 将学生蒸馏用作一种"不可知探针"，因为学生不继承专家的参数化或优化约束，只继承采样出的轨迹本身。通过27组专家-学生配对进行实证分析，并显式控制专家的分布漂移，观察其对教师和学生的影响。

Result: 27组专家-学生配对的专业化-泛化轮廓呈现极强的相关性；显式控制专家分布漂移可系统性地移动教师和学生的领域精度与通用能力保持之间的权衡；在化学、物理和多语言场景下，蒸馏学生系统性地反映专家诱导的轮廓，甚至跨越不同模型家族。

Conclusion: 研究建立了对专家训练的新视角：在缺少黄金推理时，调优选择直接控制传递给下游模型的潜在监督信号。

Abstract: Specialist distillation effectively transfers domain expertise to student models via teacher-generated reasoning trajectories. However, when these specialists are trained solely on question--answer pairs without explicit reasoning supervision, what governs the trajectories they generate? In this work, we show that specialist optimization implicitly selects from this latent trajectory space. To isolate and observe this latent distribution, we leverage student distillation not as a downstream goal, but as an agnostic probe---since students inherit no parameterization or optimization constraints from the specialist, inheriting only the sampled trajectories themselves. Through this probe, our empirical analysis unveils a tight governing relationship: across 27 specialist--student pairings, their specialization--generalization profiles correlate exceptionally strongly. Crucially, explicitly controlling the specialist's distributional drift systematically shifts both the teacher and its distilled student along a controllable trade-off between domain precision and general-capability retention. Across chemistry, physics, and multilingual settings, distilled students systematically reflect these specialist-induced profiles, even across divergent model families. Our findings establish a new view of specialist training: when gold reasoning is absent, tuning choices directly control the latent supervision passed to downstream models.

</details>


### [93] [Does Reasoning Improve Psychological Depth in Large Language Models? It Depends on Who's Judging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13773)
*Ruichen Zheng,Yihe Wang,Fabrice Y Harel-Canada,Sara Khosravi,Zeynep Senahan Yildiz,Amit Sahai,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以短篇小说的"心理深度"评测为例，揭示LLM裁判在开发集上与人类评分相关性良好（ρ=0.646），却在分布偏移的真实对比场景中失效：人类读者偏好分歧近乎随机（α=0.070），而LLM裁判却一致偏向推理模型，并与句子长度、词汇多样性等表层特征相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge 被广泛用于开放式生成评分，但其在开发集上与人类评分的相关性未必能保证在真实部署场景下的测量效度，尤其是当输出质量接近、人类偏好本身高度主观时。作者希望系统研究这一失效模式。

Method: 以短篇小说的"心理深度"为研究载体。基线：在原始标量 Psychological Depth Scale 数据集上筛选出的 7 位人类读者与一个 LLM 裁判集成（ρ=0.646）。测试：对 60 组盲测、提示词匹配的故事对（GPT-5 vs GPT-4o、DeepSeek-R1 vs DeepSeek-V3）进行人类与裁判评估，并分析读者间一致性、读者内一致性、权重模式，以及裁判分数与表层文本特征（句长、词汇多样性）的关联。

Result: 人类偏好未显示普遍的推理优势：GPT-5 略优于 GPT-4o（60.0–62.9%），DeepSeek-R1 反而落后于 V3（42.9%）；读者间一致性接近随机（Krippendorff's α=0.070），但读者内一致性与重复出现的权重模式表明这是结构性异质性而非随机作答。LLM 裁判则相反：在 89.0% 的维度级比较中、以及 60 组中 59 组的聚合 PDS 上偏向推理输出，且在全部五种评估器配置下保持一致；其分数与句长、词汇多样性等表层特征相关。

Conclusion: 开发集上的表现不足以作为部署效度的证据，尤其在分布偏移时；点估计式的 LLM 裁判会掩盖主观人类评价中的异质性。研究呼吁重新审视 LLM-as-a-Judge 的有效性主张。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge evaluators are increasingly used to score open-ended generation, yet a judge's correlation with human ratings on its development set may not guarantee valid measurement when outputs are closely matched and human preferences are subjective. We study this failure mode through psychological depth in short stories. Seven human readers and an LLM-judge ensemble selected on the original scalar Psychological Depth Scale dataset ($ρ= 0.646$) evaluated 60 blinded, prompt-matched story pairs from GPT-5 vs.\ GPT-4o and DeepSeek-R1 vs.\ DeepSeek-V3. Human preferences showed no universal reasoning advantage: GPT-5 was modestly preferred over GPT-4o (60.0--62.9\%), whereas DeepSeek-R1 trailed V3 (42.9\%), and inter-reader agreement was near chance (Krippendorff's $α= 0.070$), with within-reader consistency and recurring weighting patterns suggesting structured heterogeneity rather than random responding. The judge, by contrast, favored reasoning outputs in 89.0\% of dimension-level comparisons and 59 of 60 pairs on aggregate PDS, uniformly across all five evaluator configurations, and its scores were associated with surface features such as sentence length and lexical diversity. These results suggest that development-set performance is insufficient evidence for deployment validity on a shifted distribution, and that point-estimate judges can obscure the heterogeneity in subjective human evaluation.

</details>


### [94] [PPDL: A Real-world Industrial User Retention Ratio Forecasting Framework Integrating Physical Priors with Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13789)
*Zibo Zhao,Zhengxiong Guan,Chaoli Zhang,Linyuan Geng,Xuanbing Zhu,Zhonglong Zheng,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PPDL框架，融合物理先验与深度学习，用于大规模短视频平台渠道级用户留存率预测，工业级数据上优于现有线上方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多渠道付费获客中，早期准确预测渠道级用户留存对预算分配至关重要。留存曲线具有高流失转长期稳定、季节性规律波动和强序列自相关等时间模式，适合时序预测；但在大规模短视频平台面临渠道异质性强、全局衰减后饱和趋势显著、回看窗口短三大挑战。

Method: PPDL包含三部分：1）趋势-残差分解，趋势用Weibull分布建模、参数由MLP学习；2）残差部分在深度学习主干上设计辅助嵌入模块以保持渠道身份感知；3）设计多尺度趋势惩罚损失函数，增强模型对趋势的敏感性。

Result: 在工业级数据集（三个应用，平均每个30+渠道）上进行全面实验，PPDL在不同主干网络上均取得提升，并显著优于现有线上方案。

Conclusion: 融合物理先验（Weibull趋势）与深度学习的PPDL能有效应对渠道异质性、衰减饱和趋势与短回看窗口问题，具备工业级实用价值。

Abstract: In multi-channel paid user acquisition, early and accurate prediction of user retention at the channel level is crucial for optimizing budget allocation. User retention curves display a pronounced temporal pattern: an initial period of high churn transitions into long-term stability. This pattern is further characterized by regular fluctuations attributable to seasonality and exhibits high serial autocorrelation. These intrinsic properties make such curves highly suitable for analysis within a time-series forecasting framework. However, forecasting user retention ratio for large-scale short-video platform faces three major challenges: significant heterogeneity across channels, pronounced global trend of decay followed by saturation, and short look-back windows. To address these challenges, we propose PPDL, a novel forecasting framework that integrates physical priors with deep learning. We first introduce a trend-residual decomposition component. The trend is modeled using the Weibull distribution, whose parameters are learned via a Multilayer Perceptron (MLP). Secondly, for the residual component, we design an auxiliary embedding module on top of a deep learning backbone to maintain the channel identity awareness. Finally, to enhance the model's sensitivity to trends, we design a Multiscale Trend-penalized loss function. The proposed approach PPDL is validated through comprehensive experiments on industrial-scale datasets, covering three applications with an average of 30+ channels each. Experimental results show that PPDL achieves improvements across different backbones and significantly outperforms existing online solutions.

</details>


### [95] [Benchmarking Optimizers to Solve Inverse Problems with Differentiable Physics Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13819)
*Xiang Chen,Huanhuan Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了12个可微物理模拟器，覆盖离散力学、连续力学、原子模拟、渲染与半经验模型，并设计参数辨识、逆设计与最优控制三类逆问题，系统对比一阶、近似二阶及全局优化器的性能，为可微编程中优化器的选择与设计提供洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微物理模拟器结合了严格物理正确性与自动微分的高效性，有望革新科学发现与工程设计，但当前该范式在优化性能上存在问题，缺乏对不同优化器在各类逆问题上表现的基准评测。

Method: 构建12个可微物理模拟器，涵盖离散力学、连续力学、原子模拟、渲染和半经验物理模型；基于这些模拟器设计对应的逆问题，分为参数辨识、逆设计和最优控制三类；通过大量实验比较常规一阶方法、近似二阶方法及全局优化器的性能。

Result: 在三类逆问题上获得了不同优化器的性能对比结果，并据此分析得出了关于如何为可微编程选择与设计优化器的见解。

Conclusion: 该基准评测可为开发更有效的优化器提供启发，并进一步推动可微编程在科学与工程领域的应用。
