Abstract: Decentralized training enables large-model training over low-end GPUs and internet-grade connections, but communication along both data-parallel and pipeline-parallel axes becomes the primary bottleneck. We study post-pretraining adaptation in this setting. We propose an asynchronous two-circuit system: a fast compressed training circuit drives throughput using activation masking for pipeline-parallel (PP) transfer and compressed data-parallel (DP) synchronization, while a slow anchor circuit runs occasional unmasked forward--backward passes off the critical path. Then, we introduce a spectral correction optimizer that uses these delayed anchor priors to denoise masked gradients without blocking the fast stream. Although prior work has found aggressive activation compression unreliable, we show that masking supports post-pretraining adaptation at high compression rates when anchored this way. Pipeline-parallel compression alone yields up to a $9\times$ throughput gain, and combining it with data-parallel compression increases beyond $40\times$ over internet-grade $\sim 200$Mbps connections, while matching dense uncompressed performance across domain adaptation and continual pretraining.

</details>


### [131] [Multimodal deep learning from spectra for small-molecule structure identification: enhancing robustness with mixed-condition training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14360)
*Bowen Gao,Lei Zhu,Yiying Wang,Wenjie Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将光谱学与化学领域知识引入混合条件训练，结合专家混合（MoE）融合与可复现评测协议，显著提升小分子候选结构重排名的鲁棒性与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际小分子结构鉴定中，多模态光谱证据常出现缺失、退化或失配的情况，给多模态模型带来严峻挑战，需要提升模型在谱图可用性、质量与一致性变化下的鲁棒性。

Method: 将领域知识融入混合条件训练以用于候选结构重排名；设计针对各光谱模态的扰动与化学启发的谱图替换，覆盖不同可用性、质量与一致性条件；采用 MoE 融合并与朴素拼接对比，通过 2×2 因子实验在 30 种预设条件下评测 MSSD 模拟的 MS、IR、1H 与 13C NMR 数据，每样本最多 128 个硬候选结构。

Result: 混合条件训练在两种架构下均带来主要增益。MoE 下 MRR 由 0.9203 升至 0.9763（相对提升 6.08%），R@1 由 89.50% 升至 96.36%（提升 6.86 个百分点，相对提升 7.67%）。仅 IR 与仅 MS/MS 的 MRR 分别由 0.4337 升至 0.9307、由 0.3711 升至 0.8575，达到基线的 2.15 倍与 2.31 倍，而完整输入性能仍保持较高。

Conclusion: 将领域知识整合进训练条件设计可有效提升结构重排名的鲁棒性，且在混合条件训练下 MoE 融合还能带来额外增益。

Abstract: In practical molecular characterization, small-molecule structure identification benefits from complementary spectroscopic evidence, but missing, degraded, or mismatched spectra challenge multimodal models. Herein, we incorporate domain knowledge from spectroscopy and chemistry into mixed-condition training for candidate structure reranking, using a reproducible evaluation protocol and mixture-of-experts (MoE) fusion. The protocol incorporates perturbations tailored to each spectroscopic modality and chemically informed spectrum replacements to cover variations in spectral availability, quality, and consistency. A total of 79,462 test samples were evaluated across 30 predefined conditions using simulated spectra from the Multimodal Spectroscopic Dataset (MSSD) for mass spectrometry (MS), infrared (IR) spectroscopy, and 1H and 13C nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR), with up to 128 hard candidate structures per sample. A controlled two-by-two factorial comparison of complete-input training versus mixed-condition training and vanilla concatenation versus MoE fusion, with matched evaluation conditions, showed that mixed-condition training provided the main gains in both architectures. For MoE, mean reciprocal rank (MRR), averaged equally across conditions, increased from 0.9203 to 0.9763, a relative increase of 6.08%. Recall at rank 1 (R@1), averaged over the same conditions, increased from 89.50% to 96.36%, an increase of 6.86 percentage points and a relative increase of 7.67%. IR-only and MS/MS-only MRR increased from 0.4337 to 0.9307 and from 0.3711 to 0.8575, reaching 2.15 and 2.31 times their respective baseline values, while complete-input performance remained high. These results support integrating domain knowledge into training-condition design to improve robustness, with further gains from MoE under mixed-condition training.

</details>


### [132] [Towards Identifying the Dataset Biases Causing Phantom Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14449)
*Jonas Jürß,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于Sentence BERT嵌入的简单特征可以识别学生模型中隐藏偏见的主题，已知教师模型时MCC达0.83，未知时达0.46，且不同教师模型表达同一偏见所用词汇不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，教师模型能通过过滤掉所有显式偏见指涉的数据集将偏见传递给学生，且即使知道要找什么偏见，数据层防御也无法可靠地移除或检测该偏见。作者希望揭示这些偏见隐藏的痕迹。

Method: 使用基于Sentence BERT嵌入构建的简单签名（signature）来识别偏见主题，分别在已知攻击者所用教师模型和未知教师模型两种情况下进行评估。

Result: 在已知教师模型时，该签名识别偏见主题的Matthews相关系数（MCC）为0.83；在未知教师模型时为0.46。此外观察到不同教师模型似乎通过不同词汇表达同一偏见。

Conclusion: 偏见转移会在学生模型中留下可被Sentence BERT嵌入签名检测到的隐藏痕迹，但检测效果依赖于是否已知教师模型；不同教师模型表达同一偏见的词汇存在差异。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that a teacher model can transfer a bias to a student through a dataset from which every explicit reference to that bias has been filtered out, and that no data-level defense reliably removes or detects it even when knowing what bias to look for. Aiming to shed light on the hidden traces of these biases, we show that a simple signature based on Sentence BERT embeddings can identify the topic of such a bias with a Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.83 if the teacher model used by the attacker is known and 0.46 if it is not. Additionally, we observe that different teacher models appear to express the same bias through different vocabulary.

</details>


### [133] [Pathwise Individual Rationality in Federated Learning: A Mechanism-Architecture Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14591)
*Amin Meghrazi,Srinivasan Parthasarathy,Andrew Perrault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习中的个体理性问题，指出客户端在多轮训练中可能长期低于本地训练基线，提出短时参与保证与个性化模型评估相结合的新设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习参与有成本，客户端需在隐私、通信、计算成本与模型收益间权衡。论文以个体理性（IR）相对于自给自足（autarky）为设计目标，即联邦提供的效用不应差于本地训练，并关注逐轮路径性能而非仅渐近均衡保证。

Method: 在不同客户端数据分布异质性模型下，分析FL的逐轮累积剩余下界；证明对每轮贡献设上限的补救措施会适得其反；提出结合短期参与保证与个性化模型评估的新设计，并给出理论依据与实证验证。

Result: 客户端可能连续数百轮低于本地训练基线；限制每轮贡献会导致学习崩溃，即使在低至中等异质性下也如此；新设计可在不损害整体性能的情况下避免短期损失，中等异质性下有效，严重异质性下相较本地基线表现有前景但精度有所下降。

Conclusion: 联邦学习需在路径层面保障个体理性，简单限制贡献不可行；结合短期参与保证与个性化模型评估的新设计能在保持公平激励的同时改善客户端短期收益。

Abstract: Participation in federated learning (FL) comes at a cost. Clients trade off privacy, communication, and compute costs for potentially greater gains in model efficacy. This paper explores this tradeoff under the aegis of individual rationality (IR) versus autarky, the basic game-theoretic requirement that the federation provide utility no worse than local training. Using the above as the design target, we examine pathwise performance of FL, as a per-round bound on cumulative surplus, not just as an asymptotic equilibrium guarantee under different models of client data distribution heterogeneity. Along this path, clients can remain below their local-training baseline for hundreds of rounds. The natural remedy is to cap each client's per-round contribution so that this shortfall stays bounded, and we prove that it backfires, collapsing learning even at low-to-modest heterogeneity.
  We then propose a novel design that combines short-term participation guarantees with personalized model evaluation, while maintaining fair incentives. We provide a theoretical basis for this new approach and empirically demonstrate that clients can avoid short-term losses without harming overall performance, even under moderate data distribution heterogeneity; under severe heterogeneity, the design shows promising outcomes for clients compared to their local baseline at some cost in accuracy.

</details>


### [134] [Diagnosing Temporal Misalignment in Multichannel Time-Series Classification with Minimum Description Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14595)
*Sebastian Buschjäger,Michael Frichert,Daniel Kuhe,Jian-Jia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最小描述长度（MDL）的、与分类器和标签无关的诊断方法，用于检测多通道时间序列中的时序错位，无需重训练或对齐参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多通道时间序列分类通常假设传感器流已同步，但延迟、时钟漂移和预处理会引入相对延迟。现有同步方案多为硬件特定、难以回溯应用，导致错位问题常被忽视、分类性能次优。

Method: 对传感器组施加候选时间偏移，度量某组能否通过其余通道的表示被高效编码：码长升高说明偏移破坏了共享时序结构，码长最小值对应数据最支持的配准。该方法与分类器和标签无关，无需重训练或可信对齐参考，可用于训练和部署数据。

Result: 在两个受控合成任务和九个真实数据集上，该指标能揭示对齐结构，并在部署漂移下恢复精度。全数据集审计在FordChallenge、Opportunity、PAMAP2、UCIActivity等基准中发现稳定的非零MDL最优值，提示存在传统评估未暴露的系统性偏移。

Conclusion: 该方法为时间序列学习流程中检测、理解和校正时序错位提供了通用工具，可用于训练与部署阶段的对齐审计。

Abstract: Multichannel time-series classification commonly assumes synchronized sensor streams, although latency, clock drift, and preprocessing can introduce relative delays during data collection or after deployment. Existing synchronization solutions are often hardware-specific and difficult to apply retrospectively. Consequently, synchronization problems may remain undetected while classification performance is suboptimal. We introduce a classifier- and label-free diagnostic based on minimum description length (MDL). Our method applies candidate temporal shifts to sensor groups and measures how efficiently one group can be encoded through a representation of the remaining channels. An increased codelength indicates that the shift destroys shared temporal structure, whereas the minimum identifies the alignment most strongly supported by the data. Unlike learned synchronization methods, the diagnostic requires neither retraining nor a trusted aligned reference and can therefore test both training and deployment data for misalignments. Experiments on two controlled synthetic tasks and nine real-world datasets show that the metric exposes alignment structure and can recover accuracy under induced deployment drift. A whole-dataset audit further identifies stable nonzero MDL optima in established benchmarks including FordChallenge, Opportunity, PAMAP2, and UCIActivity, revealing potential systematic offsets that conventional model evaluation does not expose. Our method thus provides a general-purpose tool for detecting, understanding, and correcting temporal misalignment throughout the time-series learning pipeline. Our code is available under https://github.com/sbuschjaeger/mdl-temporal-misalignment.

</details>


### [135] [Know When to Stop, Where to Restart: Accelerating Multi-Turn Agentic On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14636)
*Zhiyu Gui,Kexin Huang,Jia Guo,Junkang Wu,Zihao Wang,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou,Jiancan Wu,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STRIDE加速on-policy蒸馏：通过自适应早停与前缀缓存重启，在τ²-bench上以3.73×加速匹敌全轨迹OPD并超越30B教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation依赖昂贵的自回归学生采样，在多轮智能体场景下成本高、扩展性差。现有加速方法按固定离线预算截断或迁移监督信号，忽视了教师信号可靠性在轨迹内与轨迹间的显著变化。

Method: 基于τ²-bench的实证分析发现：有效监督集中于每轮前缀，且教师认可损失的跨轮中断在时间上锁定于学生首个错误动作而非逐轮累积。据此提出STRIDE，包含：自适应早停（累积教师对数概率低于OOD阈值时终止采样）与前缀缓冲（缓存高质量前缀并在最弱正确轮处重启生成），二者共同形成逐步覆盖后续轮次的数据驱动课程。

Result: τ²-bench零售任务上，方法匹配全轨迹OPD并在3.73×加速下超越30B教师，在2.34×加速下超越基线本身；跨域多教师训练保持4.51×加速。泛化测试中，AIME 2025以5.10×加速、AIME 2024以3.08×加速优于全OPD，而两种固定预算截断基线在两评测平均下均低于全OPD。

Conclusion: 教师信号可靠性的结构化变化可被数据驱动地利用，STRIDE在不损失甚至提升蒸馏效果的同时实现显著加速，具备跨域与跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become a standard approach for transferring capabilities from large teachers to compact students. Its cost, however, is dominated by autoregressive student rollouts and scales poorly in multi-turn agentic settings. Existing acceleration methods truncate or relocate the supervision signal according to fixed, offline budgets, despite substantial variation in teacher-signal reliability both within and across trajectories. Our empirical analysis on $τ^2$-bench reveals a clear structure in this variation: informative supervision is concentrated in the prefix of each turn, and, most importantly for multi-turn agentic training, the cross-turn loss of teacher endorsement is temporally locked to the student's first erroneous action rather than accumulating gradually over turns. Building on these findings, we propose STRIDE (Stop-and-Restart on-policy Distillation acceleration), which combines two complementary techniques: adaptive early stopping, which terminates a rollout once the cumulative teacher log-probability falls below an out-of-distribution threshold, and a prefix buffer, which caches high-quality prefixes and restarts generation at the weakest correct turn. Together, these mechanisms induce a data-driven curriculum that progressively extends coverage to later turns. On $τ^2$-bench retail, our method matches full-trajectory OPD and exceeds the 30B teacher at a $3.73\times$ speedup, surpasses the baseline itself at $2.34\times$, and retains a $4.51\times$ speedup under cross-domain multi-teacher training. As a supplementary generalization test beyond the agentic setting, STRIDE outperforms full OPD on AIME 2025 at a $5.10\times$ speedup and on AIME 2024 at a $3.08\times$ speedup; averaged across the two evaluations, both fixed-budget truncation baselines remain below full OPD.

</details>


### [136] [WaterKron and FlipFlop Hessian: Information-Theoretically Grounded Quantization with Kronecker-factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14706)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 WaterKron，用于训练后量化中 Kronecker 分解 Hessian 近似的最优选择，通过 Kronecker-Hessian 失配因子 Φ 给出选择准则，导出 FlipFlop Hessian。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后量化中常用 Kronecker 分解近似 Hessian，但如何选取该近似的两个因子缺乏理论依据，本文旨在为这一选择提供率失真角度的解释与准则。

Method: 结合双面 GPTQ、行/列相关 waterfilling 尺度与熵编码构成 WaterKron；针对完整 Hessian 推导高码率失真，引入显式失配因子 Φ；最小化 Φ 转化为高斯协方差拟合问题，用经典 "flip-flop" 迭代求解，得到 FlipFlop Hessian。

Result: 实验表明，FlipFlop Hessian 在 KL 散度与困惑度上一致优于仅输入、边际以及基于 Frobenius 的 Hessian 选择。

Conclusion: Φ 量化了 Kronecker Hessian 近似带来的渐近失真惩罚，为量化中选择最优因子提供了准则，并从率失真角度论证了 FlipFlop Hessian 的合理性。

Abstract: How should a Kronecker-factored Hessian approximation be chosen for post-training quantization? We address this question through WaterKron, which combines two-sided GPTQ with row- and column-dependent waterfilling scales and entropy coding. We derive its high-rate distortion with respect to the full Hessian using an explicit Kronecker-Hessian mismatch factor $Φ$. This factor quantifies the asymptotic distortion penalty due to the Kronecker Hessian approximation and provides a criterion for selecting the factors optimally. Minimizing $Φ$ leads to a Gaussian covariance-fitting problem with classical ``flip-flop'' updates. We thus give a rate-distortion justification for using the resulting FlipFlop Hessian in quantization. We evaluate it empirically, finding that the FlipFlop Hessian consistently improves KL divergence and perplexity over input-only, marginal, and Frobenius-based Hessian choices.

</details>


### [137] [WaVeFuse: Regime-Adaptive Equity Index Forecasting via Channel-Wise Wavelet Denoising and Vertical Attention Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14733)
*Aashish Bohra,Vivek Vijay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WaVeFuse是一个用于股指预测的双分支混合深度学习框架，通过小波去噪、因果通道连续小波变换和多尺度分支自适应融合，解决噪声传播、频率混杂和静态融合三大问题，实现高精度、稳健且计算高效的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合深度学习股指预测方法存在三大局限：一是OHLCV噪声传播至衍生技术指标（TIs）；二是通道不区分的多尺度分解混杂了异质频率特征；三是静态多分支融合无法适应市场状态切换。论文旨在通过统一的双分支架构克服这些问题。

Method: 采用对称小波（Symlet-4，2级，MAD软阈值）对OHLCV去噪以抑制微观结构噪声；基于去噪价格计算七个低滞后技术指标，并通过因果通道连续小波变换（Morlet，32尺度）编码为逐时间步的尺度-空间矩阵。CNN-BiLSTM分支捕捉时间动态，双层Transformer（4头，dk∈{16,32}）建模尺度间频谱依赖；两者的表示由2-token softmax门控的垂直注意力融合（VAF）动态重加权，以适应市场状态变化。

Result: 在KOSPI、DAX、NYSE综合指数和Russell 2000（2010-2023）上以步进式验证（WFV）评估，WaVeFuse取得R²=0.81-0.96、方向准确率70.5-78.3%；在12个数据集-时期配置上MAE优于七个先进模型8.9-20.2%；Diebold-Mariano统计量（4.62-10.38，p<0.001）证实其优于调优的XGBoost基准。消融实验验证了各组件贡献。含10基点交易成本的现实回测中，方向策略四市场平均夏普比率3.69，COVID-19崩盘期间最大回撤仅7.5%。模型仅152k参数（0.68MB），GPU推理低于1.3ms。

Conclusion: WaVeFuse提供了一个计算高效、适应市场状态的稳健框架，适合研究与决策支持部署，并在多项指标上显著超越现有先进方法。

Abstract: Hybrid Deep Learning for equity index forecasting is limited by three problems: propagation of OHLCV noise into derived technical indicators (TIs), channel-indiscriminate multi-scale decomposition that conflates heterogeneous frequency signatures, and static multi-branch fusion that cannot adapt to market regime shifts. WaVeFuse addresses these limitations through a unified dual-branch architecture. Symlet-4 wavelet denoising (level 2, MAD soft threshold) suppresses microstructure noise in OHLCV. Seven low-lag TIs computed from denoised prices are encoded by a causal channel-wise continuous wavelet transform (Morlet, 32 scales) into a per-timestep scale-space matrix. A CNN-BiLSTM branch captures temporal dynamics, while a dual-layer Transformer (heads=4, dk in {16, 32}) models inter-scale spectral dependencies, and their representations are integrated by a 2-token softmax gate Vertical Attention Fusion (VAF) that dynamically reweights branches as market regimes shift. Evaluated under walk-forward validation (WFV) on KOSPI, DAX, NYSE Composite, and Russell 2000 (2010-2023), WaVeFuse achieves R2 = 0.81-0.96 and directional accuracy 70.5-78.3%. It outperforms seven state-of-the-art models by 8.9-20.2% MAE across twelve dataset-period configurations. Diebold-Mariano statistics (4.62-10.38, p<0.001) confirm superiority over a well-tuned XGBoost benchmark across four indices. Ablation verifies component-wise contributions. Under realistic backtesting with 10 basis point transaction costs, WaVeFuse's directional strategy achieves a mean Sharpe ratio of 3.69 across four markets and limits maximum drawdown to 7.5% during the COVID-19 crash. With 152k parameters (0.68MB) and sub-1.3ms GPU inference, WaVeFuse delivers a computationally efficient, regime-robust framework suitable for research and decision-support deployment.

</details>


### [138] [Privacy Preserving Gossip Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14778)
*Erkan Bayram,Mohamed-Ali Belabbas,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种去中心化隐私保护学习算法，每个智能体持有单一样本与共享模型，样本顺序学习，更新时需保持已学样本的端点映射并保护隐私；结合TwF、极小极大最优控制与私有push-sum gossip实现三方角色下的隐私与收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化学习中，各智能体仅持有单个私有样本并共享一个模型，顺序学习时面临两大挑战：一是新样本更新不能破坏已学样本的映射（灾难性遗忘），二是需在更新与聚合过程中保护各智能体的私有数据。现有方法难以同时兼顾遗忘抑制、隐私保障与去中心化收敛性。

Method: 基于Tuning without Forgetting（TwF）保持已学映射，并证明当受保护智能体集合中存在同类标签样本时TwF对学习者提供不可区分性保证；针对教师角色，将差分隐私噪声建模为最坏情况扰动，构造极小极大最优控制问题，在维持梯度隐私水平的同时避免性能损失；针对受保护智能体，本地计算投影并通过私有push-sum gossip协议进行聚合。

Result: 证明了去中心化gossip算法的几何收敛性，以及TwF中分布式投影的几何收敛性；同时给出TwF在特定条件下对学习者的不可区分性保证，并通过极小极大控制实现隐私与性能的兼顾。

Conclusion: 该工作将遗忘抑制、差分隐私与去中心化gossip统一在一个框架内，通过为学习者、教师、受保护智能体分别设计机制，实现了隐私保护下的顺序学习，并提供了收敛性与隐私性的理论保证。

Abstract: We propose a decentralized privacy-preserving learning algorithm in which each agent holds a single private sample and a shared model. Samples are learned sequentially, and each update must preserve the endpoint mappings at previously learned samples while protecting private data. This gives each agent three roles: (i) a learner that updates the model parameters, (ii) a teacher whose sample is learned at the current iteration, and (iii) a protected agent whose sample has already been learned. We build on Tuning without Forgetting (TwF) method to preserve previously learned mappings and show that TwF provides an indistinguishability guarantee for the learner whenever the set of protected agents contains another sample with the same label. For the teacher, we formulate a minimax optimal control problem that models the differential privacy noise as a worst-case disturbance to prevent performance loss while maintaining the same level of privacy for the gradient. For the protected agents, we compute the projections locally and aggregate them using a private push-sum gossip protocol. We prove geometric convergence of the decentralized gossip algorithm and of the distributed projection for TwF.

</details>


### [139] [Decision-Oriented Uncertainty Quantification for Risk Control in Earth System Spatiotemporal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14821)
*Ji Lu,Huiran Duan,Bo Zhao,Xianglong Wang,Yiru Fang,Kuo Yang,Xiaoqin Feng,Jianping Gou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向决策的地球系统时空基础模型不确定性量化框架，将预测分布经决策风险适配器映射为动作条件风险，并通过效用感知校准在下游决策损失层面保证可靠性，显著降低决策后悔与漏报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球系统建模正从任务专用预测器转向具有通用时空表征能力的基础模型，可联合编码动态地球场、外部强迫和静态地理背景进行多步预测。但在极端天气预警、防洪调度、可再生能源调度和应急资源分配等高影响应用中，仅有点预测或统计校准区间是不够的；真正重要的是能否在特定动作、损失函数和风险偏好下，把预测不确定性转化为可靠的决策风险。

Method: 提出决策导向的不确定性量化框架：1）生成未来状态的预测分布；2）设计决策风险适配器，将预测样本、决策上下文与效用函数映射为动作条件风险；3）引入效用感知校准模块，在下游决策损失层面而非仅预测值层面强制可靠性；4）用校准后的风险选择预警、调度、巡检或资源分配动作。

Result: 相比最强基线：决策后悔降低18.7%，漏报率从14.2%降至9.1%，期望效用提升11.6%，同时保持90.4%的预测覆盖率，决策校准误差从0.083降至0.047。

Conclusion: 面向决策的不确定性量化能够提升地球系统基础模型在风险敏感应用中的鲁棒性与实际运行价值。

Abstract: Earth system modeling is shifting from task-specific predictors toward foundation models with general spatiotemporal representation capabilities. Although these models can jointly encode dynamic Earth fields, external forcings, and static geographic context for multistep forecasting, accurate point predictions or statistically calibrated intervals alone are insufficient for high-impact applications such as extremeweather warning, flood control, renewable-energy dispatch, and emergency resource allocation. What matters in practice is whether predictive uncertainty can be translated into reliable decision risk under specific actions, loss functions, and risk preferences. We propose a decision-oriented uncertainty quantification framework for Earth system spatiotemporal foundation models. The framework produces predictive distributions of future states and uses a decision risk adapter to map forecast samples, decision context, and utility functions into action-conditional risks. A utility-aware calibration module further enforces reliability at the downstream decision-loss level rather than only at the forecast-value level. Calibrated risks are then used to select warning, dispatch, inspection, or resource-allocation actions. Compared with the strongest baseline, the proposed method reduces decision regret by 18.7%, lowers the missed-event rate from 14.2% to 9.1%, and improves expected utility by 11.6%, while maintaining 90.4% predictive coverage and reducing decision calibration error from 0.083 to 0.047. These results suggest that decision-oriented uncertainty quantification can improve the robustness and operational value of Earth system foundation models in risk-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [140] [Semantic Fibers and Cross-Gram Interference: A Calculus of Safety Drift in Overcomplete Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14861)
*Mohammed Ahnouch,Lotfi Elaachack*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究跨语言安全失效现象，即模型拒绝英文有害请求却服从其忠实翻译。作者通过等价关系、商表示与线性代数刻画，提出内在校准暴露度量（杠杆对偶 χ²=1/ℓ−1），将观察到的安全漂移分为三种可诊断情形，并可扩展到锥值安全头，且给出线性控制界面的诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署中的语言模型存在跨语言安全漏洞：英文有害请求被拒绝，但其忠实翻译却被服从。仅凭输出行为无法可靠刻画这一失效，因此需要一个形式化、可解释且可诊断的框架来识别并区分不同类型的跨语言安全漂移。

Method: 作者将现象形式化为经审计的等价关系，在给定商、表示、度量、特征字典、评分头、阈值与对比模型下，证明安全漂移具有精确的线性代数刻画：漂移是纤维内对比的跨Gram泛函，其最坏容许值为支撑函数，边界不变性由零化子条件刻画。进而引入受杠杆对偶 χ²=1/ℓ−1 支配的内在校准暴露度量，把观察漂移分为三种诊断情形，方法也扩展到锥值安全头。

Result: 暴露度量将观察到的漂移分为三类：可通过重校准移除的读取器故障、因病态而不可靠的精确修正、以及读取层干预无法移除的表示层碰撞。相同观察暴露可能导致根本不同的修复结论。框架可扩展到锥值安全头。一个非绑定顺序交换恒等式为线性控制界面提供诊断，其校准状态残差能预测未见状态与目标上的三控制组合误差，中位Spearman相关为0.964，而静态跨Gram基线仅为0.269。

Conclusion: 论文为跨语言安全失效提供了严格的线性代数刻画与内在校准暴露度量，能够区分三类诊断情形，避免了仅依赖输出行为判断的局限。该框架可扩展至锥值安全头，并通过非绑定顺序交换恒等式提供线性控制界面的诊断工具，在预测能力上显著优于静态跨Gram基线。

Abstract: A deployed language model may refuse a harmful request in English yet comply with its faithful translation, revealing a cross-lingual safety failure that cannot be characterized reliably by output behavior alone. We formalize this phenomenon through an audited equivalence relation and show that, for a declared quotient, representation, metric, feature dictionary, scoring head, threshold, and contrast model, the resulting safety drift admits an exact linear-algebraic characterization. Specifically, the drift is a cross-Gram functional of the within-fiber contrast; its worst admissible value is a support function, while margin invariance is characterized by an annihilator condition.
  We introduce an intrinsic calibrated exposure measure, governed by the leverage duality $χ^2=1/\ell-1$, which separates observed drift into three diagnostically distinct regimes: a reader fault removable by recalibration, an exact correction that is too ill-conditioned to be reliable, and a representation-level collision that no readout-only intervention can remove. Thus, identical observed exposure can lead to fundamentally different remediation verdicts. The framework also extends to cone-valued safety heads. An untied order-swap identity provides a diagnostic for the linear control interface; its calibration-state residual predicts a distinct three-control composition error on unseen states and targets, achieving median Spearman correlation $0.964$, compared with $0.269$ for a static cross-Gram baseline. etc.....

</details>


### [141] [AgentKV: Phase-Aware KV Eviction for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14872)
*Taowen Tony Liu,Jeffrey T. H. Wong,Can Xiao,Bowen Yang,Hao Mark Chen,Yiren Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出AGENTKV，通过按think/act/tool等阶段维护小查询缓冲区并联合打分，改进agentic场景下的KV缓存淘汰，在多个模型与任务上提升任务得分与吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic serving耗token量巨大，给KV缓存容量与解码带宽带来压力。现有KV淘汰方法假设未来注意力与近期注意力相似，仅用最近token作代表查询打分，但agentic生成中未来查询是think、act、tool等多阶段的混合分布，主角度分析显示这些成分处于不同查询子空间，导致近期代表会系统性低估未来阶段所需key。

Method: 提出AGENTKV：每个阶段维护一个小型查询缓冲区，用其并集对缓存key打分进行KV淘汰；并在持久化多轮服务路径中跨轮携带压缩KV状态，在线压缩保留的KV页。

Result: 在两个模型、六个任务领域、每种三个KV预算下，AGENTKV相比R-KV平均提升任务得分5.5分，相比Tri-attention提升5.3分；相对上游全KV的SGLang，输出token吞吐最高提升1.80倍。

Conclusion: 面向agentic负载的分阶段查询表示能更准确评估KV重要性，AGENTKV在保持多轮持久服务的同时显著提升任务表现与吞吐，验证了打破近期注意力假设的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic serving can consume orders of magnitude more tokens than chatbot workloads, stressing both KV-cache capacity and decode-time bandwidth. Most KV-eviction methods score cached keys against representative queries drawn from the most recent tokens, assuming future attention resembles recent attention. We show that agentic generation violates this assumption: future queries form a mixture over think, act, tool, and others phases, and principal-angle analysis shows these components occupy measurably different query subspaces, so recency representatives systematically undervalue keys that upcoming phases will need. We propose AGENTKV, which maintains a small query buffer per phase and scores cached keys against their union. We further implement AGENTKV in a persistent multi-turn serving path that carries compressed KV state across turns and compacts retained KV pages online. Across two models, six task domains, and three KV budgets each, AGENTKV improves task score by 5.5 points on average over R-KV and 5.3 over Tri-attention. Relative to upstream full-KV SGLang, AGENTKV improves output-token throughput by up to 1.80x. Code: https://github.com/LiuTaowen-Tony/agentkv.

</details>


### [142] [SeqMaestro: From nucleotide sequences to biological hypotheses through interpretable machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14882)
*Evgeny S. Saveliev,Krzysztof Kacprzyk,Charlotte Capitanchik,Neelanjan Mukherjee,Kate Matlin,Ryan Sheridan,Srinivas Ramachandran,Jernej Ule,David L. Bentley,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeqMaestro是一个无需编程的机器学习框架，通过可解释模型和两层接口，从核苷酸序列中提炼生物学假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核苷酸序列分析在调控基因组学、进化生物学和表型预测中至关重要。传统生物信息学方法可解释但灵活性有限；深度学习预测能力强但内部机制难以审视；可解释机器学习方法虽透明，却无法直接处理核苷酸序列。因此需要一种兼顾可解释性与序列适用性的方案。

Method: 提出SeqMaestro框架，核心是连接核苷酸序列与可解释机器学习生态的两层接口。基于该接口可拟合多种可解释模型、特征表示与提取策略的组合，利用不同透明模型间的差异性识别稳健的生物信号。系统还支持数据转换与清洗、模型拟合、超参数调优、可靠性分析及结果综述报告生成，并以无代码工作流呈现。

Result: SeqMaestro能够组合多种可解释模型与特征提取策略，识别出比单纯特征重要性更丰富、更稳健的预测关系，并自动生成情境化书面报告。

Conclusion: SeqMaestro为研究者提供了一条从核苷酸序列到生物学假设的可及路径，无需大量编程或机器学习专业知识，使可解释序列分析更易普及。

Abstract: Nucleotide sequence analysis is central to problems spanning regulatory genomics, evolutionary biology, and phenotype prediction. Classical bioinformatics methods extract interpretable sequence properties such as motifs and k-mer composition, but their flexibility is limited. In contrast, modern deep learning models can learn powerful predictive representations directly from raw sequences, yet their internal representations and decision mechanisms are difficult to inspect. Interpretable machine learning methods (e.g., sparse linear models and decision trees) provide human-understandable representations of predictive relationships but are not designed to operate directly on nucleotide sequences. Here, we introduce SeqMaestro, a machine learning framework that proposes biological hypotheses from nucleotide sequences using interpretable models. Our solution is centered around a two-layer interface that connects nucleotide sequences with the broader ecosystem of interpretable machine learning. SeqMaestro uses this interface to fit diverse combinations of interpretable models, feature representations, and extraction strategies, leveraging variability across transparent models to identify robust biological signals and richer predictive relationships than feature importance alone can provide. The system also supports data transformation and cleaning, model fitting, hyperparameter tuning, reliability analysis, and synthesis of results into a contextualized written report. By providing these capabilities through a no-code workflow, SeqMaestro is designed to make interpretable sequence analysis accessible to researchers without requiring extensive programming or machine learning expertise. SeqMaestro thereby provides an accessible route from nucleotide sequences to biological hypotheses.

</details>


### [143] [Cross-Block Conditioning in Deep Boltzmann Machines for Statistical Data Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14934)
*Junichiro Niimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 observed-block multi-prediction，使 DBM 在统计融合的两面板（部分共享协变量、结果不重叠）下可训练；大规模网格实验表明增益主要来自“在预测一个结果块时条件于另一个结果块”，而非生成式预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计数据融合场景中没有任何一行能同时观测到两个结果块，因而无法获得标准多预测训练所需的留出目标的真值，导致无法直接使用判别式准则训练 DBM。作者希望在此设定下仍能获得可用的判别式目标，并借此检验联合模型是否真的必要。

Method: 将多预测目标限制为每行实际观测到的目标（observed-block multi-prediction），对任意缺失模式均良定义，行完整时退化为原始准则；再把联合模型的贡献分解为表示部分与推断部分，通过消融各组成部分来定位增益来源；用置换一个结果块以破坏其与另一块的关联作为对照。

Result: 在两个消费者面板上、样本量与协变量宽度共 35 个网格单元、875 次运行中，微调 DBM 在全部单元中均优于十五种对比方法；但生成式预训练的贡献仅在其中一个数据集的最小样本量下出现，在另一数据集上不存在。真正的增益来自预测一个结果块时条件于另一个结果块，贡献分别为 +0.19 和 +0.07 个百分点，在 35 个单元中全为正，且不随面板增大而衰减，也不依赖第二隐藏层或更多推断。破坏两块之间关联的置换会完全消除该增益。

Conclusion: 不衰减的小效应与会衰减的效应性质不同：该条件化增益基于任何“协变量到结果”的映射模型都无法接受的证据。因此联合建模的价值在于利用结果之间的关联，而非生成式预训练本身，尽管绝对幅度较小。

Abstract: Statistical data fusion combines two panels that share a block of covariates but observe disjoint outcome blocks, and in its traditional form no row observes both outcomes at once. That rules out the discriminative criterion one would rather train a Deep Boltzmann Machine with, since multi-prediction training needs ground truth for whatever it holds out. We propose observed-block multi-prediction, which restricts the multi-prediction objective to targets drawn from what each row actually observes. It is well defined for any missingness pattern and reduces to the original criterion when rows are complete. Having a discriminative criterion that survives the setting lets us ask whether the joint model is needed at all, by separating what it contributes into a representation part and an inference part. On two consumer panels, on grids over sample size and covariate width spanning 35 cells and 875 runs, the fine-tuned DBM is the best of fifteen methods in every cell; but almost none of that advantage comes from generative pre-training, which is confined to the smallest sample size on one dataset and absent on the other. It comes from conditioning on one outcome block when predicting the other. This term amounts to +0.19 and +0.07 percentage points, is positive in all 35 cells, and, unlike every other contribution we measure, neither decays as the panels grow, nor requires a second hidden layer, nor requires more inference. Permuting one outcome block to destroy its association with the other removes the gain entirely, which is what the account predicts. The margins are small. But a small effect that does not decay is a different object from one that does, because it rests on evidence that no model mapping covariates to outcomes can accept.

</details>


### [144] [Cloud Workflow Scheduling Based on Graph Attention-Driven Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14952)
*Zongjin Li,Shaohan Feng,Chunxi Yang,Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GA-HRL框架，用多叉图注意力网络(GAT)结合带预测子截止期的任务表示，将云工作流调度建模为事件驱动分层半马尔可夫决策过程(SMDP)，由任务调度(TS)和容器调度(CS)两个智能体交替用PPO训练。实验表明其在成功率相当的情况下通常获得更高容器利用率和更低能耗，在速度波动最大时以微弱成功率换显著节能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态云工作流调度需同时兼顾截止期满足、容器利用率与能耗，并应对任务执行速度随机、通信依赖放置位置、任务与容器决策耦合等难题。工作流天然为DAG，但传统向量或矩阵状态无法充分刻画依赖拓扑，难以表示任务紧迫性与结构关系。

Method: 1) 为任务分配预测子截止期以表征紧迫性；2) 用多头图注意力网络(GAT)从演化DAG中提取依赖信息；3) 提出图注意力驱动的分层强化学习(GA-HRL)，将调度建模为事件驱动的分层SMDP，工作流到达和任务完成触发调度事件；4) TS智能体先将就绪任务分配到可行已有容器或请求新容器，CS智能体再为请求的容器进行主机放置；5) 两智能体用独立PPO交替训练。

Result: 在2018年阿里巴巴集群trace上，GA-HRL保持有竞争力的工作流成功率；在成功率相当的设置下通常实现更高容器利用率和更低能耗；在最大执行速度波动下，以小幅成功率损失换取能耗大幅降低。仿真代码已开源。

Conclusion: GA-HRL通过图注意力表示与分层强化学习有效整合任务调度与容器调度决策，在成功率、容器利用率和能耗间取得良好平衡，为处理DAG工作流拓扑的云调度问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dynamic cloud workflow scheduling must balance deadline satisfaction, container utilization, and energy consumption while dealing with stochastic task-execution speeds, placement-dependent communication, and coupled task and container decisions. Workflows are naturally modeled as directed acyclic graphs (DAGs), but conventional vector- or matrix-based states do not fully capture their dependency topology. To better represent task urgency and structural relationships, we assign predicted sub-deadlines to tasks and use a multi-head graph attention network (GAT) to extract dependency information from the evolving DAGs. Based on these representations, we develop a Graph Attention-Driven Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GA-HRL) framework and model the scheduling process as an event-driven hierarchical semi-Markov decision process (SMDP). Workflow arrivals and task completions trigger scheduling events. At each scheduling event, the Task Scheduling (TS) agent first processes the currently ready tasks by assigning them to admissible existing containers or requesting new ones. The requested containers are then processed by the Container Scheduling (CS) agent for host placement before the environment advances. The two agents are trained alternately using separate Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). Experiments on the 2018 Alibaba cluster trace show that GA-HRL maintains competitive workflow success rate and, in settings where success is comparable, generally achieves higher container utilization and lower energy consumption. Under the largest speed variation, it trades a small success-rate margin for substantially lower energy. Simulation code is available at: https://github.com/zongjin130/GA-HRL.

</details>


### [145] [High-Probability Nash Regret for Decentralized Learning in Markov $α$-Potential Games: Episodic and Fully Online Asynchronous Algorithms with Applications to Markov Congestion Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14959)
*S. Rasoul Etesami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无穷时域折扣马尔可夫博弈中纳什均衡的去中心化学习，针对马尔可夫α-势博弈提出KL投影自然策略梯度算法，并在情景式与全在线两种设定下给出有限时间高概率NE遗憾界，消除了分布不匹配系数，并应用于独立资源马尔可夫拥塞博弈与在线作业调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在bandit反馈下，去中心化学习纳什均衡面临分布不匹配系数随状态空间规模急剧增长的难题，且现有结果缺乏全在线异步学习下的有限时间高概率保证，因此需要设计能消除该系数、并容许近似与估计偏差的算法。

Method: 针对马尔可夫α-势博弈，提出KL投影自然策略梯度（NPG）算法，涵盖采样期冻结策略的情景式设定与每步仅获单个实现成本样本、沿持续轨迹异步更新的全在线设定；识别状态级势结构；并将框架专门化到独立资源马尔可夫拥塞博弈，建立其近似势与转移敏感性性质，从实现成本构造去中心化估计预言机。

Result: 分别得到情景式与全在线设定的有限时间高概率NE遗憾界，阶为Õ(T^{-1/4})与Õ(T^{-2/15})（含固定近似项）；界中消除了分布不匹配系数，并容许潜在近似、估计预言机偏差与转移敏感性；状态级势结构给出对势近似误差α具加性依赖的更紧保证；应用于随机机器上的策略性在线作业调度，得到可扩展的去中心化稳定调度策略学习算法。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为马尔可夫α-势博弈中全在线异步去中心化学习提供有限时间高概率NE遗憾保证，从遗憾界中去除分布不匹配系数，容许固定估计预言机偏差，并为IMCG提供具有限时间保证的可扩展去中心化学习。

Abstract: We study decentralized learning of Nash equilibria (NE) in infinite-horizon discounted Markov games under bandit feedback, focusing on Markov $α$-potential games. We develop KL-projected natural policy gradient (NPG) algorithms in two settings: an episodic setting with frozen policies during sampling and a fully online setting in which players receive a single realized cost sample per time step and update their policies asynchronously along a continuing trajectory. We establish finite-time high-probability NE regret bounds of order $\widetilde O(T^{-1/4})$ and $\widetilde O(T^{-2/15})$ for the episodic and fully online settings, respectively, up to fixed approximation terms. Crucially, our bounds eliminate the distribution-mismatch coefficient, which can scale prohibitively with the size of the state space, while accommodating potential approximation, estimation-oracle bias, and transition sensitivity. We further identify a state-wise potential structure that yields sharper guarantees with additive dependence on the potential approximation error $α$. We specialize the framework to independent-resource Markov congestion games (IMCGs), establish their approximate-potential and transition-sensitivity properties, and construct decentralized estimation oracles from realized costs. As an application, we introduce strategic online job scheduling on stochastic machines and obtain a scalable decentralized algorithm for learning stable dispatching policies. Overall, our results provide the first finite-time high-probability NE regret guarantees for fully online asynchronous decentralized learning in Markov $α$-potential games, remove distribution-mismatch coefficients from the regret bounds, accommodate fixed estimation-oracle bias, and provide scalable decentralized learning with finite-time guarantees for IMCGs.

</details>


### [146] [Learning to Solve Stochastic Controls with Unknown Drifts and Running Rewards: Theory, Algorithms and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14972)
*Jin Ma,Gaozhan Wang,Jianfeng Zhang,Xunyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究漂移系数与运行奖励函数未知的连续时间（可能高维）随机控制问题，基于松弛控制与熵正则化的探索式强化学习框架，提出具有理论保证的策略迭代算法，学习最优值函数与最优探索反馈控制策略，并通过数值实验验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实随机控制问题中，漂移系数和运行奖励函数往往未知，缺失这些模型要素使得传统方法难以直接应用。作者希望借助探索式强化学习框架，开发既有理论支撑、又高效可扩展的算法，同时求解探索式HJB方程对应的最优值函数与最优探索反馈控制策略。

Method: 采用 Wang、Zariphopoulou 和 Zhou (2020) 的探索式强化学习框架，使用松弛控制和熵正则化。当扩散系数不含控制时，基于仅依赖原始动态扩散部分的辅助状态过程，给出最优值函数及其梯度的概率表示；通过对所定义映射及其不动点的精细分析，引入策略迭代算法并证明其收敛性。最后研究扩散系数依赖控制的特殊情形，需要 Hessian 的概率表示。

Result: 建立了最优值函数及其梯度的概率表示，构造了策略迭代算法并给出收敛性证明，通过多个数值示例展示了算法性能；对控制依赖扩散的特殊情形，进一步引入 Hessian 的概率表示。

Conclusion: 该工作为未知模型下的连续时间随机控制提供了理论上可靠、计算上有效的强化学习算法，拓展了探索式 RL 框架的适用范围，并为扩散依赖控制的更复杂情形提供了分析基础。

Abstract: We study continuous-time and possibly high-dimensional stochastic control problems where drift coefficients and running reward functions are unknown. Due to these missing model primitives, we take the exploratory, reinforcement learning (RL) framework of Wang, Zariphopoulou, and Zhou(2020) with relaxed controls and entropy regularization. The objective is to develop theoretically grounded, efficient and scalable RL algorithms to learn both the optimal value functions (which also solve the exploratory HJB equation) and optimal exploratory feedback control policies. When the diffusion coefficients do not contain control, we employ probabilistic representations of both the optimal value function and its gradient based on an auxiliary state process depending only on the diffusion part of the original dynamics. With a delicate analysis on some properly defined mappings and their fixed points, this leads to the introduction of our policy iteration algorithms and their convergence. We demonstrate the performance of our algorithms through various numerical examples. Finally, we study a special control-dependent diffusion case where probability representation of the Hessian is called for.

</details>


### [147] [Pick Your Poison: Learning to Select Poison Sets for Stronger LLM Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15029)
*Aashiq Muhamed,Mona T. Diab,Virginia Smith,Andrew Ilyas,Matthew Jagielski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出随机采样投毒样本会严重低估后门攻击的最坏情况脆弱性，并将投毒集选择形式化为预算受限的集合优化问题，提出SAILS方法在有限评估预算下高效搜索高攻击成功率的投毒集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门投毒评估通常固定投毒样本数量并从候选池中随机采样，这会导致对最坏情况脆弱性的严重低估。作者发现，在模型、干净数据和投毒数量均固定的情况下，仅改变投毒集的选择，攻击成功率就会从3%大幅波动到80%，说明投毒集的组合选择是影响攻击效果的关键因素。

Method: 作者将投毒样本选择形式化为"oracle预算受限的集合优化"问题，并提出了SAILS方法。SAILS通过几百次微调-评估运行学习一个集合打分器，用以对海量候选投毒集进行排序，然后只对少量候选集进行实际审计，从而在有限预算下找到高攻击成功率的投毒集。

Result: 在三个LLaMA-3-8B后门设置中，SAILS相比最强的影响力基线方法在留出攻击成功率上平均提升30个百分点；该方法能够从小规模微调迁移到全规模微调，并可扩展到代码生成、智能体（agentic）以及仅API访问的后门场景。

Conclusion: 投毒集的组合选择对后门攻击效果至关重要，随机采样会显著低估最坏情况风险。SAILS提供了一种预算高效的方法来识别高风险的投毒集，揭示了现有后门评估的低估问题，并具有跨任务与跨设置的泛化能力。

Abstract: Backdoor poisoning attacks add poisoned examples to otherwise-clean finetuning data, pairing a trigger with a target behavior that the model learns to produce when the trigger appears. Existing evaluations typically fix the number of poisoned examples and sample them at random from a candidate pool. We show that this can severely underestimate worst-case vulnerability: across three LLaMA-3-8B backdoor settings, holding the model, clean data, and poison count fixed, attack success ranges from 3% to 80% depending only on which poison set is chosen.
  We formalize poison selection as oracle-budgeted set optimization and introduce SAILS (Set-level Audit-Informed Iterative Learned Selection), which learns a set scorer from a few hundred finetune-and-evaluate runs, ranks millions of candidate sets, and audits only a small shortlist. SAILS improves held-out attack success by 30 percentage points on average over the strongest influence baselines, transfers from small-scale to full-scale finetuning, and extends to code-generation, agentic, and API-only backdoors.

</details>


### [148] [Structured Features Overfit Where Random Features Grok](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15047)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Miloud Bessafi,Frederic Cadet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过带限傅里叶特征映射实验，表明结构化特征映射下并不出现Xu等人（ICML 2026）所证明的过参数化岭回归中随1/λ增长的记忆-泛化延迟（grokking）；峰值留出准确率随带宽单调下降，其关键边界在活跃支撑（active support）而非容量比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Xu、Vardi与Safran（ICML 2026）证明在无结构随机高斯特征映射上，过参数化岭回归会出现grokking，且记忆与泛化之间的延迟随权重衰减按1/λ增长。作者希望检验这一延迟现象是否依赖特征映射的无结构假设，探究在结构化特征映射上是否仍会出现先记忆后泛化的行为。

Method: 在Z_p^2上构造带限傅里叶特征映射，目标为位于可表达类内、承载单字符的target；固定正权重衰减，沿带宽sweep扩大频带（从1089个活跃模式到4225个模式），测量峰值留出准确率与容量比q/n，并通过掩码回退到1089个活跃模式来考察活跃支撑的作用；分析教师加权经验Gram矩阵谱。

Result: 扩大频带使峰值留出准确率从1.00单调降至0.07，全程无先记忆后泛化区制。退化非插值效应，在容量比q/n=0.638处即开始，远低于插值阈值。活跃支撑有清晰边界：固定名义维度、将频带掩码回退到1089个活跃模式，可将留出准确率恢复至1.000且种子间零方差；而全部4225个模式时准确率仅0.185。

Conclusion: 在结构化特征映射上不存在与无结构情形相同的grokking延迟。活跃模式数通过教师加权经验Gram矩阵的谱（而非容量比）发挥作用，因此该现象是关于特征几何的陈述，而非双下降的重新表述。

Abstract: Xu, Vardi and Safran (ICML 2026) prove that over-parameterized ridge regression over an unstructured random Gaussian feature map groks, with the delay between memorization and generalization growing as $1/λ$ in the weight decay. We show that on a structured feature map the same delay does not appear. For a band-limited Fourier feature map over $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$ carrying a single-character target that lies inside the expressible class, enlarging the band at fixed positive weight decay drives peak held-out accuracy monotonically from $1.00$ to $0.07$, with no memorize-then-generalize regime anywhere along the sweep. The degradation is not an interpolation effect. It sets in at capacity ratio $q/n = 0.638$, far below the interpolation threshold, on separate grounds from the exact null space that appears above it. What does have a sharp boundary is the active support. Holding the nominal dimension fixed and masking the band back to $1089$ active modes restores held-out accuracy of $1.000$ with zero variance across seeds, while the full $4225$-mode band collapses to $0.185$. The number of active modes acts through the teacher-weighted spectrum of the empirical Gram matrix and not through the capacity ratio, which makes this a statement about feature geometry and not a restatement of double descent.

</details>


### [149] [Sensory Precision Inference for Multimodal Arbitration under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15065)
*Tin Mišić,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多模态感知推理模型，联合更新潜在信念与各模态感官精度，通过自由能最小化实现感官冲突下的自适应仲裁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中多感官流常受噪声、缺失数据和模态不一致干扰，智能体需在竞争性感官假设间自适应仲裁。主动推理虽提供不确定性引导的推理框架，但动态推断的感官精度在感官冲突下多模态仲裁中的作用尚不充分。

Method: 构建多模态感知推理模型，通过迭代自由能最小化联合更新潜在信念与模态特定的感官精度；感官精度动态既反映不确定性又主动塑造信念演化；引入学习到的感官精度先验，产生结构化的、类别相关的精度模式并影响跨模态推理。

Result: 在合成多模态MNIST（视觉、听觉、触觉）数据集上，动态精度推理提升了噪声证据下的重建鲁棒性，支持从缩减证据中进行连贯潜在推理，并在冲突模态间实现稳定仲裁；学习到的精度先验生成可解释的精度结构，塑造推理动态与跨模态潜在结构。

Conclusion: 感官精度推理是不确定性下自适应多模态信念形成的机制性控制过程，精度动态是稳健且可解释的多感官整合的计算机制。

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating on multisensory data cannot assume that all sensory modalities remain consistently informative. In real environments, sensory streams are frequently corrupted by noise, missing data, or inter-modal incongruence, requiring adaptive arbitration between competing sensory hypotheses. While active inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty-guided inference, the role of dynamically inferred sensory precision in generative multimodal arbitration under sensory conflict remains comparatively underexplored. We propose a multimodal perceptual inference model in which latent beliefs and modality-specific sensory precisions are jointly updated through iterative free-energy minimization. In our proposed model, sensory precision dynamics not only reflect sensory uncertainty but actively shape the evolution of latent beliefs during multimodal conflict. In addition, we introduce a learned prior over sensory precisions that induces structured, class-dependent precision patterns and influences cross-modal inference dynamics. We evaluate the model using a synthetic multimodal MNIST dataset combining visual, auditory, and tactile representations of digit classes under controlled sensory noise and inter-modal incongruence. Results show that dynamic precision inference improves reconstruction robustness under corrupted sensory evidence, supports coherent latent inference from reduced sensory evidence, and enables stable arbitration between conflicting modalities. Furthermore, learned precision priors generate interpretable precision structures that shape inference dynamics and cross-modal latent structure. These findings support sensory precision inference as a mechanistic control process for adaptive multimodal belief formation under uncertainty, highlighting precision dynamics as a computational mechanism for robust and interpretable multisensory integration.

</details>


### [150] [Ensemble-Conditioned Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15077)
*Ross Irwin,Alessandro Tibo,Jon Paul Janet,Simon Olsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将分子设计重新定义为对构象系综的模式与性质的联合优化，提出"系综条件引导"框架，使3D分子生成模型同时受模式条件和系综性质条件控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子设计通常被视为寻找能采用单一生物活性构象的分子，但实际上分子以构象分布的形式存在，许多决定候选分子可行性的性质依赖于整个分布而非单一构象。因此需要一种能同时优化构象系综模式与整体性质的分子设计新范式。

Method: 提出系综条件引导框架，同时对模式（形状、药效团轮廓或蛋白口袋）与性质（对整个分布计算的聚合标量）两条轴进行条件化。模式条件在推理时通过组合各条件下的向量场自适应地构成，条件可被靶向或回避、跨模态混合、以任意数量组合；引入自适应对称学习以组合来自不同参考帧的条件，并扩展生成框架支持可变尺寸生成。

Result: 在多模式条件化与系综性质优化的新基准上进行了评估，并将框架应用于双靶点结合剂设计和活性态选择性激动剂设计两个实际药物发现任务，结果显示在两种情况下引入额外状态条件相比单状态条件均改善了期望结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将分子设计从单一构象优化拓展为构象系综的模式与性质联合优化，单一训练模型即可表达广泛的设计任务，并在实际药物发现任务中验证了系综条件化的有效性。

Abstract: Molecular design is typically approached as a problem of finding molecules which can adopt a single bioactive conformation. In reality, molecules occupy a distribution over conformations, and many of the properties which determine whether a candidate is viable depend on that distribution rather than on any single conformer. We reframe molecular design as an optimisation of both the modes and properties of molecules' conformational ensembles, where modes can be represented as shapes, pharmacophore profiles or protein pockets, and properties are aggregate scalars computed over the whole distribution. To realise this we introduce ensemble-conditioned guidance, a framework which conditions 3D molecular generative models on both axes simultaneously. Mode conditions are composed adaptively at inference by combining the vector fields produced under each condition. Conditions may be targeted or avoided, mixed across modalities and combined in arbitrary numbers, allowing a wide range of design tasks to be expressed with a single trained model. We introduce adaptive symmetry learning to allow conditions from different reference frames to be composed, and extend our generative framework to enable flexible-size generation. We evaluate on new benchmarks for multi-mode conditioning and ensemble property optimisation, and apply the framework to two practical drug discovery tasks, dual-target binder design and active-state-selective agonist design, where in both cases conditioning on the additional state improves the desired outcome over single-state conditioning.

</details>


### [151] [$\mathbb{SL}(n)$ Representation Learning: An Intrinsic Mixed-Curvature Space with Higher Curvature Capacities and Deeper Order-Aware Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15083)
*Xingrun Li,Yusuke Mukuta,Xin Yang,Yinyu Ye,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ 空间作为一种新的表示几何，通过简单的 $\det(A)=1$ 约束和左不变 Schatten-$p$ Finsler 结构，在统一空间中实现混合曲率、曲率耦合、顺序敏感与深层复合能力，并在多个图基准上显著超越现有表示流形基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合曲率表示学习主要依赖积流形，需要手动指定不同曲率空间的组合方式，并将曲率贡献分散在各个因子上，难以自然地统一建模丰富几何结构。

Method: 引入 $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ 空间：由 $\det(A)=1$ 约束定义，配备左不变 Schatten-$p$ Finsler 结构；利用其非交换群结构提供顺序敏感性，利用非幂零 Lie 代数在任意深度上具有非零嵌套 Lie 括号，从而支持深层顺序感知复合。

Result: 在多个尺度的图基准上持续优于广泛的表示流形基线；在 KEGG 上将平均失真较最强基线降低 $44.3\%$，在 HumanCyc 上降低 $40.5\%$，在 OGBL-PPA 上将 Hits@20 提升 $42.8\%$；Flickr30k-Order 实验进一步支持其捕获高阶依赖的能力。

Conclusion: 一个看似简单的结构约束可以产生出乎意料的丰富几何、容量与复合能力，并在统一表示空间中实现，表明 $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ 是混合曲率表示学习的有力新方向。

Abstract: Mixed-curvature representation learning seeks to capture rich geometric structures that cannot be adequately modeled by a single curvature regime. Existing approaches largely rely on product manifolds, which require manually specifying how different curvature spaces are combined and separate their curvature contributions across factors. We introduce the $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ space, a representation geometry defined by the simple $\det(A)=1$ constraint and a left invariant Schatten-$p$ Finsler structure. Despite this minimal construction, $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ exhibits pointwise negative, zero, and positive flag curvature around a common flagpole, while its mixed-curvature and curvature-coupling capacities are asymptotically maximal relative to the intrinsic geometric upper bound. Beyond geometry, its noncommutative group structure provides inherent order sensitivity, and its non-nilpotent Lie algebra admits nonzero nested Lie brackets at arbitrary depth, enabling deep order-aware composition. Empirically, $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ consistently outperforms a broad range of representation manifold baselines across graph benchmarks at different scales. It reduces average distortion over the strongest baselines by $44.3\%$ on KEGG and $40.5\%$ on HumanCyc, and improves Hits@20 by $42.8\%$ on OGBL-PPA. Experiments on Flickr30k-Order further support its ability to capture higher order dependencies from ordered composition. Together, these results show how a seemingly simple structural constraint can yield unexpectedly rich geometry, capacity, and composition within a unified representation space.

</details>


### [152] [Beyond Numerical Time Series: A Unified Benchmark for Multimodal Forecasting with Heterogeneous Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15087)
*Peng Chen,Zhihao Zhuang,Hongzhou Chen,Junhao Huang,Aiping Yang,Mengsen Wu,Yiding Liu,Xilin Dai,Zewei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MUSE-Bench，一个面向异构上下文的多模态时间序列预测统一基准，覆盖8个领域、14个数据集与6类上下文，并在统一设置下系统评估多类预测范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序预测基准以数值为中心，难以评估影响真实时间动态的上下文信息；现有多模态基准则存在数据与上下文覆盖有限、评估设置割裂、过度依赖聚合指标等问题，因此需要一个统一、系统的多模态时序预测评测基准。

Method: 构建MUSE-Bench，包含8个领域、14个数据集和6类上下文（元数据、事件、节假日、新闻、图像、数值协变量）；在共享的非重叠预测窗口、共同目标观测以及一致的点预测与概率预测指标下，评估统计方法、数据专用模型、基础模型、多模态模型和通用LLM预测方法，并进行消融实验。

Result: 主要发现有三：一是数值时序基础模型在总体排名中占优，多模态基础模型Aurora落后于领先数值TSFM但优于所有评估的数据专用模型；二是消融显示外部上下文能提升四个上下文感知模型，而错误或时间错位的上下文会降低性能；三是通用LLM作为直接预测器表现较差，LLM引导的修正也未带来一致提升。

Conclusion: MUSE-Bench支持系统评估预测模型如何利用上下文，并为未来多模态预测研究提供基础。

Abstract: Most time series forecasting benchmarks remain numerical-centric and provide limited support for evaluating contextual information that shapes real-world temporal dynamics. Existing multimodal benchmarks also suffer from limited data and context coverage, fragmented evaluation settings, and overreliance on aggregate evaluation. In this paper, we propose \textbf{MUSE-Bench}, a unified benchmark for multimodal time series forecasting with heterogeneous context. It comprises fourteen datasets across eight domains and six types of context: metadata, events, holidays, news, images, and numerical covariates. We evaluate diverse forecasting paradigms, including statistical, data-specific, foundation, multimodal, and general-purpose LLM forecasting methods under shared non-overlapping forecast windows, common target observations, and consistent point and probabilistic metrics. Extensive experiments yield three main findings. First, numerical time series foundation models dominate the overall ranking, while Aurora, the evaluated multimodal foundation model, trails the leading numerical TSFMs but outperforms all evaluated data-specific models. Second, ablations show that external context improves the four evaluated context-aware models, whereas incorrect or temporally misaligned context degrades performance. Third, general-purpose LLMs perform poorly as direct forecasters, and LLM-guided refinement does not yield consistent improvements. MUSE-Bench enables systematic evaluation of how forecasting models utilize context and provides a foundation for future multimodal forecasting research.

</details>


### [153] [Temporal Self-Distillation: Faster Inference in Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15177)
*Shijian Xu,Andrea Miele,Metod Jazbec,Volker Roth,Eric Nalisnick,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时序自蒸馏（TSD），一种on-policy单阶段方法，通过将早期去噪分布蒸馏向最终承诺时刻的分布，使dLLM在更激进的并行解码下仍保持质量，显著推进速度-质量前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLM）通过并行生成多token来实现快速推理，但当并行解码过于激进时性能会严重下降。现有加速方案（如离线蒸馏）需要复杂的两阶段流程和离线教师生成，成本高、流程繁琐。

Method: 时序自蒸馏（TSD）：一种on-policy的简单方法，将模型在较早时间步的去噪分布蒸馏至token被承诺（commit）的最终时间步的分布，促使早期预测更好地预判模型的最终输出。由于教师信号来自模型自身，TSD无需离线教师生成，且可无缝适用于基础策略和后期训练（post-trained）策略。

Result: 在数学、规划和代码领域的七个基准上，TSD大幅将速度-质量前沿推向低计算量区间，达到与离线蒸馏相当的速度提升，同时避免复杂的两阶段流程。

Conclusion: TSD提供了一种简单、单阶段的dLLM加速方案，通过自蒸馏实现更激进的并行解码，在保持质量的同时获得有竞争力的加速效果。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) promise fast inference by generating multiple tokens in parallel, but suffer severe performance degradation when parallel decoding is pushed too aggressively. We introduce Temporal Self-Distillation (TSD), a simple on-policy method that trains dLLMs for fast inference by distilling predictions across time. Specifically, TSD distills the model's denoising distribution at earlier timesteps toward its distribution at the final timestep at which a token is committed. This encourages earlier predictions to better anticipate the model's eventual output, enabling much more aggressive parallel decoding. Because its teacher signal comes from the model itself, TSD requires no offline teacher generation and applies seamlessly to both base and post-trained policies. Across seven benchmarks in mathematics, planning, and code, TSD substantially shifts the speed--quality frontier toward the low-compute regime. TSD thus provides a simple, single-stage approach to accelerating dLLMs, achieving speedups competitive with offline distillation while avoiding a complex two-stage pipeline.

</details>


### [154] [Rethinking Correctness for Uncertainty Estimation in Clinical Prediction with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15180)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Jinning Liang,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双轴框架评估临床预测中"正确性判定标准"的质量，发现标准选择会显著影响不确定性估计(UE)的评价结论，建议在比较UE方法前先验证正确性判定标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在电子健康记录与医学图像的临床预测中日益应用，识别不可靠预测对安全部署至关重要。不确定性估计(UE)可检测此类预测，但其评估依赖于"正确性判定标准"——该标准若与人类判断不一致或扭曲下游UE性能，会导致关于模型可靠性的错误结论。

Method: 提出双轴框架，从两个维度评估正确性判定标准：(1)与人类判断的一致性；(2)对人类参照的UE性能的保真度。在3个临床预测任务、3个模型上评估8种判定标准，使用2名评审员标注的450条预测；并在4种UE方法、23,254条临床预测上检验标准选择的影响。

Result: 在各任务中，经典精确匹配(EM)取得最高的人类一致性与最低的UE扭曲；基于BERT的匹配(BEM)与LLM评判者也表现出较强的人类一致性。标准选择使错误检测AUROC变化高达0.146，并逆转了UE方法的相对排名。LLM评判者还会选择性地接受无效或不确定输出，在30个人类识别的错误中接受了16个。

Conclusion: 正确性评估是临床UE评测的组成部分，在比较UE方法之前应先对其正确性判定标准进行验证。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly explored for clinical prediction from electronic health records and medical images, where identifying unreliable predictions is important for safe deployment. Uncertainty estimation (UE) enables detecting such predictions, but its evaluation depends on a correctness criterion that determines whether each model output is correct. If this criterion disagrees with human judgement or distorts downstream UE performance, conclusions about model reliability can be misleading. We introduce a two-axis framework that evaluates correctness criteria by their agreement with human judgements and fidelity to human-referenced UE performance. We assess eight criteria across three clinical prediction tasks and three models using 450 predictions annotated by two reviewers. Across the audited tasks, canonical exact matching (EM) achieved the highest observed human agreement and lowest UE distortion, while the BERT-based matching (BEM) and LLM-judge also showed strong human agreement. Across four UE methods and 23,254 clinical predictions, criterion choice changed error-detection AUROC by up to 0.146 and reversed the relative ranking of UE methods. The LLM-judge also selectively accepted invalid or uncertain outputs, accepting 16 of 30 such human-identified errors. These results demonstrate that correctness assessment is an integral component of clinical UE evaluation and should be validated before UE methods are compared.

</details>


### [155] [Convergence rates for generative drifting flows: fixed-scale obstructions and multihead acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15193)
*Arthur Stéphanovitch,Eddie Aamari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究漂移模型（drifting models）的收敛速率，指出单一固定分辨率会隐藏目标分布的细尺度特征，导致收敛极慢；提出多头（multihead）方法整合连续分辨率上的尺度归一化信息，恢复了指数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移模型可在训练时渐进式传输、生成时一步采样，是通往更快生成式AI的途径。但作者想探究：在理想条件下（排除有限数据和优化效应），漂移过程能否快速收敛到目标分布？固定分辨率的处理方式是否构成瓶颈？

Method: 理论分析漂移过程的收敛速率，发现其关键取决于对空间尺度的处理方式；针对单一固定分辨率导致细尺度特征"近乎不可见"的问题，提出多头方法，在连续分辨率上结合尺度归一化的信息。

Result: 证明了单一固定分辨率下目标分布的细尺度特征可能近乎不可见，导致收敛极其缓慢；而多头方法能在标准参考分布附近恢复指数收敛。

Conclusion: 固定分辨率是漂移模型的关键瓶颈；多头方法提供了一条简单路径，以更快实现一步生成式模型。

Abstract: Drifting models offer a promising route to faster generative AI: they perform gradual transport during training, while generating new samples in a single step. This paper asks whether the underlying drifting process can converge rapidly to a target distribution under ideal conditions, before finite-data or optimization effects are introduced. We show that its convergence rate depends critically on how it handles spatial scale. With a single fixed resolution, fine-scale features of the target can become nearly invisible, leading to extremely slow convergence. We introduce a multihead approach that combines scale-normalized information across a continuum of resolutions. We prove that this multihead approach restores exponential convergence near standard reference distributions. These results identify fixed resolution as a key bottleneck and provide a simple route to faster one-step generative models.

</details>


### [156] [ProtoGuide: Prototype-Driven Guidance for Class-Conditional Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15239)
*Salvatore Romano,Marco Grassia,Pietro Liò,Giuseppe Mangioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ProtoGuide：一种无需重训、与骨干模型无关的后处理引导框架，用冻结的孪生GNN和类别原型为离散图扩散模型提供逐边梯度引导，在EDGE与DiGress上显著提升分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型是图生成的重要方法，但标准类条件机制在训练时把类别信号嵌入去噪器，导致条件机制与训练模型强耦合，无法在不重训的情况下更换目标类别。连续域中可用分类器梯度引导冻结模型，但离散图扩散采样的是离散边状态，梯度无法穿过采样的图传播，因此需要一种新的后处理引导方式。

Method: ProtoGuide 在每一步反向过程中，将去噪器对每条边的输出松弛为可微的软邻接矩阵，用冻结的孪生GNN嵌入，并与目标类原型及其最近竞争类原型打分；得到的逐边梯度经余弦调度衰减后注入去噪器输出。全部组件保持冻结，只需更换原型即可切换引导目标。

Result: 在五类真实网络和EDGE、DiGress两种不同架构骨干上，ProtoGuide将宏分类准确率从50.7%提升至73.5%（EDGE）、从73.6%提升至83.8%（DiGress），并在该配置下优于DiGress内置的条件训练。提升在未引导模型最弱的类别上最大，且各类别间不均匀；多数设置下逐图覆盖率仍较高，分布影响依类别而异。Best-of-N选择基线在充分过采样时可达到相同准确率，但图多样性代价显著。独立初始化的分类器、方向性测试和少样本分析支持引导是目标导向且对极小支持集稳健。

Conclusion: ProtoGuide 证明了在离散图扩散中可实现类似分类器引导的后处理机制，解耦了条件机制与训练模型，具备骨干无关、可即插即用、支持少样本切换目标类别等优点；同时在准确率与多样性之间存在权衡，效果依类别而异。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are a prominent family for graph generation, but standard class-conditional mechanisms embed the class signal in the denoiser during training, tying the conditioning mechanism to the trained model. Classifier guidance avoids this coupling in continuous domains by steering a frozen model with a classifier's gradient, but discrete graph diffusion samples discrete edge states, so gradients cannot propagate through the sampled graph. We introduce ProtoGuide, a post-hoc, backbone-agnostic framework that recovers an analogous mechanism. At each reverse step the denoiser's per-edge output is relaxed into a differentiable soft adjacency, embedded by a frozen Siamese graph neural network, and scored against a target-class prototype and its nearest competitor; the resulting per-edge gradient, damped by a cosine schedule, is injected back into the denoiser output. All components stay frozen, so guidance is retargeted by supplying a different prototype. On five classes of real-world networks and two architecturally different backbones, EDGE and DiGress, ProtoGuide raises macro classification accuracy from 50.7% to 73.5% and from 73.6% to 83.8%, and outperforms DiGress's built-in conditional training under our configuration. Gains are largest where the unguided models are weakest, and are not uniform across classes. Per-graph coverage remains high in most settings, while distributional effects are class-dependent. A Best-of-N selection baseline matches this accuracy given enough oversampling, but at a substantial cost in graph diversity. An independently initialized classifier, a directionality test, and a few-shot analysis support target-directed steering and robustness to very small support sets.

</details>


### [157] [Draining Fictitious Knots: Restoring Distance-Awareness Guarantees for High-Dimensional Spline Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15274)
*Masoud Ataei,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出KAN不确定性量化方法DAREK在高维下因"虚构结点"而失效，并提出排水不确定性机制恢复距离感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAREK基于Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理采用逐分量形式，虽计算高效且具备距离感知误差界，但在高维中，各维度结点组合会在联合输入空间产生与实际训练数据无关的"虚构结点"，导致不确定性估计在远离真实观测处错误报告低不确定性，违背距离感知保证。

Method: 论文精确识别该失效模式并刻画其几何结构，提出"排水不确定性机制"：从任意虚构结点区域向最近真实结点构造单调递减的不确定性路径，作为实用启发式修正。

Result: 在2D合成基准和100维人脸数据集上，排水方法将采样距离感知(SDA)从85%提升至98-99%，与高斯过程相当但计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 排水方法有效缓解虚构结点失效模式，在高维下恢复理论距离感知，兼顾精度与效率，为KAN不确定性量化提供可行修正。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) with spline activations have recently shown promise for interpretable function approximation. Distance-Aware Error for Kolmogorov Networks (DAREK) introduces a computationally efficient bottom-up approach to uncertainty quantification by equipping KANs with distance-aware error bounds; yet, in high-dimensional settings, the theoretical guarantees can be weakened by the emergence of fictitious knots. Inspired by the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem, DAREK adopts a componentwise formulation in which each input dimension is treated separately; as a result, induced knot locations may appear in the combined input space without corresponding to actual training data. These fictitious knots mislead the DAREK uncertainty estimator into reporting low uncertainty far from any real observation, violating the distance-awareness guarantee. We identify this failure mode precisely, characterize its geometric structure, and propose a drainage uncertainty mechanism that restores distance-awareness by constructing a monotonically decreasing uncertainty path from any fictitious knot region toward the nearest real knot. The proposed drainage method provides a practical heuristic correction that mitigates the fictitious-knot failure mode while restoring theoretical distance-awareness in high-dimensional settings. Experiments on a 2D synthetic benchmark and a 100-dimensional face dataset show that drainage raises sampled distance-awareness (SDA) from 85% to 98-99%, matching Gaussian processes at lower computational cost.

</details>


### [158] [Impute-EM: Native Mixed-State Diffusion Models for Heterogeneous Data Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15284)
*Sergei Kholkin,Kirill Sokolov,Dmitry Baranchuk,Evgeny Burnaev,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Impute-EM：以 EM 风格交替"用当前模型补全缺失值—在补全数据上重训扩散骨干"，并采用原生混合状态扩散（高斯+掩码类别），避免 one-hot 松弛，在混合类型表格插补上取得最佳分布保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构数据挖掘中数值、类别、二元变量常共存且缺失普遍。现有插补方法（尤其扩散类）多将离散变量转成连续替代（如 one-hot 松弛），导致模型状态空间与数据本身的"离散+连续"混合结构不匹配。

Method: 提出 Impute-EM：一种期望最大化风格框架，交替执行"用当前模型对缺失项插补"与"在补全数据上重新拟合扩散骨干"两步。并实例化为原生混合状态扩散骨干，由高斯分量与掩码类别分量组合，不做 one-hot 松弛。理论上刻画了精确设置下的更新过程。

Result: 在精确设置下证明了观测掩码索引边际在极限处与目标匹配，同时明确指出仅凭不完整观测通常无法辨识完整数据分布。实验上，在混合类型表格插补中取得最佳的分布保真度（下游建模所依赖），文本插补作为原生离散骨干的受控验证。

Conclusion: 原生建模离散变量并配合 EM 式交替训练，可在混合类型数据插补中提升分布保真度；但需注意完整数据分布在不完整观测下的一般非可辨识性。

Abstract: Missing values are ubiquitous in heterogeneous data mining, where numerical, categorical, and binary variables often coexist. Many imputation methods, especially diffusion-based ones, treat discrete variables through continuous surrogates such as one-hot relaxations rather than modeling them natively. This creates a mismatch between the model state space and the mixed discrete and continuous structure of the data. We propose Impute-EM, an Expectation Maximization style framework that alternates between imputing missing entries with the current model and refitting a diffusion backbone on completed data. We instantiate Impute-EM with native mixed-state diffusion backbones for heterogeneous data, combining Gaussian and masked categorical components without one-hot relaxations. In exact settings, we characterize the update and show that the observed mask-indexed marginals match the targets at the limit, while making explicit that the full data distribution is generally non-identifiable from incomplete observations alone. Empirically, Impute-EM delivers the best distributional fidelity on mixed-type tabular imputation, on which downstream modeling relies, with text imputation serving as a controlled validation of the native discrete backbone.

</details>


### [159] [Admissable: Training Reinforcement Learning Agents against Adversarial Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15297)
*Paul Stahlhofen,Luca Hermes,Tim Kochs,Markus Vieth,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习中的对抗性特征缺失问题，提出形式化定义与对抗训练算法，在三个MuJoCo任务上提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使强化学习算法能应用于真实场景，必须在不利条件下保证安全性。本文关注对抗性特征缺失：对手通过遮挡智能体观测中的特征来尽可能降低其性能。

Method: 形式化定义了强化学习中的对抗性缺失性，并将其与ℓ∞范数有界对抗扰动及缺失数据学习等相关概念进行比较；开发了一种对抗训练算法。

Result: 在三个MuJoCo基准环境中验证了该对抗训练算法对对抗性缺失的鲁棒性提升效果。与使用随机均匀缺失训练的基线相比，该方法在三个任务上均取得更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对抗训练能有效提升强化学习智能体在面对对抗性特征缺失时的鲁棒性，优于随机缺失训练的基线方法。

Abstract: In order to make Reinforcement Learning algorithms applicable in real world scenarios, safety must be ensured even under adverse operating conditions. In this work, we consider the challenge of adversarial feature missingness: a scenario in which an adversary occludes features from the agent's observation in order to reduce performance as much as possible. We formally define adversarial missingness for Reinforcement Learning and compare it to the related concepts of $\ell_\infty$-norm bounded adversarial perturbations and learning with missing data. We develop an adversarial training algorithm and show its effectiveness in increasing robustness against adversarial missingness on three MuJoCo benchmark environments. Compared to a baseline trained with random uniform missingness, our method achieves better robustness on all three tasks.

</details>


### [160] [When Correlations Mislead: Confounder-Aware Multi-View Urban Region Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15305)
*Sean Bin Yang,Ying Sun,Zongyi Xu,Tung Kieu,Jilin Hu,Bin Yang,Kristian Torp,Hua Lu,Torben Bach Pedersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CURE，一个"混淆因子感知"的多视角城市区域表征学习框架。它先对各视角用区域图结构编码，估计共享潜在成分，并在跨视角交互前削弱其投影影响，再用分层图感知融合模块聚合残差视角表征。三个真实城市实验表明性能提升、缺失/噪声视角下鲁棒，跨视角整合可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市区域表征学习常融合移动流、POI、土地利用等多源异构数据，但现有多视角方法主要靠强化视角间交互来改进区域嵌入，忽视了各视角特有的区域结构，并可能传播由共享潜在因子引起的相关性，从而降低下游预测的稳定性。作者旨在克服这一主要局限。

Method: CURE框架包含三步：1）对每个视角用其区域图结构进行编码；2）估计一个共享潜在成分，并在跨视角交互之前削弱其投影影响（去混淆）；3）使用分层图感知融合模块，结合局部与全局区域上下文来聚合残差视角表征。

Result: 在三个真实城市上的实验显示：CURE 提升了预测性能；在视角缺失和含噪声输入时保持鲁棒；通过共享成分分离和上下文相关的视角加权，实现了可靠的跨视角整合。

Conclusion: CURE 通过分离共享潜在成分（混淆因子）并保留视角特有结构，缓解了多视角相关性传播问题，为多视角城市区域表征学习提供了更稳定、鲁棒的方案，并支撑流动性分析、公共安全预测与服务需求估计等下游任务。

Abstract: Urban region representation learning commonly combines heterogeneous data sources, such as mobility flows, points of interest, and land-use information, to support tasks including mobility analysis, public safety forecasting, and service demand estimation. Existing multi-view methods typically improve region embeddings by strengthening interactions across views. However, such methods often overlook view-specific regional structures and may propagate correlations induced by shared latent factors, which can reduce the stability of downstream predictions. To overcome this major limitation, we propose CURE, a confounder-aware framework for multi-view urban region representation learning. CURE first encodes each view with its regional graph structure, estimates a shared latent component, and then reduces its projected influence before cross-view interaction. A hierarchical graph-aware fusion module subsequently aggregates the residual view representations using local and global regional contexts Experiments on three real-world cities show that CURE improves predictive performance, remains robust under missing and noisy input views, and provides reliable cross-view integration through shared component separation and context-dependent view weighting.

</details>


### [161] [The Universe of Universes: Benefit Yield Functions, Implosion Thresholds, and Infrastructure-Aware Optimization in Multi-LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15314)
*Danielle Franklin,Vasu Raj Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"宇宙之宇宙"（UoU）框架，将全部主流大模型视为结构化检索语料库，并形式化"收益产出函数"（BYF）与"内爆阈值"θ*，研究集成规模N对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成与混合智能体系统只把模型当响应者并聚合输出，未研究性能随集成规模N的变化；基准研究只确认单模型性能平台期，模型崩塌文献只关注迭代训练对单体分布的退化，二者均未形式化集成层面的内爆阈值、生态系统级"认知遗传漂移"（EHD），也未将AI制造速度作为θ*的协变量。

Method: 将整个主流LLM生态系统视为结构化检索语料库，构建组合式自动推理（AR）与机器学习（ML）架构用于跨模型检索增强生成；形式化刻画BYF（每增加一个模型带来的边际性能增益），并识别内爆阈值θ*，即BYF降至零、总体性能开始下降时的集成规模。

Result: 给出了BYF的正式刻画，并识别出内爆阈值θ*，即集成规模达到该点时边际收益归零、总体性能开始退化。

Conclusion: 该框架对多模型AI系统的采购政策与AI赋能系统测试科学具有直接启示，并为生态系统级退化与协变量分析提供理论工具。

Abstract: We introduce the Universe of Universes (UoU) framework, which treats the full ecosystem of major large language models (LLMs) as a structured retrieval corpus and proposes a compositional Automated Reasoning (AR) and Machine Learning (ML) architecture for cross-model retrieval-augmented generation. The central contribution is the formal characterization of the Benefit Yield Function (BYF), the marginal performance gain per additional model added to an ensemble, and the identification of the implosion threshold θ*: the ensemble size at which BYF crosses zero and aggregate performance begins to degrade. Existing LLM ensemble and mixture-of-agents systems treat models as responders and aggregate outputs, but do not study performance as a function of ensemble size N across the full model universe. Benchmark research confirms performance plateaus at the individual model level; model collapse literature establishes that iterative training on AI-generated outputs degrades individual model distributions. Neither body of work formalizes the ensemble-level implosion threshold, models Epistemic Hereditary Drift (EHD) at the ecosystem level, or treats AI manufacturing velocity as a co-variable of θ*. The framework has direct implications for DoD multi-model AI acquisition policy and the emerging science of testing AI-enabled systems.

</details>


### [162] [Robust and Efficient Communication for Multi-Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15361)
*Rafael Pina,Varuna De Silva,Corentin Artaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARC（多智能体正则化通信）框架，基于条件互信息的信息论原理，用注意力架构与消息正则化机制学习高效通信协议，在通信瓶颈和有损信道下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习中的有效通信是分布式智能的基石，但如何保证生成的消息既具有信息量、又能承受物理约束（如通信带宽受限、信道有损）仍是重大挑战。现实中的自主机器人网络与去中心化系统正面临这类约束。

Method: 提出MARC框架，受条件互信息这一信息论原理启发；采用基于注意力的架构，并搭配独特的消息正则化机制，该机制旨在最小化对未来系统状态的不确定性，从而诱导学习出高度具有代表性的通信协议。同时在严格的通信瓶颈与有损信道条件下对方法进行评估。

Result: 在复杂协作任务中，MARC显著优于当前最先进方法；对消息特征的深入分析表明，即使在显著数据压缩的情况下，MARC仍能保持高性能。

Conclusion: MARC为在资源受限环境中部署智能体提供了一条可扩展路径，在通信受限与有损条件下仍具鲁棒性与高效性。

Abstract: Effective communication is a cornerstone of distributed intelligence in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), yet ensuring that generated messages are both informative and robust to physical constraints remains a significant challenge. This paper introduces Multi-Agent Regularized Communication (MARC), a novel framework inspired by information-theoretic principles of conditional mutual information. MARC employs an attention-based architecture coupled with a unique message regularization mechanism designed to minimize uncertainty regarding future system states, thereby inducing the learning of highly representative communication protocols. Crucially, we evaluate MARC under stringent communication bottlenecks and lossy channels, simulating the real-world constraints of autonomous robotic networks and decentralized systems. Our results demonstrate that MARC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in complex cooperative domains. Furthermore, we provide a deep analysis of message characteristics, proving that MARC maintains high operational performance even under significant data compression, offering a scalable path for deploying intelligent agents in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [163] [Representing Clinical Conditions on Vital Signs from Healthy Individuals using Latent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15379)
*Rafael Pina,Varuna De Silva,Mindula Illeperuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于条件变分自编码器的深度生成模型，将健康个体的生命体征数据转化为模拟特定临床病症的模式，以缓解医疗场景中代表性数据不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在医疗等复杂信号处理应用中至关重要，但训练模型需要丰富数据集，而获取具有代表性的临床数据集往往困难。因此需要方法在缺少真实病症数据时扩充可用数据。

Method: 基于条件变分自编码器的深度生成模型。使用公开的ICU数据集训练模型，再用从健康个体采集的生命体征数据进行评估，并提出一种距离度量来衡量生成样本与目标临床标签的对齐程度。

Result: 模型不仅能学习ICU数据的内在动态规律，还能将健康个体的数据重塑为与特定临床病症生命体征相符的形式；相比测试的基线方法，所提距离度量显示模型生成的样本更符合预期临床标签。

Conclusion: 该生成模型可用于在缺乏代表性病症数据时扩充生命体征数据，为医疗机器学习应用提供可行的数据增强途径。

Abstract: Machine learning can be crucial to help scale complex signal processing applications in scenarios such as healthcare. However, these machine learning models need rich datasets to be trained and there are often cases where it is not possible to access representative datasets. In this paper, we propose a deep generative model based on conditional variational autoencoders with the objective of augmenting the vital signs of healthy individuals in a way that mimics the patterns of a certain clinical condition. More specifically, we use a publicly available ICU (Intensive Care Unit) dataset to train our model and then evaluate it using the vital data that we have collected from healthy individuals. Our results demonstrate that the proposed model can not only learn the underlying dynamics of the ICU data but, more importantly, can reshape our collected data from healthy individuals in a way that is aligned with the vital signs of a certain clinical condition. We propose a distance metric that shows how our model can generate samples that are more aligned with the intended clinical labels when compared to the tested baselines.

</details>


### [164] [CodeTS: Verifiable Text-to-Time Series Generation via Executable Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15393)
*Xudong Yuan,Shunyu Liu,Tongya Zheng,Huiping Zhuang,Mingli Song,Kaixuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CodeTS 提出以代码为中间接口的 Text-to-Code-to-TS 可验证框架，结合对齐三元组监督初始化与多阶段执行奖励的 RLVR 训练，实现强零样本时间序列生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-TS 方法缺乏从文本描述中显式推导生成逻辑以指导时间序列合成的机制，即文本需求如何塑造目标时序模式不明确。

Method: 将 Text-to-TS 重构为 Text-to-Code-to-TS：先把文本时序描述映射到显式代码空间，由可执行代码规定时序模式，再通过代码执行获得时间序列。为在无真实代码标注下可靠学习，从结构化时序属性构建对齐的 Text-Code-TS 三元组进行监督初始化；并设计多阶段基于执行的奖励，验证格式有效性、代码可执行性与时间序列质量，使真实 Text-TS 对为 RLVR 提供训练信号。

Result: 在短、中、长三种生成长度的八个基准上，CodeTS 提供了强零样本方案，优于基于 LLM 的基线，并在平均结果上超过在目标数据集上训练的监督式生成基线。

Conclusion: 以代码作为可验证的中间生成接口并结合执行级奖励的 RLVR，可有效提升 Text-to-TS 生成，且无需目标数据集训练即具竞争力。

Abstract: Text-to-Time Series Generation (Text-to-TS) provides a promising paradigm for synthesizing time series from natural language, enabling scenario-specific generation when real observations are scarce or costly to acquire. However, existing methods typically lack an explicit mechanism for deriving generation logic from textual descriptions to guide time series synthesis. In this paper, we propose CodeTS, a verifiable framework that uses code as an intermediate generation interface, reformulating Text-to-TS generation as a Text-to-Code-to-TS process. CodeTS first maps textual temporal descriptions into an explicit code space, where executable code specifies how textual requirements shape target temporal patterns, and then obtains the time series through code execution. To learn this code generation process reliably without real code annotations, CodeTS constructs aligned Text-Code-TS triplets from structured temporal attributes for supervised initialization. More importantly, we further design multi-stage execution-based rewards that verify format validity, code executability, and time series quality, enabling real Text-TS pairs to provide training signals for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks across short, medium, and long generation lengths demonstrate that CodeTS provides a strong zero-shot solution for Text-to-TS generation, outperforming LLM-based baselines and achieving better averaged results than supervised generative baselines trained on the target datasets.

</details>


### [165] [Single-condition neural solvers encode transferable response spaces for parametric differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15432)
*Wenbo Cao,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出线性化子空间迁移(LST)与主动迁移建模(ATM)，利用单条件神经解模型的输出雅可比构造可复用的响应空间，实现跨条件PDE求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数化PDE算子学习通常需构建覆盖预设域的全局模型，依赖跨条件数据或昂贵的物理约束训练。

Method: LST利用单条件训练的神经解模型输出雅可比定义可复用响应空间，通过在响应空间坐标上最小化目标PDE系统残差恢复目标解；ATM以迁移后残差作为覆盖指标，选择性从额外单条件模型获取响应空间。

Result: 在六个系统上，单条件响应空间支持跨条件迁移；增加空间扩大表示能力时精度提升。相比物理约束算子基线，ATM降低了误差和离线构建成本，代表性案例中精度提升数个量级，目标适配为毫秒至秒级。

Conclusion: 研究确立了神经求解器作为可复用局部参数模型的地位。

Abstract: Operator learning for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) typically builds global models over prescribed domains, requiring cross-condition data or costly physics-constrained training. Here we show that the output Jacobian of a neural solution model trained at one condition defines a reusable response space for cross-condition solution variations.
